A New Adventure · Chapter 1
1

Met in Mossholde

In-World18th of Talil, 1246 WhereMossholde PlayedJanuary 1st, 2025

Mossholde - Talil 18, 1246

The campaign begins in Mossholde - a hunting and trading outpost (one building - multi-story) south of Mount Everbloom in the Borealian Sway territory. The individuals there that night were:

  • Az’efal “Affy” Fairhand, a female half-orc fighter,
  • Cerul Slate, a male aasimar cleric,
  • Kaizo, an old male elf monk,
  • Fungus Roundbelly, a hill giant goliath warlock,
  • Hamalex “Ham” Milton of The Andërs, a male high elf bard, and
  • Aerowen “Ro” Umberwood, a female sorceress

Everyone met at the outpost and Introductions were made, each sharing a bit about themself. Ro’s dad was rendered ill from a powder that was thrown on him by a robed figure. She is not sure who is behind the attack.

Marlowe, man in leathers, with a dagger, ran the outpost. He had a half-orc female (messy, not attractive - does hard labor - ferrier) named Harva that assisted him. Affy talked to Harva about her origins - she doesn’t really know. Marlowe is her uncle and took her in. She tells Affy that she would be more welcomed to the east, south, maybe in Inverland.

Marlowe makes stew - we figure out where to sleep. Ro asks Affy to bunk together - and she agrees. Fungus sleeps by the fire; Kaizo meditates by the fire. Ham and Cerul take the single bed room (Ham said he would stand). Fungus keeps trying to talk to Kaizo as he meditates - Kaizo does not break. Fungus tries to go through K’s pack and he gets whacked out of nowhere by K. Fungus sings all night…just because he is jolly.

Interruption at Night

Early in the morning (middle of the night) - we are jolted awake/out of trance. We step into the courtyard and see Harva open the doors…figure stumbles in. A bloody elf with an arrow embedded who calls out for help. Ro goes to his aid (healing); Affy runs to the door to see if she sees anything. Fungus gives him a hand up. He says his group was ambushed by small blue skinned people in the valley. Marlo thinks he must be hallucinating. Cerul and Kaizo aren’t sure who they might have been. Questions the man. He says they killed a wolf…and they were looking at helping the cubs when they were ambushed. These beings disappeared and then showed up and shot them. He commented that they were careful not to go on the “old Citadel” lands…ruined during the Awakening…but maybe they people have no connection. His friends (2 others) were killed. Taberry Elich (elf) says he was ½ day away in the valley to the south.

Kaizo checks out the arrow. Seems more like a short bow arrow. Fits the story of being attacked by small beings. Group decides to leave now - hoping to arrive by dawn.

Fungus and Ro get a lantern. The group leaves. Kaizo takes off in the front. Affy has to correct his direction. As we go, we hear the forest/wildlife waking up. After 4 hours we find evidence of the fight, but do not see the bodies (just belongings). Fungus grabs the weapons (2 long bows and 2 short swords), Ham and Cerul can’t track the cubs. We are in a clearing and the ground is damp. We see an area off to the side that seems trampled…and see a pile of flesh sitting there (looks like it was cut off the bones and discarded, no bones). Affy notices small canine footprints and humanoid footprints…and well as blood drops and a clear trail. Fungus and Affy talk about the barbary of the actions (nothing like she has seen, even among orcs). Kaizo starts to lead on the new trail. Fungus sniffs his bottles and feels like we are close. We see a ruined building across the clearing and the mountains in the distance. The group decides to be sneaky…if they can.

The Broken Citadel

Fungus and Kaizo lead the group, sneaking. Kaizo is humming the song Fungus sang the night before. Cerul is stealthy behind them. Ro, Affy and Ham are 20-30’ back…also trying to be quiet, but doing a bad job.

Fungus and Kaizo start to approach on the right side, where the walls have crumbled and there is an opening in the corner. They slow up to wait for the party. We all catch up. No one is able to be quiet from this vantage point. Fungus catches a whiff of something cooking. Fungus & Kaizo start to hear some conversation that they can’t understand behind a wall. Affy understands what is being spoken…she knows it is goblin-ish, but not the dialect she knows. She picks up 3 different voices. Ham says he is going to go over alone to the point where he can see them to try to draw one out to the group. He sees these warty, blue creatures. He uses his whistle to try to lure one out (“approach”) with success

IT’S INITIATIVE: Combat vs. Gremlins. Ro decides to see what happens. As Fungus goes to hit him, he misses and the guy disappears and reappears next to where Kaizo was holding his action, waiting to see if his companions emerged from the ruins. He attacks Kaizo and Kaizo reacts and kills him. Affy runs to the entrance and yells “Surrender now and you’ll live. You are surrounded.” in goblin. Cerul runs up and casts firebolt - that misses (and the guy disappears). Ro runs up and has no line of sight so holds her action. Ham runs up - no line of sight. Kaizo runs into the ruins behind full cover…saw them as he entered. A figure emerges from the inner walls and speaks in an unrecognizable language. He is in fancy clothes and holds a skull and he ducks behind a wall. A blue guy shoots an arrow at Ham - misses. Affy is hit by an arrow. Runs in and sees a few and takes cover since they are out of range. Cerul runs in and firebolts one and kills it. Ro runs in with a firebolt and hits one under partial cover and damages it. Ham runs in and tries to hit one with a quill. Fight continues. One takes Ro down. Affy takes that one out. Cerul heals Ro. Ro takes one out. Kaizo, Ham, and Cerul go after the “fancy” guy. Fungus goes after the blue guy who ducks behind the wall. Kaizo misses - face to face with fancy man. Man misses K with skull. Affy gets the first crit of the campaign and kills the fancy man with the skull. Cerul gives chase to the remaining blue guy and misses. Ro tries to hit with a fire bolt and misses. Ham runs in with a quill and true strike and hits and takes him down.

Kaizo thanks Affy and she steps forward and looks like she might embrace her…but she nods her head and a little smile cracks. We notice they were dining on wolf. We see one wolf pup in a cage. We see cots, a bloated bladder with bumps on it. Ham picks up with bladder and it feels like it has coins. He pierces it and 12gp fall out. Kaizo loots the fancy man. He has a coin purse (24gp and 60sp) and has a magical ring. Fungus goes upstairs to check and finds a room with stacks of bones of various sizes that have been organized. Barrels and crates with supplies. Alchemy kit, Poisoner’s Kit, other supplies. Bones in the center of the room surround a trap door. Other items in the room look like they were stolen goods from travelers. Other room is a chamber with recently repaired ceiling. Canopy bed. Recently framed paintings and a wardrobe. Next to the bed is a pedestal with a small jar with an unknown substance in it (powdery substance that looks familiar to Ro). and a flask that has a liquid in it (not a healing potion - looks like poison). Clothes here are very nice, some costumes. Blue guys seem to be gremlins upon inspection - there is a fey connection here. Ro investigates the chambers thoroughly - trying to figure out who this guy was. She doesn’t find anything that reveals who he is.

Each get 6gp and 10sp. Kaizo has Ham inspect the magical ring - Fungus agrees it is magical (we don’t know what it does). Fungus says he will hold and he and Cerul will identify it later. Fungus puts the poison flask in his pouch. Ro takes the jar of powdery substance. We free the wolf pup and Ro plans to keep it, if possible.

The session ends with the party at the trap door.

– The End of the Session –
A New Adventure · Chapter 2
2

Beneath the Citadel

In-World19th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Broken Citadel PlayedJanuary 24th, 2025

The group takes a short rest.

Ro and Kaizo decide to take the wolf pup back to Mossholde before darkness comes. Ro takes the powder with her. Fungus takes the other items in his sack, but leaves the sack there.

Beneath the Broken Citadel

Fungus performs a ritual over the trap door…looks for traps. Affy opens the door. There is a spiral staircase. We descend the stairs - Affy, Cerul, Ham, then Fungus. Arrive at 20’ sq. chamber. Room lit by phosphorous moss. There is an archway to the south. Ham notices where dust has been moved around - not footprints - but where piles of dust have collected on the sides of the walls. Affy notices patterns on the walls of the moss similar to the patterns on the floor. Cerul pokes at the moss that Affy points out. He notices that the glow happens at the roots/core of the moss…once you flick it the glow fades as it falls to the ground. We go through the arch and the ceiling opens up.

Very well crafted area. Earthy smell. We notice symbols on the walls. Not sure what they mean. Seems to be more symbols vs language. The hall opens up into a 30’ square chamber. Looks like a lab of some type (alchemy). Initially looks long ago used. Ham thinks it has been recently disturbed, but that most of the use might have been older. Fungus looks beyond the doorway…doesn’t leave…but sees a hallway that extends as far as he can see. Ham notices a groove in the south wall near the hallway. He finds a secret door. Ham and Affy have a discussion about “not judging size,” while Fungus pulls the door open. It is a corridor that is a dead end. Affy steps in, but doesn’t find anything. Cerul finds a secret door at the end. Affy opens it. Piles of bones are stacked in the corner of the room. There are skeletons against the wall. Affy steps in - INITIATIVE.

Affy gets hit, Cerul is missed. Affy is missed, Cerul is hit. Fungus jumps in and casts a spell that hits. Ham throws a quill and misses. Cerul casts a spell on 2 of them; one takes damage. Affy hits one and kills it. Two attack Affy and one hits her. One attacks Cerul and misses. Fungus casts a spell at one. Ham sends another quill and hits. Cerul casts a spell and hits. Affy takes a swing and misses. The skeletons attack and miss Cerul and Affy. Then one attacks Cerul and (silvery barbs) misses and drops his weapon. Fungus misses. Ham hits. Cerul casts a spell, one is hit and is taken out. Affy finishes off one with her sword. One left and it misses Affy. Fungus is mad and attacks with hellfire and hits it. Ham throws a quill and hits. Cerul uses rapier and hits and takes it out. We look around and see more lab supplies, tools - similar to what we saw in the other room. There is another archway leading out. We search the room and skeletons and don’t find anything of value other than the hammer. Cerul takes it. It is a magical light hammer. We look through the arch…Affy sees a long hallway with a door several feet down to the left and another even further down to the left. At the first hallway, Affy looks north and sees it is a hall that leads to another hallway and a set of double doors in the hallway. Once in the hallway, we notice a familiar archway to the left. To the right, the hall ends in rubble, but something is on the ground about 80’ down. As Ham moves to investigate the item on the ground, Fungus tries to open the double doors (they are locked).

INITIATIVE.

Ham casts mage hand and pokes the “goo” when he’s 30’ out. Affy follows. Fungus is at the door and tries to pick the locks. Ham throws a dart at the goo and hits and damages it. Affy considers the situation. Cerul throws a firebolt and hits. Affy stows her sword and moves toward the goo 15’. The goo moves up, but is still out of Affy’s range. Fungus tries to pick the lock and succeeds: Cerul gives him an exasperated look for opening another door while they are fighting something. Ham throws and quill and misses and backs up. Cerul uses a firebolt and kills it…oozing into the cracks. Fungus opens the door and sees a dimly lit circular room. He sees a summoning circle in the middle of the room. Sees a number of symbols around the room. There is a cube in the center of the circle covered with runes. He hears something shuffle in the shadows…and sees two grotesque creatures emerge in the room, a smell of sulfur in the air and making terrible noises.

INITIATIVE.

One of the creatures moves toward Fungus. He closes and locks the door and runs down the hall like a huge baby. Ham looks at where the ooze was to see if there is anything there. Fungus watches the doors and hears something thumping them. He goes to the door and fumes come out through the cracks and burn his lungs with acid damage. Fungus unlocks the doors and opens as he runs away, using the door as a shield.

INITIATIVE.

Ham moves closer to the door and holds his action to throw a quill. Sees fumes coming out the door. Cerul takes half cover and readies an action to fire off a spell. One comes out the door and is hit by Cerul’s firebolt, but it doesn’t hurt him as much as he would have thought. It was absorbed by the fatty flesh. It is also hit by Ham’s quill…and radiant damage. It moves forward and tries to bite Ham and misses. Another comes out the door. Fungus moves back and then casts a spell at the one in the back, but fumbles. Affy shoots an arrow and kills it…its blubber splashing on the other one behind it as it turns into dust. Fumes waft toward Ham…con save and fails and coughs, taking acidic damage. He attacks and misses and moves back as far as he can. Cerul steps out to firebolt him and hits. It starts to head toward Fungus in a dash. Fumes head toward Fungus and he is not harmed. He then casts a spell and runs away. Affy shoots an arrow and misses. Ham moves next to Affy and attempts to message the creature in Common (“have you ever had anyone in your head before?”). There is no reaction or response from the creature. Cerul steps up to firebolt it and hits. It turns around and moves toward Affy and Ham. Affy chokes on the fumes, but takes no damage, thanks to Fungus bestowing luck on her. Moves back 30’ and shoots an arrow and kills it, turning it into dust. We close the door to the room, knowing that these creatures seem devilish and head back to the far hall and the alcove/room we didn’t explore (Affy, Cerul, Ham, Fungus). We see a large intricate circle in the center of this room (this is a teleportation circle - out of character). This was long ago used…ancient sconces. Cerul senses that this is probably a teleportation circle that has probably not been tracked by the Tegenwald Phet…he makes notes to pass to “his people” (the Phet). He copies down the symbol. Fungus sits and tries to cast a ritual using his various vials and pouches. Affy and Ham question who “these people” are that Cerul is talking about. He mentions this group - who espouse freedom and magic and the light - and he is a member of it. Ham ponders the history of this group…and knows they were almost banned a long time ago, but rose to power again 20 years ago. Ham vows to help Cerul get this information to his people if it will help him rise to prominence with them, something Cerul says he isn’t interested in, but does want to help them. Affy knows they are a magical group. After about 10 minutes, Fungus realizes this is a conjuration circle/symbol. He wants to see if there is any other magic in the area. He looks back at the ring (isn’t sure of the magical school - might be divination or enchantment) and hammer (it has a simple enchantment). Fungus leaves this area and starts to move south and east, looking to see if his spell identifies anything else. This tunnel ends in a heap of rubble as well. We see a large dark hole through the rubble.

INITIATIVE.

Fungus sees something undead heading our way. Ham throws a quill with radiant damage and it hits. Then he backs up. Fungus casts a spell on it and hits hard and then backs up, cursing it with disadvantage and it steps up to hit Affy. He misses. Affy hits it with her sword and kills it. She yells - “anyone else?”

We hear a drip, drip. We see a deep hole…very dark. We get the impression that we aren’t ready to proceed here.

We make our way back to the circular chamber where the 2 blubber men were. Pentagram etched into the floor - we think it could be associated with Asmodeus (prince of darkness). There is an arched gateway with vines in the far wall - there is no longer power here - but this looks to have been a long ago used portal. The cube on the floor in the middle of the circle glows with magic. The pentagram is a traditional summoning circle. Fungus moves to the golden, metallic cube - drawn by its magical glow. There are runes on each face of the cube - and there is a mechanism to move the faces about (out of character - Rubik’s cube meets hellraiser). Fungus starts to manipulate the cube…runes shift positions. Cerul and Ham are working on capturing the runes on parchment, as Affy looks at the portal. After nine clicks of the cube [Fungus rolls 9 - quickness], magic extends from the cube and engulfs Fungus (he feels quicker - gets 10’ more movement per round). He picks up magic from all schools as he holds the cube. Ham walks into the circle to take the cube from him, but it is frozen and won’t move again. Affy investigates the portal and realizes this was a gateway to/from the feywild (picks up hints from the vines). Affy notices the keystone at the top is missing. She notices the dent in the floor and figures that someone came through and took the keystone to effectively “close the gate.”

Ham tells Affy that he is impressed with her intelligence. He muses that he never would have thought that of her. Cerul continues to draw and note everything in the room…he studies the cube and realizes the cube has Sylvan properties and may have also come from the feywild. We think the gremlins and bone collector may be tied to this as well, as they had fey properties. Looks like they came from the feywild and were left here.

This Old Citadel clearly had a lot of uses - feywild portal, summoning circle from the hells, an old teleportation circle long forgotten, and a hole/tunnel down deep to who knows where.

Ham launches into a soliloquy to talk to Cerul about would the Phet use this information wisely…and would it help Cerul gain more favor with them.

We decide to go back upstairs and make our way back to Mossholde before we further explore this hole.

Cerul knows he needs to get information to the Tegenwald Phet - might be best to go to Qal’dynn to find a contact to send this info.

We know Ro needs to get the powder to a library or someone who can identify its uses and see if there is an antidote that can be applied on her father. Again - Qal’dynn might be the closest, biggest place to seek knowledge for this.

Fungus thinks the portal to the Feywild might help him figure out his power. He pockets the cube in his bag.

Mossholde

In Mossholde, the group gets a long rest. Fungus no longer feels energized by the power of the cube. He realizes that the cube is no longer frozen - maybe this is something that could be used every day.

Ham studies the ring - it is a Ring of Truth. Cerul finds that the hammer is a +1 light hammer. Fungus gives the cube to Ham to study (he will “turn” it next session).

– The End of the Session –
A New Adventure · Chapter 3
3

Road to O’naren

In-World20th of Talil, 1246 WhereMossholde PlayedFebruary 2nd, 2025

Kaizo and Ro made it back to Mossholde (while the rest of the group were in the Citadel). An animal merchant (Clarton Waitor) with an albino rongri pet and a wagon appeared in the town shortly after they arrived. Said he was heading east. Wasn’t really stopping there, but Ro decided to travel with him and the pup…said she would meet us eventually in Qal’dynn. Kaizo noticed that the merchant’s horses seemed oversized.

The group meets up with Kaizo the following morning after returning to Mossholde. Ham has been studying the cube to see what he can make of it. Cube has mysterious, puzzle-like power…not sure where/how it is drawing power. Runes have ancient Sylvan linkage, but nothing he’d seen before. Fungus needs a pearl to help. Ham decides to try to cube - Cerul stands far back, but Kaizo, Affy, and Fungus stand close to Ham. [Ham rolls a 32-riches]. On the third turn, we hear a crack. Cerul smells sulfur in the air. A puff of smoke flashes near Cerul and something stands next to him with a pointy hat, purple robe. After 6 turns, the cube stops. We all feel our coin pouches get heavy from the cube. This red-skinned guy shows up…we all think he is associated with the cube and question him. His says this seems like magic that his “parents would be interested in.” He says he wants to be “the great.” He and Ham have that in common. His name is Ireron. Kaizo takes a long drag of his pipe and blows out smoke that resembles a mimic. He questions Ireron about where he “poofed” from/to last time. He went to a sheep farm. This “poofing” happens quite a bit. Fungus asks if he “poofs with a purpose.” He says he doesn’t really know. Since maturity, he’s been poofing farther from home. Ireron asks what Kaizo is a master of.

Nylean’s Camp

We decide to head out. Nice fall day. About halfway through the day we find a used campsite - no one there. At the end of the day we find a shelter/building that has been used by travelers. Board set up to dress animals. Not big enough to house folks. A dark-haired elf comes out and introduces himself. He is uncomfortable with the diversity of our group - Kaizo and Ham talk to him in elvish. Affy catches on and steps forward and extends her hand to introduce herself. Nillian shakes it (and wipes it off). Kaizo and Ham convince/persuade him to be kind. He offers to sell us rations at half price. He is willing to buy/sell other stuff. We ask him about other travelers - he confirms Ro/Clarton passed through. No other travelers in past couple of days. Says travel slows down as it gets colder. Right now most are hunting and gathering to stock up for colder weather. Nillian offers us a couple of tents to use for the night. Ireron tells us that his mom spoke of “camp rules”. We ask him who his mom is - it is Mage Kasminos (Kaizo has heard of her). Kaizo meditates outside a bit; Ireron casts an alarm around the tents. In the morning, Nillian brings us hot beverages and we pack up the tent.

Affy decides to give the cube a try. Ireron stops her and asks if she is invested in it. The group discusses how to turn. Ham says he could recreate what he did yesterday (more coin). Affy prays to her goddess and asks for a positive outcome. Kaizo and Ham step back 30’ - everyone else close up. [Affy rolls a 71-tired]. It moves fast and quickly…and then she manipulates it herself - more than a dozen times - then there is a flash. Those of us close by have levels of exhaustion (1-3 depending on our rolls). Cerul, Ireron, and Affy tried to pay attention (Affy, Cerul, Ireron, and Fungus think they could avoid doing this again - but will have to roll to try to avoid).

We decided to travel despite being tired…moving at half speed. We made it to the next campsite about 10 miles away. Find another clearing off the path and set up camp with bedrolls. Kaizo builds a fire and Ireron casts an alarm around the area, several 20” cubes. We ask him how effective the spell is (tells us a story, “one time there was a squirrel”). Kaizo meditates. Winds pick up during the night. Night is uneventful. We get a long rest.

22nd of Talil

Next morning, Affy gives Ireron the cube. Cerul gets 60’ out; Kaizo gets 30’ out. Fungus, Affy, and Ham get close. [Ireron rolls a 79-relax]. Flash of light for 20’...those inside get benefits of a long rest. Fungus takes the cube from Ireron to hold. We decide to head out. Still not at full speed (Cerul still has a level of exhaustion). Fungus sings; Ham jumps in. Cerul, Fungus, and Ham speak “giant” and sing in that language. We only travel 10 miles and stop again. Ireron sets up the alarm again. We all decide to rest. Kaizo meditates. Ireron’s alarm goes off and Kaizo sees Ireron pop up to look around. Kaizo relights the fire. They both see a wild dog sniffing around who then scampers off. The two have a conversation about his perimeter. The rest of the night passes uneventfully.

23rd of Talil

We wake up and discuss the alarm and the dog. Ireron mentions that Kaizo said to turn alarm off for wild animals and Affy disagrees. Group decides that Ireron can try the cube again - trying to recreate the relax condition to help Cerul. [Ireron rolls to get relax and avoid tired - he rolls a 12 - age]. Cerul, Affy, and Fungus observe. Kaizo stays back with Ham. Ireron learns that you cannot replicate a pattern in back to back days. Ireron, alone, is afflicted with aging - he is now in his 50s (permanent). He gives it to Fungus. Ham breaks out in song. We decide to travel on. We pass strangers, make small talk. We travel a little faster, but still a little slowed. We travel 20 miles. Find a clearing with lean-tos. Ireron sets up the alarm. Uneventful.

24th of Talil

Cerul feels great today. Fungus wants to try the cube. Cerul, Affy, Ireron get close, while Fungus rolls. [Fungus tries to replicate riches…he rolls Int of 15, rolls 90 - comprehend]. A flash of light envelops Fungus and his head glows. He feels more knowledgeable in the way of linguistics. He knows the Sylvan language (but doesn’t know it yet). As he looks at the cube, he realizes that he now understands it better (it has Sylvan runes). He feels like the name of the cube is “Feywoven Glyphic.” He says it in Sylvan (Lelphfelthalis - group laughs). We head out on a day of travel. Travel 20 miles and get to another campsite (probably a day outside of O'naren). There are a couple of small hunting parties camping here as well. We notice that these people seem to do night hunting - they don’t camp at night. Fungus trades short swords for rations. Affy asks if the hunters looked at him weird and he says no…and tells her that one was looking at her like they “like” her. You never know. Usual provisions at night.

25th of Talil

Early in the morning, Kaizo catches movement near us as the day is breaking (he was meditating). He feels it is dangerous. They are like large moving twigs. He wakes the group. Affy shoots an arrow at one and misses. Kaizo stands up and moves behind a bush. Cerul stands up and dons his shield and stands still. Ireron moves and casts a firebolt and misses. Fungus stands and moves and makes big noises and casts hex and hellfire and kills him. One attacks Cerul and misses. Ham inspires Affy. And then attacks one with a quill and hits. Then the rest attack: Ham and hits, Fungus and misses, and runs up. Affy shoots with a bow and hits; Kaizo hits one with his staff and shatters it; Ireron casts magic missile and hits; Cerul hits one with firebolt; Fungus kills one with hellfire; one tries to hit Affy and misses; Ham tries to hit with a quill and does so, then gives Affy inspiration again. One attacks Ireron and crits. Affy hits one with an arrow. Kaizo takes another out. Ireron casts magic missiles on one and kills it; and on another and injures it. Cerul attacks one but it saves. He heals Ireron. Fungus hexes and hellfires the remaining one and kills it. We check over the dead bodies for loot - just see a bunch of sticks. Ham decides to try the cube. Kaizo doesn’t like it. Ham tries to NOT age, then not tired. [Int roll - 16; He rolls a 76-unknown figurine]. Cerul and Kaizo step 60’ out; Affy and Ireron study, but Fungus is close. Cube glows and rattles after 7 turns in a new pattern. A shiny stone forms under the cube and drops down (it pooped a stone). Ham picks up the stone and thinks it is magical. Ireron can identify the stone (got pearl from his mom). After 10 minutes, he thinks it is a command word associated with it (once one attunes). It will suck in the soul of a beast nearby into the ethereal plane and then they can call upon that beast to help. (stone becomes a figurine once you pick the beast). Ham keeps the stone for now (Fungus tells him it was his will that drew forward the stone). Then we continue forward, travel for just under an hour. A commotion breaks out in front of us - woman and child screaming. Initiative.

Hired Killers

We hear horse hooves in the distance; the woman and child are scared. Fungus welcomes them and casts a spell on himself and his club. Kaizo runs up and asks them to come up with him - trying to take them into a bush to shelter. Ireron steps forward and readies a spell (firebolt). Horses round the corner and one raises a crossbow to fire; Ireron’s spell goes off and misses. Guy jumps off the horse and shoots an arrow at the woman and misses. Another hits the child with an arrow and the kid falls prone and crawls. Another moves up and sends firebolt at Fungus and stays on a horse. Another shoots a crossbow at Fungus. Cerul attacks and hits, heals child. Ham runs up and casts spell on one of the horses to make it prone. Affy dashes forward, weapon drawn…ready to hit if attacked. Woman is confused; she and her son hide behind a tree. Fungus pulls up next to Affy and casts bane on 3 of them (2 save and 1 fails). Kaizo tries to convince them to move, he hides; Ireron observes them (2 elves and 2 humans) and asks what is going on; bad guy answers “you know not what you deal with, leave the woman and child and go”; Ireron fires a bolt into the air and asks everyone to stop; one attacks Fungus, one is crushed by his horse and it falls prone, but he is able to wedge free and hide behind his horse; another hides behind a tree with his horse; another hides behind a tree and uses crossbow to hit Fungus and then hides. Ham moves forward, sings, and casts dissonant whispers (guy fails). Affy steps forward to hit one, and misses (but still damages - yells at him). Mom and kid hide in bushes. Fungus attacks and hits. Kaizo flips out of the bushes and lands, pauses, and then hits the prone guy. Ireron tries to interact again, is brushed off, he looks at the woman on the horse and holds his reaction of his firebolt to release if she does not validate her goodness; prone guy stands and attacks Fungus and misses; then attacks him and hits; another shoots at Cerul and hits; lady runs away and Ireron’s firebolt hits; Cerul hits one with firebolt; Affy takes the head off of one and runs after another; Mom and Child are hiding; Fungus casts hellfire on the horse running away and it saves; Kaizo dashes and catches up with the woman fleeing on horseback and swings his staff to knock her off and hits and kills her, knocking her off and falling and hitting her head on a rock. Ireron tells last guy to surrender or die; he runs away and tries to hit Fungus and misses. He tries to hide and does. Cerul readies his toll the dead spell. Ham talks to a horse - I’m going to call you “Washington,” and handles the horse successfully. Affy yells “come out, f…er…I have your horse,” horse steps away; Affy moves up and sees the guy hiding and points him out to Cerul. Mom and kid come out of hiding and say, “are they gone?” Fungus runs over to try to see the guy who is hiding and beckons the guys to “come out,” and casts a spell on himself. Kaizo handles the horse successfully. Ireron had walked back to the woman and child and converses with her. She says “I have to get out of O'naren.” Remaining guy runs out into a clearing and tries to go after the woman and child, shooting an arrow at Ireron…it misses him and it kills the woman with his poison bolt. Cerul tolls the dead and the guy fails and takes damage. Ham mounts the horse and runs over and hoofs the man, but misses. Affy tries to handle the horse and fails, asks the guy what his horse is named. Fungus tries to hellfire the guy and misses. Kaizo loots the dead woman (holy symbol dedicated to Negrum, has mace, robe, chainmail shirt). Ireron magic missiles the remaining bad guy and steps forward to block the guy. Guy runs over to the kid and kills him. Cerul firebolts him and he is injured. Ham rides the horse to the guy and whispers to him and the guy saves and takes half-damage. Affy tries to handle the horse and fails and walks toward the crowd. Fungus casts a spell on the guy and hits and kills him (his head explodes).

We collect all four horses. We loot all the bodies. Other items we find: rusty scimitar, light crossbow, 6 daggers, leather armor, hand crossbow, very nice short sword, thieves tools. We know that Negrum is the god of the undead. 4 gp, 32 sp, 38 copper.

We then search the Mom and Child: tucked into the mom’s pouch is a scrap piece of parchment; sack of basic supplies (they are on the run). Couple of bedrolls, a few days rations. The parchment is a burned…appears to be part of a larger map. Looks to be a town. Along the edge of the parchment we see “routes compromised (and maybe a dash) Vrathin is….” We don’t know of a Vrathin. Ireron casts detect magic….the cube is very powerful, but also sees magic on the sword. He identifies it…it is a +1 short sword. Kaizo takes the sword. We see no marks of any kind to distinguish any of the six. Affy makes sure to give the mom and child a burial.

O’naren

We take/ride the four horses with us. We continue to head out. We reach O’naren. We approach the stables on our way in. There is a barn and home; large, fenced in area with a mix of horses. There are two people at the stables. We see a 2-story building with guards. Affy goes up the stairs to tell the guards what happened in the woods. The guards accuse Affy of killing the two and Affy gets upset. She turns around and calls Kaizo up to help explain. He goes up and they talk in elvish…he explains what happened. He then asks who is in charge of the town and they tell him it is Lord Etham. Kaizo says we seek his audience. They suggest the Nestling Sword is the inn to stay in overnight and say they will tell the Lord where we are. Meanwhile, Fungus tries to get in the water and the guards tell him to stop - this is the Driftglow and you cannot bathe in the waters (can’t even put your feet in). Kaizo tells them we need supplies, food, a place to stay…and the guards start pointing everything out.

– The End of the Session –

Will look at selling horses, eating, finding place to stay, and talking to the town leader next time.

In O’naren

In-World25th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Pale Lantern, Qal’dynn PlayedMarch 3rd, 2025

In Qal’dynn

Continue at guard tower. Guards are called Realmwardens. We ask them about the Inn…he points to a terrace. Ham goes to the Stables to board the horses and see if they also have riding horses for sale, too. Affy, Fungus, and Kaizo walk toward the terrace where the Inn is…in search of Ro and drinks.

Ham speaks to staff at the stable (female (Maynari) and male (Adris - he’s the ferrier)): talks about origins of horses and needs (new shoes). He pays 1gp per horse per night for stabling; 2sp for new shoes. Talks about a trade of 2 draft horses for 1 riding horse, maybe purchasing another. After talking, Ham talks to a new riding horse (“Jefferson”) and pulls out his magical stone and the essence of the horse is into the stone (figurine of wondrous power - treat like a Nightmare). He tests the figurine and dismisses the horse and it transforms back into the stone, which now looks like a horse. Stable lady says, “You got that from the Oracle” and starts to walk away.

As we walk around we see that the buildings are community housing with shops on the lower level. Kaizo notices a butcher nearby (Fungus goes to buy sausages-a gp worth - inquires about an alchemy shop and was told to go to Qual’dynn), a weaver (baskets), book shop, bath house...we head up to the Temple. Kaizo looks around the temple, sees floral carvings (Fralee is primary one worshipped here). Two elves in this area look eerily similar to the elves at the Stables. We pass through and go out to the terrace, walk over to the tavern and walk in.

Truffle Trap Tavern stone/wood building, has a 4-story tower with great views. Ro is there and spots a half-orc on the tower’s second level…notices he’s been there a while and is loose-lipped (“made some good coin, no loose ends, brat wasn’t going to make it”). Ro spots us coming and meets us on the lower level with Jasper, the wolf pup. The group clues her in on what has happened to us - finding the cube on the lower level of the Broken Citadel, trying to save the mother and son and killing their pursuers.

Kool’ok is a half-orc that Ro overheard…Affy approaches to ask about what he said. He denies knowing anything about a child being killed, etc. Ham approaches. Mentions Vrathin (and we know he is scared)...he gets up to leave. Kaizo cuts him off…asks about herbs (is told to find the Nightbud). Ham gets up to follow. Kaizo and Ham are both trying to be stealthy. Kool’ok notices them both and heads for the woods. Ham continues to follow. Kool’ok is disturbed and asks Ham to leave him alone. He walks to a realmwarden, but the guy shrugs him off. Kool’ok heads to the stables and Ham follows (he messages Kaizo to gather all of us). Ham messages Maynari to please delay that guy and she does (he messages again that it isn’t because he is a half orc, but because he needs his friends to come). He says Vrathin is the “mapmaker” in town. Talk commences. He says he is a middleman. Vrathin paid him to do a job. Ham messages a realmwarden. One comes over and Ham fills him in. Kool’ok tells the guard to take him in. They go and head into town. Ham asks Maynari about Vrathin - she confirms he is a mapmaker. She says she hasn’t seen his wife and son. Maynari says Vrathin has been in town a long time. That he adopted the child from the Abbey (south of here). Affy knows the Abbey of Montrest is home to one of the largest orphanages in the area. Rumor is the Abbey is protected by stone giants. It is now the Abbey of Light.

We talk among each other as we walk to the mapmaker. We see the guards take Kool’ok to the Town Hall. We discuss that the child might have known something and that his dad (or parents) wanted him killed. Kaizo says we need to go to the Abbey. We also want to talk to Vrathin.

As we walk around we see shops of all types. Fungus goes shopping for food. Kaizo goes to herbalist. Affy wants to go to the mapmaker. Ro hangs with Fungus. Ham hangs with Affy.

Kaizo shops for herbs, Ro and Fungus there also. Meledra introduces herself. They talk. Fungus and Kaizo observe jars of powder similar to what Ro found. Ro shows her the jar she has and asks if she can identify it. Meledra holds it up and shakes it (it lights up a little, seems to react to movement); she is uneasy. She tells Ro it is alchemical, not an herb. It reminds me of “an essence powder,” that is believed to anchor souls (suspending people between worlds). Ro asks if she knows of anyone who might know more. She says her cousin Iofyr (her male cousin who is “different” and has been into alchemy for a long time)...he is in Qual’dyn. There is usually an ask if you want knowledge from him (quid pro quo). She tells them about how he asked his mother to visit the silent circle for “nightbud.” Kaizo asks her about nightbud and she tells him that the gnolls have made gathering that problematic. She tells them that there is a path behind the tavern and inn that leads to the silent circle that is overseen by druids…but that gnolls have overtaken it. Kaizo offers a trade of nightbud for an herb from Ferndale. She says to talk to the satyr (who must be the druid)...she has no nightbud.

Meanwhile…Ham and Affy head to the mapmaker. Ham pulls out the parchment. Tells him that Kool’ok approached us about taking care of the job. He tells us that he hired people to “bring his wife and kid in,” that she is “part of the Cloak” and is doing bad things. He said that the Lord (Lord Etham) would like to talk to them. Ham asks for payment and he gives us a bag of coins (10gp). He wants them brought back alive. We think from what he says that the leader probably wants him dispatched. Kaizo continues to watch Vrathin close up and leave his shop - looks like he headed to his apartment above the shop. We think there must be another ally in town who would have warned them about the route being compromised.

Affy and Ro know the Cloak of Defiance is a thieves guild order that was sitting in pockets before the Awakening - in conflict with the Malfease. It eventually overthrew the Malfease and is now the primary black market/underground group in Myrdae.

We talk about going to the Inn to get rooms. Plan to get up early and head to the silent circle to help the town with the gnoll problem. We go to the Nestling Sword Inn. Main house is nice; building to the left is better. Proprietor comes up to help. She is a little “racist” and Affy plays like she and Ham are a couple. We all get rooms in the modest section (Affy and Ro; Kaizo and Ham; Fungus on his own; Ireron and Cerul). But Fungus gets a nicer room in the modest section. Ro smuggles Jasper into the room.

O’naren - 26th of Talil

After a long rest and good night’s sleep, the group heads up to the silent circle before sunrise. We see a satyr walking around “cleaning up” the area, tending to soil near various stones. We see paw prints in the soil. She asks us to help with the cleanup and tells us the gnolls took the nightbud last night. We offer to help take out the gnolls…she tells us that 5-6 come each night. She knows there are at least 7. The leader has a bone whip. She says this is a circle to worship Fralee. This druid is named Kasani. We ask her about the nighbud growing patterns and its use (to relax and meditate). We decide to go back to town and then come back later that night to arrive around 10PM - stake out the place and prepare for the gnolls to come back. Before we go, Ham asks Kasani about the Cloak of Defiance…she doesn’t know much about it. Kaizo talks to her about “spreading the influence of the nightbud” around the world. She says she will consider it, but let’s solve one problem at a time.

Group goes back to town, arrives at lunch. Fungus and Ro go to jeweler. They hear that the Director runs the mines. That the workers here are oremasters. Everything appears normal. Ro investigates a little - looking to see what is being mined. They see a triad of buildings, connected to each other, evidence of a forge. See an armorsmith, weaponsmith, and jeweler in these buildings. All are elves and appear to be kin - have a unique look, not seen on others in town (very striking, jet black hair with silver eyes). Fungus goes to the jeweler…he is trying to avoid making eye contact. They notice a source of mithral might be there. Ro asks if that is mined here and told yes. The chain vest they see if worth 500gp. They find out that this is the “Tooasaurs” family (very notable family in smithing and jewelcraft). Fungus brings out the cube and shows it to them. They don’t have any idea about it. He wants to use it and tries to get the riches. Ro stands next to him. He is not trying to avoid anything. The armorsmith is next to him. Fungus warns him that not everything is good…and the armorsmith decides to stand away from him. They take the cube outside. The weaponsmith decides to stand 15’ away with one of her shields. The jeweler hands Fungus 3 stones to hold and stands 20’ back. The armorer stands 15’ away next to his sister. Fungus manipulates the cube [rolls an 80-enhanced; his two lowest stats (which were both an 8) increased by 1 point each] Fungus glows and starts to twitch…he is enhanced. We don’t observe anything other than twitching. Ro watched the manipulation of the cube (might have a chance to repeat it since she was up close). The group around doesn’t observe anything. He then asks the jeweler if he can buy a pearl and is looking for a dog collar (advised to go to the leather worker). He buys a 100gp pearl.

Affy, Kaizo, and Ham (meanwhile) go to the Town Hall. We ask about the Lord and he is “away on business.” We then ask if they know about the gnoll problem and they are aware. We offer to help with it and they offer us 50gp to rid the town of the problem. We ask who is the leader in the office right now…and are told Captain Salal is here, but currently entertaining a “diplomat” right now. We see two elves down the hall having a discussion. Ham messages him…and is told to make an appointment with Caddius. He continues and presses him. The Captain invites us to join he and his companion for lunch (this is someone from Caelora…they are talking about problems in the Borelian Sway). We start to tell him everything. When Ham reveals he is related to the Anders, he stops us and says “This isn’t a Cloak problem, this is Rel’Udomina.”

– The End of the Session –

Silent Circle Gnolls

In-World26th of Talil, 1246 WhereO’naren PlayedMarch 20th, 2025

O’naren - 26th of Talil

Cerul meets up with Ro and Fungus as they are leaving the jewelers. They went to head to one of the higher terraces to find the Oracle - Cerul is hoping to find a Phet connection and share information with them. As they head in that direction, they pass the Gallery, but don’t stop. They then walk by the Tannery & Fletcher - Fungus wants to find a collar for the wolf pup. At the tannery, a female high elf greets them (Vyen Rainsong). Ro and Fungus describe what they want for Jasper - leather with many holes; maybe something with green; tooling with his name. It will be 5sp and Fungus pays.

Meanwhile…Affy asks who are the Rel’Uldomina. The captain says he doesn’t know, but is trying to find out. He says this group is spreading around - show up as standard business men, but try to get information about towns. We tell him about the mapmaker and his wife and child. We show him the map we got from the wife’s things. We tell him that she and the boy are dead - killed by sellswords. We tell him the mapmaker hired Kool’ok. We tell him it all seems suspect. We tell him we are going to help with the gnoll problem. Kaizo’s insight tells him that we are all on the same page; that the captain opened up when Ham revealed his origins. He offers help with arms, blades, armor….tells us to come back and talk to Caddius should we need anything.

Affy, Ham, and Kaizo leave and head toward the Jeweler - looking for the rest of the group.

The Oracle

Cerul, Ro, and Fungus head to the Oracle - a two-story round building, walls lined with scrolls and books, circular staircase. They see a female (brown skin, silver hair, violet eyes, tattoos) - violet/crimson dress. Cerul reveals that he is looking for a Phet member; Ro asks if their coming was foretold. They get wine and tea. The Oracle’s name is Zanasee Tsal’daree. Ro recognizes the surname (famous/strong in the arcane arts). Cerul starts to talk about the information that he has - important for those who defend justice and goodness. He pulls out the papers and info about the Broken Citadel, describing how the teleportation circles and gates have been used. She says she will need to do some research and get back to them. She looks at Fungus and tells him to seek Lofyr. She gives Fungus the Charm of the Slumbering Grove (an amber gemstone). Tells him to talk to Lofyr about it. It is encased in the petals of the Nightbud. Ro then asks her about her father’s plight and the jar of powder. She exclaims that Iofyr would also be a source of information - that they are on the same path. Ro tells her that she and Fungus are also “co-parenting” a dog, too. Some discussion about the dog’s name. Ro then shows her the amulet around her neck. She says that she will need to study to see if she can come up with information about it, and if she does, will contact Ro. Cerul then tells her about his past - coming up in the Abbey, but also a past with giants - and shows her trinkets that were supposedly gifts from his parents. She doesn’t know anything about them and suggests that answers might be at the Abbey (Cerul says that he asked questions then). Ro asks how she is able to see visions (and shares how it happens in her village with pig entrails). She mentions that she uses the “Weave” to see things. She expresses interest in meeting her village Wisdom to learn more of their ways. Cerul puts away his trinkets and asks everyone about the large white feather and what bird it may be from. They head out.

The group eventually meets up with each other - Fungus isn’t difficult to spot. As they walk by the Fletcher, Cerul asks about the feather and they say it doesn’t look like any bird/animal around this town. Looks like it is from a big white, flying bird. Groups brief each other about what they did. Fungus tries to figure out what his stone is…can’t figure it out. Thinks it is some type of receptor. Kaizo thinks it isn’t from this world.

The Silent Circle

Group heads back to the silent circle area. Fungus sings us a song as we travel; Ham playing the Lute. Twilight turns into night as we arrive there. It feels different by night - blackened veins, stench of decay. Kasani sits unnaturally still…eyes glowing with green light. Three gnolls sit there and shake, covered in spores. We see a wailing mushroom next to everything. It is initiative.

Kaizo and Cerul run up. Cerul shoots a firebolt at a gnoll and misses. Affy runs around the side, weapon drawn. Ham runs up and messages the mushroom. Ro tries to determine if Kasani is alive…looks like she has been invaded, thinks she is beyond help (influence of demonic gnolls, likely). Kasani stands up and moves. Fungus dashes around the other side. Gnolls stand up and move. Kaizo attacks and misses. Cerul moves in and casts a spell. Ham casts a spell, blinds the druid. Ro casts spell on the druid. Affy swings her sword and misses, but grazes the gnoll. Kasani tries to attack Cerul and hits, but he reacts with a shield and it misses. Fungus tries to attack and misses. He, Affy, and Kaizo are all hit, Kaizo taking damage to his foot. Kaizo attacks and hits. Cerul hits. Ro hits and kills the possessed druid. Affy hits. Ham whispers to the gnoll. Fungus attacks a gnoll, misty steps on a rock above the mushroom and heals Affy. Gnolls miss Affy. Mushroom wails. Kaizo attacks a gnoll and misses. Cerul casts spell on gnoll and it saves. Affy kills a gnoll and hits another. Ham whispers to one and it saves. Ro moves and casts spell on a gnoll and hits and kills it with a firebolt. Fungus tries to chill the incoming new gnolls - one is indifferent. Remaining original gnoll swings at Ham and stumbles. Kaizo runs up to hit and misses, but does an unarmed strike and hits. One new gnoll walks off. The other attacks Fungus with disadvantage and misses. Cerul sends a firebolt to the mushroom and hits it and kills it. Ham attacks an infected gnoll and kicks it. Ro attacks the last original gnoll (nat 20) and almost kills it. Affy then runs up and kills it. Fungus swings at it and misses. Kaizo limps in the direction of the new gnoll. Gnoll turns to leave and Fungus tries to hit it and misses. Cerul tries to firebolt the fleeing gnoll…and misses. Ro heals Kaizo. Affy and Ham dash after the gnoll. Fungus and Kaizo take a moment to chill. Cerul hits him with a firebolt. Affy moves Ham forward 30’. Ham commands the gnolls to come back, they turn around. Ro tries to hit one with a firebolt and misses. Fungus and Kaizo are sitting and meditating. One gnoll attacks Ham and crits. The other runs up, but can’t attack. Cerul attacks the familiar gnoll and hits. Ham attacks and gives inspiration to Cerul and kicks one. Ro tries to firebolt and misses. Affy runs up and misses, but it takes grazing damage. Fungus casts a spell and hits. Kaizo stands and moves forward and bonus dashes and hits. The one left hits Affy. Cerul misses. Ro hits. Affy hits. Ham kills it. Fight is over.

Affy sees a distinct path from here leading to a potential gnoll camp. We loot the bodies. The druid has nice boots. We look around the circle. What was a nice, natural area is corrupted. We find 7 Nightbud seeds - all that remain. Kaizo protects the seeds and takes them in his pouch. We find a potion in the druid pouch…it is yellow liquid with a bobbing eyeball. We discuss the wailing mushroom - was it part of the chaos? Affy tries to determine if the circle was an origination point for the blackening. We determine that the druid was defending the circle and something else interfered and took over. Ro and Affy and everyone try to burn the dead bodies and corruption in the circle (all except Fungus who is trying to identify items we found). We start a fire and the fumes are unfamiliar - not something we have smelled before, likely a smell not of this world. Fungus reveals that the boots are Boots of Elvenkind. And the potion is Clairvoyance. Kaizo gets the boots; Fungus keeps the potion and grabs ears before we burn them.

We take a long rest here…there are no issues. Kaizo tracks the group down the trail. We see a cave up ahead with dark fungus vines. We see blue smoke emerging from the cave mouth. We hear “nasty angel blood” come from the cave. This is where we stop for the night.

– The End of the Session –

Cackletongue Lair

In-World27th of Talil, 1246 WhereCrackletongue Lair PlayedApril 17th, 2025

Cackletongue Lair - 27th of Talil

We had a long rest in the purified Silent Circle. We go to the gnoll cave - jagged maw is partially concealed behind tangled moss and twisted limbs of gnarled trees. The earth has a damp glow. When we arrived we heard a high pitched voice inside the cave saying “What’s that stench? Nasty angel blood!” We know that gnolls have some fiendish properties, possibly put on the material plane by a Demon Lord. We are unaware as a party that anyone has celestial blood.

The opening is 15-20 ft wide. At the top it’s about 10 ft wide. Kaizo goes into scout and sees the corridor turns 90 degrees to the right. It’s damp and there is a stench of decay. The walls are rough stone slick with moisture. There are fragments of bones around. As he steps in, Kaizo notices light hues of violet and yellow bioluminescence on the walls. Kaizo comes out and explains what he saw.

We go back in as a group, with Kaizo scouting 30 ft ahead of the party. Kaizo sees the tunnel opens up into a foul-smelling chamber with a low ceiling. The room is wide, about 40 ft across and oblong shaped. The room is cluttered with bones, cracked bowls, gnawed wooden plans, and makeshift bedding. There is the earthy smell of clusters of mushrooms mixed with the stench of old blood and rot. Kaizo hears a high-pitched snicker from the shadows. He thinks he sees a sparkle in the debris.

Kaiso casts a spectral dancing fairy (Druidcraft) 30 ft away. He notices that where he saw the flicker, some of the larger of the mushrooms start to pulse and spores come up. He comes back to the party. Ham feels like the fungus is not natural - the fungus brought the gnolls back to life before, and these are bigger. Ro and Cerul walk back with Kaizo to try to burn the mushrooms. As Ro looks to the right, she sees the glitter in the debris and it appears to be fairly large. Cerul thinks it looks like a silver pole or cylinder. Ro fires a firebolt and takes out a mushroom. One of the mushrooms on each side starts to move closer to us. It looks like the whole room starts to shift. Next round they will be on top of us. Kaizo throws a dagger at a mushroom and it falls. Kaizo recommends to the party that we should take them out at a distance. Ham decides to try to dash across the cavern and makes it to the other side where it opens up and bends to the left. One mushroom takes an opportunity attack on him and Ham takes poison damage. A few spores sink into him and he hears “Ezulgarutt will get you” telepathically in his head. Cerul firebolts a mushroom and it explodes. Affy takes out her longbow and obliterates a mushroom. Ro takes out another. There are about a dozen left.

Fluttering is starting to come through the air. We hear “Ezulgarutt” echoing over and over and have to make a bonus action to make a wisdom saving throw or have disadvantage on the attack. Kaizo fails the wisdom save but makes his attack, slicing the mushroom with his sword. He keeps running to the end of the cavern. Ham sees a rock land near his feet that came from the end of the tunnel. He picks up the rock but doesn’t see anything distinctive about it. He turns and fires a dart at the mushroom behind him and takes it out. There are 10 mushrooms left. Cerul fails his wisdom save, and runs out between them and burst Radiance of the Dawn in a 30 ft radius and took out all the remaining mushrooms. The telepathic voice is gone.

The silver object shines brightly in the radiant light from Cerul. It appears to be something covered up but Cerul sees a small flickering of green. Cerul lights up his shield. Affy nudges the silver thing with her foot - it might have been one of their weapons. It’s a huge silver club with a jade gemstone on the end.

Ham and Fungus continue forward and the ground shifts beneath their feet. They fall into a 15x15 ft pit with a large formless mass that begins to rise. They take bludgeoning, slashing, and piercing damage from their fall. Affy heard them yell and hastens forward to help her friends. The ooze creeps up, moving very slowly until it’s about 5 ft away. Its greyish form extends upward and the pseudopod form turns into a brain shape and looks towards Ham. He takes 16 pts of psychic damage and goes down. Fungus misty steps out and tries to take Ham out, but can’t misty step with him. He gives 10 temp hp to Ham.

Ham stands up, his head throbbing. He sees a grey ooze reaching toward him. Ham casts mirror image. The side of the pit is 20 ft on an angle. The rest of the party dashes to try to catch up. Affy throws a trident down into the ooze and hits. The trident falls out. The ooze, unaffected by mirror image, swings its pseudopod towards Ham and misses. Fungus casts Sorceror’s Burst at the ooze and causes psychic damage. Ham casts Command and tells the ooze to flee. Cerul throws a rope down over the edge. Kaizo throws his last dagger at the ooze and misses. Ro creates a ledge halfway up the wall. Affy fires her longbow at the ooze and hits, causing Slow. On his turn, the ooze flees 10 ft away towards the back of the pit. Fungus casts Sorceror’s Burst and causes some fire damage. Ham tries to climb the wall using the ledge, and then uses the rope to get to the top. Cerul casts Toll the Dead on the ooze. Kaizo holds his action in case the ooze comes up. Ro tries to use firebolt and it appears to be resistant. Affy fires her bow again but rolls a nat 1. The brain forms as it moves 10 ft towards the party. It attacks Ro with its brain but fails. Fungus casts thunder damage on the ooze. Cerul Tolls the Dead again and the ooze is whittled down to a much smaller ooze. Ham fires and misses and retreats from the edge. Ro’s Toll the Dead takes it out. Affy jumps down to retrieve her trident. She looks around and finds 3 gemstones: azurite, blue quartz, and turquoise (10 gp each).

Fungus picks up the silver club (the jade gemstone is worth 150 gp). We hear laughter from further down the corridor. We skirt the lip of the pit and continue down the corridor, with Kaizo scouting ahead. He gets a whiff of smoke down the corridor to the left. To the right, he sees a room with a stale feeling - the scent has more of rusty, coppery smell. The ceiling is low, and the room is filled with torn furs and scraps. In the far corner there is a hole and the edges seem fairly fresh. He catches the glint of a wiry antenna sticking up - a twitching form the size of a dog, but much fatter and rounder. It appears to be on top of a shield gnawing on it. Kaizo points to the hole and casts Druidcraft in the hole, creating the faint odor of a skunk. He sees the antenna pop up and look around and then turns back to gnaw on the shield. It looks like a gold bug eating a shield. Ro has read of a creature called a “rust monster” that feeds on metal.

Kaizo stealths and continues down the corridor. Stairs descend into a huge chamber but the hallway continues to circle around. In the chamber the ground is uneven, littered with stone, rotting plant matter, mold, and bioluminescent fungus. In the center is a makeshift throne of giant rocks, with a gnoll sitting. His fur is matted, his eyes are glassy, and he is puffing on a hookah. Blue smoke swirls through the air. Kaizo notices he is hovering slightly above his throne. There are other gnolls in the room and they are sitting in a stupor. Kaizo returns to the group to describe what he saw. He thinks there were 3-5 gnolls, but it was hard to tell in the thick smoke.

Kaizo hears a voice saying “Come on, Nazex wants you. Go take it! Go take it!” The voice is coming from the room. Affy moves forward and fires her bow but misses. Kaizo uses Druidcraft to snuff out the hookah flame. Ham casts Command on chief (gnoll pack leader) and another gnoll, so they halt. The other two gnolls shoot with their bow at Kaizo, who uses a reaction to deflect the attack, minimizing the damage. The other gnoll shoots at Ham, causing piercing damage. Ro shoots a firebolt at a gnoll causing minor damage. Cerul uses Toll the Dead on the same gnoll, but he saves. Cerul casts Spiritual Weapon and drops it on the gnoll’s head, but it misses. Affy enters the cavern and strikes with her greatsword but rolls a nat 1. She uses Action Surge and takes an additional attack and hits with the sword, adding a psionic blast. She notices that there is a gnoll laying on its side. Kaizo jumps in to attack the same gnoll as Affy. He swings his short sword and hits. With his offhand he strikes but misses. He uses Flurry of Blows to cause more damage and the gnoll falls. Fungus runs into the cavern and casts Hex on the chief and he has disadvantage on Wisdom saving throws. Ham casts Command on the chief (AKA “hookah boy”) and he will stay there. A gnoll attacks Fungus and pierces him with his claw, but he makes his concentration save. A gnoll fires at Kaizo, and another swings at Affy. Ro hits the chief with Toll the Dead. Cerul hits the chief with his Spiritual Weapon and Tolls the Dead on another gnoll. A small creature, a quasit, joins the fight. A pulse of the fungi releases necrotic energy. Affy, Ham, and Cerul fail the con saving throw, but Cerul has resistance so doesn’t take damage. The quasit strikes at Fungus. Affy attacks and Action Surges. Kaizo attacks with his short sword and unarmed strike. He uses Flurry of Blows. Ham Commands the Chief again to Halt. Fungus attacked the quasit. The quasit returns the attack but misses. A gnoll fumbles his attack and the other shoots at Fungus and misses. Ro hits the chief with Magic Missile. Cerul attacks the chief and another gnoll with Toll the Dead.

The room fungus pulses again and the party takes necrotic damage. Quasit poofs across the room. Affy targets another gnoll but misses. Kaizo takes down a gnoll with his bare fists. Fungus smacks the chief. Ham takes down the chief and his hookah explodes. Gnoll moves and Affy gets opportunity attack but misses. He shoots Ham. Ro heals Ham. Cerul starts to glow and sprouts wings from his back (huh, so that’s what kind of feather it is). Cerul bursts Radiance and two gnolls fail and both go down, and quasit takes damage. The room fungus starts to pulse but dies down with the pack leader gone. Quasit disappears with a snicker. Fungus casts Faerie Fire but nothing is illuminated. Cerul casts See Invisibility and sends his Spiritual Weapon after the quasit and takes him down.

The pack leader was Gnazex - he has a ring with a garnet gemstone that looks valuable. He has a small pouch with 2 buds of nightbud in it - Kaizo takes both.. He also has a potion of healing on him - Ham puts it in inventory. Fungus identifies the ring as a ring of fire resistance.

– The End of the Session –

Elle’berian Shrine

Cackletongue Lair - 27th of Talil, 1246

We decide to head back to town…to rest up and seek out the Captain to tell him what happened at the Silent Grove and the gnoll caves. Cerul talks about how surprised he was about the wings that emerged…it starts to make sense why the quasit mentioned “the stink of angel blood.” It was Cerul…he has celestial blood. Ham and Affy talk about “daddy issues” and “son of a whore” issues…and the fact that we are a group of misfits.

By the time we get back to O’Nearen, it is around noon. Ham is itching to use the cube. Cerul and Kaizo and Ro stand 30’+ away. Affy and Fungus stand with Ham, trying to pay attention. Ham rolls a [04] - he feels more vertically mobile, as do Affy and Fungus. They can climb at a speed equal to our movement speed - [Climber]. These three can try to replicate this in the future (arcana and history are the skills that will help). We don’t notice this, however, since we aren’t climbing at the moment. Kaizo jokes that the three grew horns.

Half-orc Visitors

Ham hands the cube back to Fungus (he puts it in a side pouch). As we enter the town, we see a group of 4, then 5, who stand out…because these are half orcs. Affy looks at them - they are not familiar, but she observes they are all wearing a silver pendant…that reminds me of a story that I heard when I was little from B’denfu (she told me that my father wore a pendant like that). Affy tells the group she’s going to talk to them. About that time, the barmaid walks out and accuses them of not paying their tab and glares at me. Affy steps in and tries to act like there was a misunderstanding - is able to persuade the group to take part and also tries to tell the realmwardens to back down. Affy goes inside and pays the 1gp tab. Kaizo flips her 3gp and we all go inside to eat. Tavrus (who Affy called Rolf) and his 4 buddies, Affy, and Ham go inside to eat. Affy, Ham and the half orcs sit down. Tavrus asks what is up…Affy lays into them. They are travelling - heading from town to town, not working. They hate people turning their noses to them. Affy and Ham think this isn’t the entire truth. Ham challenges them to a card game. Ham wins the first round and 1gp. He then tries again - with the pendant on the line now. He wins the pendant and Tavrus is mad. Ham offers him some coin for the story. Calden was a prestigious guy who they were following around the city 2 years ago. They could tell he was loaded. They stole the pendant from him. Ham pays them 7gp for the pendant and the information.

The Herbalist

Kaizo heads to the herbalist. Talks to Meledra about the nightbud, tells her it has all been destroyed except what he has. He proposes that she reserve a back room as a sanctuary for the three elders for a plant he has druid-crafted from a seed in front of her. For every nightbud, one seed goes to the sanctuary for anyone coming in looking for balance, harmony, and transcendence: those get it for free if available. She can sell whatever other seeds it makes. She agrees.

Hall of O’naren

At the hall, greeted by male half elf. Asks what they are here for…and they say the reward. They mention Ham, and he says “oh - Ambassador Hamalex….” and he is more helpful then. He goes down the hall and opens double doors, but Cerul and Fungus can’t see what’s going on. The guy returns and asks if he can fetch Ham…says there is other business that involves “the Ambassador.” Cerul says he will run and get him, while Fungus asks for a drink. They discuss wine options. Cerul gets Affy and Ham and Cerul cleans up Ham on the way back. They collect Kaizo on the way back. When they arrive, Fungus says, “Oh look - it is the Ambassador.”

The hall overseer, Caddius, is there to greet the group upon the return. Opens the doors and sees an elongated chamber, benches, raised dias, banners…and realmwardens. Looks like official business. Ham is keen to hear the use of the Ambassador title. Captain steps forward and is glad to hear the news that the gnolls were taken care of - send Caddius to fetch something. There is an elf - regal looking - in the chamber, too. Allow me to introduce Ravus Eldoran. He says I’ve just arrived from Qual’dynn…there is interest (bowing to Ham) in certain diplomatic matters that we should discuss. We all take seats. He has come from Oldport, has been to Qual’dynn…he is not from Oldport. He says that there have been mishaps in Oldport and that he has heard good things about Ham. Says Ham’s name has been damaged, but not beyond repair. Captain then share news - just received info that Qual’dynn has shut its gates. King Highvale has suspicions that there are evil doings there and has shut the doors. We sent a courier there, but have not heard back yet. About that time, Caddius walks in with 50gp reward for the gnoll problem. Ravus asks if we have heard of the Rel Udomina…and the Captain says we have talked about them. He says they are the reason why the gates have been closed in Qual’dynn. And says that we have an opportunity to help them out…which can also restore Ham’s reputation. They are concerned about the Courier and the message he was carrying (from the Lord of O'naren to King Highvale). We ask about the mapmaker - he has disappeared. Tinvere Milan is the Courier (an elf). We discuss in what capacity we will go forward to find the courier - as official reps of O'naren? Ravus says that they would like us to rid the lands of the Rel Udomina. This will be official business to find the courier, but unofficial for the rest. Captain agrees to write a letter. Fungus asks about coin. They offer 10g/each to accept the quest and when/if we return with information, will pay us an additional 25gp/each. They agree to cover expenses (will reimburse us up to 40gp). Fungus says if we get more news, maybe we can get more coin? They said if we can do something to resolve the problem, we will get more coin - and would probably get an audience with the Lord of O'naren. Qual’dynn is a 2 day trip.

We leave the Town Hall and head to the Inn and Tavern. Fungus plans to drink himself silly, while the rest want to rest. Rooms at the Nestling Sword Inn are 5sp, 8sp, or 2gp. We spend 10sp for rooms for Ro and Affy, 8gp for rooms for Cerul, Kaizo, Ham, and Fungus. We take long rest. We decide to head out at first light.

The first day has uneventful travel. We pass people heading to O'naren since Qual’dynn was closed. We ask about conditions and specifically about the courier, but they reply that there are lots of people near the city trying to get in the gates.

Before making camp, Affy and Ham stand close (10’) to Kaizo as Fungus pulls out the cube on top of Kaizo (Cerul and Ro are not close by). He didn’t try to replicate anything - just rolled. Fungus rolled a 78 - as he makes the final turn we hear a noise and a bright light emerges behind Kaizo’s head and he is glowing. Fungus gets a +1 charisma bonus that is permanent [Vibrant]. The climbing buff disappeared at dawn. We now make camp. We set up a watch, but the night passes uneventfully.

29th of Talil

The next day we wake up. Affy wants to try to replicate the roll from the previous night. Fungus is next to Affy, but everyone else stands clear. Affy rolls a 13 - and cannot replicate the turns (fails history check). Affy freezes up on the last movement. The cube starts to make her arm translucent - like glass. Then her entire body is glassy…and she shakes it off…but now she is semi-translucent. She feels like her movement speed is reduced by 5’. Armor class is now 13 as a base (crystallized skin). This is a noticeable difference in appearance, but won’t change her AC since she wears armor. She’s upset, but Fungus says it is sexy. Affy dirties herself up with dirt and mud as they head out.

Pinprick’s Peculiarities

Road winds ahead. Around noon, we spot a splash of color - deep purple, reds, golds - on a round wagon. [We did NOT use horses for this trip] Looks like a barrel…and it is pulled by a mini shaggy, red elk (not bigger than a pony). See gnome flailing at us…he is a travelling merchant (what will it be - fortune or folly). He opens the wagon and we see Pinprick’s Peculiarities sign…if you don’t need it, you are boring. What’ll you have? Fungus says what do you have for me. He shows him a magic blanket (full rest in 4 hours) - he buys it for 25gp. He glances at Ham and says I have what you need and pulls out a little vial, an inkpot and says dip your quill in that. Ham does and the ink is green (mood ink - based on how he feels). The inkpot also giggles. It is 30gp. Ham says he will consider buying it, but asks first if Pinprick has seen the courier. He hasn’t…then points to Cerul and shows him a snail shell. You speak into it and the message will be delivered to anyone he wants (5gp). Fungus says he will buy it (Snail of Echoing Whispers). Kaizo asks what he has for an old man. Pinprick shows him a powder and an extract…and then decides he would rather offer it to Ro.

He looks over at Ro…that shimmer, that is not common glass. May I see that (the small orb pendant that Ro wears around her neck)? Ro lets him see it. Pinprick has a fit about this…he says this is a magical device that is humming (Ro has never heard the hum)...he says he saw this once before in a ruined amphitheatre that was swallowed by the Seameet (Seameet is swamplands…brackish water). He is out of sorts after handling the item. He doesn’t want to buy or take it - just says it is very powerful.

He changes the subject - asks if Ro wants the powder (gives you darkvision for 10 minutes - Moon Leaf Powder) - he has two of these and Fungus buys them both. Kaizo asks him about the Extract Pinprick has - it makes thorns grow on you (+1 AC for 10 minutes - for 20gp). Kaizo makes him a counteroffer - 15gp and an offer to sell Nightbud (tells him who to contact in O'naren, the herbalist). He agrees - and Kaizo gets an Ironthorn Extract. Fungus talks to Pinprick about joining him…they agree that when Fungus gets his own cart and his own Mifflehoof, he can. Affy asks if he has something for her. He mentions a ball of endless twine. Then a glowball to see in the dark for an hour (he has 12, will be 60gp) - Fungus buys them all. He throws a vial at Fungus and it falls slowly. He has three of them - these vials protect the contents. It is weightless and never breaks when dropped (would have to take a hammer to it to break it). The vials are 10gp/each. Fungus buys 2 and Kaizo buys 1. Affy asks about the inkpot and persuades him to buy it for 7gp. He does and she gives it to Ham (since he bought her the pendant). Ham plays with it over time (Giggling Inkpot).

Defaced Elle’berian Shrine

We continue on. We see a small stone shine off the path. We notice a symbol (of the Borelian Sway) on it that has been defaced. We see kneeling, cloaked figures at the shrine. They see us (green/cream cloaks) and smile. One stands up and approaches - “it is a shame - even the sacred places aren’t safe these days.” He indicates that he and his companions are going to leave. Ham asks who they are. They are lonesome pilgrims. Ham asks Fungus to clean up the shrine and he does. These people are male elf, female half elf (the speaker), two humans (who look alike), a dwarf (wearing elvish cloak), and a tiefling. Ham asks her if she knows who did this…and tells her it was the Rel Udomina. She asks how he knows and he says it is a hunch. They act like they don’t know who that is, but Kaizo picks up that she is full of crap. He asks her “are you sure you don’t know who that group is?” They don’t like being questioned…it is Initiative.

Fungus lights up one bad guy and Affy with faerie fire. Ro steps back and firebolts the one who is lit up and hits. Affy hits the same guy with her sword. Ham gives Affy bardic inspiration and then casts dissonant whispers on the same guy and hits, killing him (exploding head). He then unarmed strikes the female and hits. Kaizo attacks the female with a sword and fumbles, then misses twice. Cerul casts a spell on all bad guys but one (radiance of the dawn) and they all failed their save. Dwarf shoots at Cerul and misses. Another moves and shoots and misses Cerul. Another moves away and misses. The female misses and disengages and flees. Another shoots a crossbow at Fungus and hits, with poison damage. Ro fires magic missiles at the dwarf. Fungus casts sorcerous burst on the tiefling and misses. Affy moves to the female “surrender and you can live”. Ham dissonant whispers the one Affy warned, doing some damage. Kaizo swiftly moved to the other female but missed with his short sword. Cerul fumbles with a firebolt and also misses with spiritual weapon. Ham was hit with piercing and poison. One took cover behind a tree. One with a crossbow misses Kaizo and takes cover behind a tree. One hits Ro for a little damage. One casts thunderwave on Kaizo but he saves and only takes some of the effect. One ran, but Kaizo gets in an opportunity attack and does some damage. Fungus misty steps by one behind a tree and shilelegh’s but misses. Ro casts toll the dead on one and does serious damage. Affy dashes and attacks for a critical hit bringing one of the women down. Ham casts a 2nd level dissonant whispers knocking out another one. Kaizo attacks one of the 2 remaining hitting for some piercing damage, and follows up with flurry of blows adding a little. Cerul tried to hit one behind a tree, but she saved. He moved closer to the tree. Kaizo is double teamed by the last two bad guys and is hit twice. Fungus misty steps and uses sorcerous burst on the one on Kaizo and hits. Ro moves and misses with a firebolt, but uses sorcery to hit and do some damage. Affy dashes forward and asks “who wants to surrender and live?” Ham dashes over. Kaizo hits the guy on his rear with his little sword and then uses flurry of blows and hits once and misses once. Cerul tolls the dead and they save. One attacks Kaizo and misses. The other tries to hit him with a spell and misses. Fungus hits the one on Kaizo and knocks him prone. Ro moves and tries to firebolt and misses. Affy dashes and tries to knock one out, but crits and kills him by pushing her sword hilt through his eye socket. She makes one last attempt to get the last guy to surrender (he tells her to f—off). Ham tries to whisper to one and she saves, but still takes damage and dies. They are all dead.

Ro - 17 hit points, spell slots: uses 2 sorcery points

We then rifle through their belongings. We find normal stuff - leather armor, long bows, etc. Fungus collects some of these things with the intent to sell. We notice that the dwarf had a folded vellum parchment in a pouch. It has a silver wax emblem, resembling a severed chain link. We have suspicions that this is an organization’s symbol vs. a person, but we aren’t sure what it is. We open the parchment to see what it says. It is in code and is elvish. Ham takes a stab to decode. “The target’s location will be exposed once the final sigil is retrieved. Strike the diplomat before he reaches the sanctuary. No names, no witnesses. Retrieve the charter, destroy the seal. Qual’dynn must not send another envoy.” We notice a torn corner on the side of the piece…says Ambassador Entryvian Vondrell.
