Crescent arch carved into a rock with the word “whisperbend” and then an X…looks like a secret passage (into Qual’dynn?).

We look at the lady next - she was carrying an odd pouch. It has an internal stitching made out of something special - a shadow silk. We think it must be symbolism of some type (like if you carry this it means something). We find a silver signet ring with a symbol that is a stylized rising sun. Cerul recognizes this as the Abbey of Mont Rest. The onyx medallion is cracked. Ham thinks this was likely once a magic item that has been used/burned. This was a mage. We think she must have been at the Abbey or interacted with someone at the Abbey. She used some type of magic. The onyx represents petrification (Ham is able to ascertain - must have turned something into stone at some point).

The Missing Courier

Secret Path into Qal’dynn

In-World29th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Elle’berian Shrine PlayedMay 18th, 2025

The Elle’berian Shrine - 29th of Talil, 1246

We have the sealed scroll and other items. We think we still want to go to the city of Qal’dynn, but will need to find the hidden entrance. As we tidy up, Fungus detects magic - doesn’t sense anything other than with the onyx (petrification - already expended). Ham thinks the people we killed were disguised as people of the land…and were the ones defacing the shrine. They may have been part of the Rel Udomina…we aren’t sure.

Ham is trying to study the map to see what he can see. He’s frustrated with it, but hands it to Affy to check. There is scratching that she notices, but can’t see what it means. Thinks the entrance is on the south side of the city - maybe near a large hill. We will need to get closer to the town to figure it out. We decide to proceed forward and go as far as we can and then make camp. Fungus holds the cube, Ham holds the unopened scroll, Affy holds the map, Cerul has the signet ring and the broken onyx amulet.

Disciples of the Wind

We travel for a few hours and as the sun is disappearing, we see a fire and tents ahead. There are lanterns - green/orange/gold - circus feel. We hear music, smell herbs. We see wagons that are familiar. This is a group of nomads. Fungus approaches, dancing (not very well). They motion us in, offer us food. Tell us we can join their camp for the night. “Oh you like Yavin,” is the comment to Fungus (she’s the one playing, singing).

They introduce themselves as “the disciples of the wind.” Ham and Ro think these people are as they seem. Nothing nefarious. Affy thinks there are around 30+ people - mostly humans, elves, half-elves. Cerul is looking around for musicians - sees the one lady with the dulcimer, and a few people with chimes. Kaizo notices a female human sitting around who looks like the mom of the singer/player. He talks to her about where they are travelling to/from. They said they were trying to go to Qal’dynn…but the gates were shut and no one can enter. They said they are going to O'naren tomorrow. Affy asks who their leader is - and it is the woman. Affy then asks if they would be willing to take a message to someone in the town for us and she says she will. Kaizo comments on the skill of Yavin…and how her mother must be proud. And she says that she is proud. He then asks about her husband (and the girl’s father - who the girl gets her talent from) and she reponds that he is deceased…based on something that happened near Palason back in the day. Cerul asks Yavin if she will play his little flute and she does. She asks where he got it and he tells her he’s always had it - isn’t sure where it came from. He is happy to hear what it sounds like. She tries to teach Cerul how to play it. Affy asks the mom about the Palason incident and Affy sees that the lady is uncomfortable. Affy tells her about the “roads are safer tonight” because of the fight they had earlier in the day. Her name is Eraline - she is the “way god” for the disciples. She asks Affy about where she’s from, but Affy dodges. Conversation turns to Cembus and how he provides safe travels. Affy says she will say a prayer to their god tonight.

As the night passes, Ham talks to members of the party about going to find the gate tomorrow. Kaizo overhears the gnome talk about “I don’t know where Pearson went - he does this all the time.” He asks about this Pearson…and finds out that this person is the merchant we encountered: Pinprick. We reveal that we encountered him. The gnome asks if we saw her treasures. Kaizo ends up pulling out the unbreakable vial he purchased and dumps the seeds in a pouch and then presents her with the vial. He asks her to smell it and she says it isn’t hers - smells too skunky - and gives it back. She asks us to join them if we want and asks us what to expect in O'naren. Kaizo tells her we took care of dangers on the road and describes the town.

Kaizo then goes back to talk to Eraline - he asks what guides her. She says they are one with the earth, led by Cembus. Affy asks if she has been to Bactdelm. Kaizo tells her to seek out the herbalist in O'naren. She asks if we were aware of the rare pools in that town and we tell her that they kept Fungus out of the pools. She laughs and replies that they are elven pools.

The group gathers before going to sleep. Ro is going to write a note to the Captain - telling him what happened on the road (we found the dead courier, have the sealed scroll that we are planning to try to take to the king). Kaizo talks to Fungus about the vials that he has. Fungus feels a little bad, but isn’t sure he will say anything or do anything. Affy thinks we should keep a watch as well, but most of the group wants to sleep. Affy will keep watch part of the night. Kaizo will meditate by the fire. Ro and Jasper will sleep by the fire. Night is fairly uneventful.

Visit from Tanana

Not long after Fungus goes to sleep, he wakes up a little disoriented - having had a riddle in his head - see below. And wakes up. Hears fluttering. More rhymes. He looks around and everyone is asleep. He feels a touch on his left and hears more rhymes in his head. Then he looks to the right and hears more in his left. Then hears more rhymes. Then he sees a small fairy. “I’m Tenonna, she sent me” and disappears. Fungus knows this is related to his patron.

These are the rhymes he hears from the fairy:

“Dreamy Fungus, so overgrown,

Power's path is not your own.

Come from grime and shadow-fed,

Shoddy’s secrets bloom ahead.”

“Witchlight fingers, borrowed breath—

You cast with gifts not truly left.

A bargain blooms where roots entwine,

In feybound soil and warped design.

“She weaves with twine and tattered thread,

A crooked crone with thoughts unread.

She feels you so, dear mushroom child,

But love from hags? Now that’s wild.”

“She’ll ask you for a root once bound,

A glyph that grows in buried ground.

But watch, dear Fungus, what you seek,

For glyphs may whisper when they speak…”

When we all wake up, Kaizo just looks at Fungus (not knowing what happened) - just trying to make him feel guilty about the goods he has. We got a long rest. This area is the Cradlecrest…hills, low mountains. We head towards Qal’dynn. We travel for a few hours in the morning until we finally see the city start to come into view. It is very Elven…mixed in with the natural surroundings (the city walls are beige and meld into the hills). We see tall spires behind the walls - adjusted for the hills inside the city. “Beige” feel, very pretty and nice. We approach on the river side - Raelura River. Affy tries to study the map is is overwhelmed by the city. She makes out the river and walls, but not much else. Knows that we need to go to the southside of the city. We approach in a valley between the hills and river. Pass people who have been turned away. We know it is going to be on the outside walls on the right, through the woods and forest. The river cuts through the city. We decide to go to the gates first - to try to get inside the city. Fungus wants to go to the front of the line, but we end up waiting our turn.

Qal’dynn

We finally make it to the front of the line and see the wardens with a swan symbol on their chests. Fungus says, “You have a swan.” Ham then steps forward and tries to talk to the gate keepers - saying we have a message for the king. They claim that they have heard this all day. They pull out messages…some look real, some not. Ham then speaks in elven and introduces himself. Ham tries - asks if they know who Ravus is. They said they would pass on a message to him, but won’t let us in. Affy then questions him since Ravus isn’t inside the walls. Just tells them they shouldn’t name drop unless they know who is or is not in the town. They call her an orc…she turns and walks away, spitting as she goes.

Group leaves the gate area and heads into the woods, following the walls to the right. Ham tries to find the entrance. We think we find a path - that was trying to be hidden. We go through the dense area, trees, hills, rocks, etc. Path is not a trail…but we finally get to an area that dead ends into the hillside. Tall hill, walls of city, dense shrub. We would not have found this except for the map.

Secret Passage

Affy starts to inspect the rock for signs of an entrance to the hidden area. Her hand passes through the rock and feels hurt. She pulls back and feels pain in her hand. She is poisoned. She says “whisperbend” and tries again and goes completely through the rock. Ham follows. It is very dark inside as a tunnel opens up. Ham casts a spell to see if he can light anything up and finds that it is dark because the tunnel turns to the left immediately. He messages Cerul and he says the password and comes through. Then Fungus goes through. Then Ro. Then Kaizo. We decide to follow the tunnel. Affy and Cerul lead (Cerul lights up his shield), followed by Kaizo and Ham, then Ro and Fungus. We find that the light is magically suppressed. We realize we need magical light to see. We circle around, then it bends the other way…follow it for a while. So far the tunnel is all earthen. Eventually, the path opens into a broad cavern and at the center is a hulking statue 20’ in front of a door. Nothing is lit in the room, but we make out this large figure. We advance forward and it is Initiative.

The Guardian

This figure looks strong and bulky - a construct of clay or earth. Ham tries to cast a spell and it saves. Kaizo takes a glowing marble, advance forward, speaks the password, and hold his action. It attacks Kaizo and Affy (hits both). Fungus attacks (hexes him). Cerul attacks with Radiance of the Dawn (it saves). Ro attacks - casts Magic Missile. Affy attacks with her trident, hits and knocks him prone. Ham commands him to halt and he does (no movement, loses a turn, effectively). Kaizo attacks with advantage and hits. He misses his turn. Fungus attacks and misses and steps back and the statue hits him. Cerul hits with a rapier using truestrike. Ro hits him with magic missile. Affy hits him. Ham tries to command again and it saves. Kaizo attacks and hits. Then it his Kaizo and Affy. Cerul attacks with his rapier and hits and heals Kaizo. Fungus misty steps and heads for the door. Then hexes him and taunts him. Ro casts magic missile again. Affy hits him and knocks him prone, also dealing magic force damage. Ham commands again and he hits (it is held). Kaizo hits and also flurry of blows. Cerul hits with truestrike and his rapier. Ro tries to hit with mold earth. Affy hits and then uses action surge and hits again and takes him down. Long fight!

As the fog clears, our darkvision comes back. We check him out…he is made of stones and earth. Fungus takes a stone as a souvenir. We look around the room. We see a door in the far wall. It is an ancient stone door with carvings on the door frame. Ham reads the “Society of the Aetherion Academy” on the door frame. Cerul knows this is one of the primary universities in the Borelian Sway. Knows they are located in Qal’dynn. Cerul comes to investigate the door…he finds a spot under moss and lichen that looks almost like a heart with jagged edges. Fungus goes to beat on the statue and he finds a gem shaped like a heart inside…and uses it to open the door. Cerul puts the gem in the hole. The door opens and slides to the slide, revealing a staircase going up. We take the 100gp gem (amethyst) once the door is open.

– The End of the Session –

30th of Talil, 1246 | Secret Passage

We decide we are going to go up the stairs. Ro and Jasper decide to stay back in the chamber to stand watch. Kaizo goes up to check at the top of the stairs and finds nothing. Strange Chamber

Secret Chamber

We close the iron door and look at the new door Affy revealed. This new opening leads to a 5’ hallway that goes 15’ that opens into a small room on the right and another hallway on the left and a hall continues beyond the small room. We see a small shelf in the room area - there are books on it and a small chest next to it. Ham checks and thinks the chest is trapped. He tries to disarm the trap (everyone else stands back). A needle shoots out and poisons him. Cerul heals him as Fungus unlocks the chest. We find a red flask, lots of sp and some gp. These documents are incriminating of that cult…and plans to overthrow towns, leaders, etc. Ham carries the papers in his pack.

Ladder to Trap Door

To the left of this small room, the hallway continues to a grooved wall that looks like a ladder heading up. Kaizo stealthily climbs up the stairs, but Affy grabs him to stop him. Fungus explored the other passage and found a dead end.

Pedestal Room

Affy goes to check and finds a door to push…she pushes on it until she hears the click. She listens and doesn’t hear anything except a slight humming. Affy pushes it open into a well lit 25’ diameter circular chamber. Around the room are violet couches, a curved path ahead with stairs leading up, and a small pedestal in the center of the room that is giving off a small glow. Affy sees that there are sconces in the room that open doors…and closes the door they came through. The group heads to see the pedestal. It has a thick stone base and a mirrored, almost glass top. Fungus looks into the glass and sees an image of a grand elven hall. He sees what looks to be King Highvale and three advisors. Fungus hears conversation, but doesn’t make out the words. He can tell the king seems unsettled. One advisors has sigils floating around his head (arcane energy). The king is tapping his blade. Words in elvish (now that Ham and Kaizo have stepped forward)...the king is concerned about his position and the city. Ham realizes this is a scrying tool…someone is spying on the king. We pick up the glass and take it with us…wrapping a bedroll and ropes around it.

Commons Area

We decide to go up the stairs that lead from this room. Kaizo leads the way and stealths. It ends in an iron door. Kaizo hears people speaking elvish on the other side. He isn’t sure how many people he hears, but knows it is more than two people. Ham and Kaizo decide to open the door and pose as part of the group. Fungus, Affy, and Cerul stay back…if they say “Affy” we come in guns blazing; if they say “Fungus” we come in as hired hands.

They enter a large square room with 4 guys in it who ask “what are you doing here?” Each wall has an iron door. There are several bookshelves on the walls in between all of the doors. Lots and lots of books. There are 4 tables and the guys are standing in the middle, saying “I don’t believe we’ve met.” Kaizo says “I thought we’d see you earlier” and Ham says “We are looking for books on the history of Qal’dynn.” They say - those books are over here…did The Prominent send you down? Ham: No - Esinni didn’t. They: We don’t call him that. Why did you come through that door? Ham: We got lost in the tunnels. IT IS INITIATIVE.

Fight happens. Two with short swords and two with crossbows…all using poison. Kaizo hits one. Then both Ham and Kaizo are hit. Cerul runs in and casts a spell that hits all 4. Fungus runs in and kills one with a spell. Ham asks if anyone wants to parlay…since Fungus took one down quickly…and casts command on two of them. One fails (he is halted). Affy hits one. Kaizo hits one with his weapon and then again unarmed and kills him and starts to search him. One attacks Ham, but Cerul distracts him and he misses. The other attacks Cerul, even though Fungus tried to distract him, and hits with damage and poison. Cerul attacks and misses and heals Ham. Fungus uses poison spray to hit one and kills him. With one left, Ham tries to persuade him to talk. Then Affy hits him “you should have taken his deal, you @&*(& elf” and kills him. We take a few moments to search the people and the books. No armor - just clothes - they have hand crossbows and short swords (Fungus takes them all, except one of the short swords that Affy takes) and thieves’ tools (Fungus x 2, Ham, Kaizo) and one flask of poison (Fungus). They also have 48sp and 8gp. We also find small tattoo on inside of the forearm (flame with mask of stern grimace) of each person. We think it is probably the symbol of the Rel U’domina. Affy takes a short sword and cuts the tattoos off all 4 arms. We take bodies to the pedestal room and put them on the couches, as Fungus cleans up the mess from the fight.

Hired Guard

We decide to listen at the door the guys pointed to before they died. Kaizo hears nothing. Ham hears something but it is too far away to make out. Ham and Kaizo open the door…same plan as before. The halls turns…(Ham messages Affy to bring the others and follow). Kaizo stealths to look down the hallway. He hears the sharpening of a blade (rock on steel) to the passage to the north and sees a small alcove and a passage that turns to the right. Group catches up and then stops, as Ham and Kaizo make the turn, heading toward the alcove. They see a guy sitting in the alcove in armor with javelins on his back, sharpening a long sword, a shield to the side. Him: I don’t remember seeing you come through. Kaizo: I think the others need you Him: What are you talking about, what do you mean they need me? Kaizo: They want you at the library (persuade). Him: I don’t think you are from these parts. Ham steps in, but the guy looks at Kaizo’s arm and doesn’t see the tattoo. INITIATIVE.

Kaizo crits. Fungus runs in (hearing the fight) and tries to spray him and misses. Affy runs in (saying Affy) and hits. Cerul runs in and casts and spell and misses. Ham tries to talk to him and asks him to throw his weapons down (persuasion). The guy then says “gold speaks” and Ham shakes his head and he attacks Kaizo, but Kaizo deflects some of the damage with a reaction. He then tries to attack Affy and misses. Ham continues to babble…(thy runneth they mouth). Kaizo attacks and misses, then tries with his fist and misses. Fungus tries to spray him again and hits. Affy hits again with her sword. Cerul tolls the dead on him. Ham tries to persuade again. The guy backs down and says he was hired to watch this place (if you don’t see the symbol on the arm, kill them). He was paid 5gp to watch the hall. Affy pulls out one of the skin tattoos and shows him, and the guy is grossed out. Affy asks his name (Vognar)...he lives in the city. Cerul questions why the city is closed - he doesn’t know much. We ask if we think we could go through the University (through the spiral staircase…through the secret door…and through the study hall). He thinks we could get out if we went unnoticed. We talk to him about needing to get his wife and kids and flee the city, but decide to let him stay put and “do his job” and tell no one he has seen us. He pulls out a kit to heal himself and Fungus cleans up the area. Affy threatens to take care of his wife and kids should he betray him. He says he will do what we’ve asked and Ham feels he is telling the truth.

We decide to stay in the tunnels to try to find others in the group, including The Prominent who passed by the guard hours ago. Cerul heals the group, including the guard. Guard tells us that left seems to be where the crowd goes, and right where The Prominent goes more often. Kaizo tells the guard “we are here to save your city and your family - don’t disappoint us.”

We double back the way we came. We go to the library room - Affy listens at the west door and it seems quiet. Ham listens at the south door and it is quiet. Ham opens that door. It is a long hallway, and we pull sconces as we go - looking for hidden doors. We go 70’ and it branches off with a hallway to the right and double doors to the east. Affy listens at the doors and doesn’t hear anything, but she sees double doors to the other side.

The Prominent’s Chamber (?)

She pushes the doors open in front of her - it is a well-lit study room. She sees a desk and a lady standing up with 2 large dogs that leap out from behind her, turning to see a small dragon by my face, as it opens its mouth. She says “Affy” and we stop here for the night.

Affy takes the crowbar and Fungus some caltrops.

We see 6 cells with iron bars and on the far end, a door. The cells are 10x10. We hear a couple of “prisoners” talking.

We don’t see a guard. We see on the far cell on the far left a goblinoid creature. On the second cell on the right we see a young half elf. Affy and Ham walk up to the cells. Half elf looks like he’s been in a while…has no belongings. Keanus Evergaze…says “you aren’t with them, are you?” Affy asks who he is…and he replies. Fungus offers to replace his candle. Affy questions why they have him…he is the nephew of Entryvian (the ambassador, former diplomat). Ham wants to unlock him…asks if he can get us an audience with the king. He says he can.

Goblinoid creature asks to be released as well. Kaizo questions him. He says he was taken from the woods, likely captured to be fed to the dogs. He asks for his socks behind the door (same as Keanus asked for his clothes behind the door, too). His name is Fzoid. Cerul opens the door and returns everything to them. We release the goblin with a jar of pickles from Fungus. We tell him how to leave…and he does.

Kaizo doesn’t think Keanus is who he says he is…rolls insight.

As we get to the pit…we see a natural circular room, 25’ diameter. There is blood everywhere. There are lines of blood that lead to the pit. It appears to be very deep…Cerul throws something down, but we don’t hear it hit the bottom for some time. Looks like a disposal pit.

We head back to the library (room 3). Affy listens at the door, hears crackling fire. There is an elegant chamber with all kinds of clothing hung on hooks and mannequins. There are huge candleholders and mirrors. There is a circular round table in the center with skin dyes, perfumes, disguise kits, etc. Fungus takes a lot of the items in this room. These are clothing from various cities and towns all over Myrdae.

We leave and advance to the guard again. He tells us that “something is coming - we need to leave. Can you show me how you came in so we can get out of here?” He heard something that “doesn’t sound good” and “has never heard this before”- we tell him how to escape and he does. We advance to the door ahead of us to take cover to see what might be coming down the spiral staircase.

The door opens into a large gathering hall with a raised dais and curtain….with lanterns (not sconces). Laying there is a dead body (of a Rel’Udomina agent?). Affy closes the door, allowing the group a chance to talk and strategize. Affy loots the dead guy and he appears to be one of the Rel’Udomina (12sp, a dagger). Keanus tries to leave and Ham can’t grab him. The group takes off after him…he gets to the base of the stairs and Kaizo tries to stun him and he does, falling on the stairs. We had given chase and are now in the hallway. We hear feet coming down the stairs and hear the echoes of boots and sharp clinks. Sounds like a battle above.

We see someone who sees Kaizo…who sees a Qal’dynn Realmwarden who says “Halt. You shouldn’t be here.”

Seelie and Unseelie - Seelie are good and Unseelie are bad.

Ridley is looking for a mage named Rueh’unar (he is a bounty under and the House Ruendor has hired Ridley to find him). Ridley is from Caelora. Lorian doesn’t know where the mage is. We eat lunch here.

Ridley thinks the guy is in this town. He is old and has golden faded hair, piercing blue eyes, and purple and green robes. His bounty is to kill him onsite. We decide to chill out in the park/woods area. Relys decides to make a potion of healing - she buys materials from an herbalist and then makes one. Fungus trades a philter of love potion for 2 potions of healing at the alchemist. Kaizo smokes. Ridley and Affy walk around looking for the mage. RIdley thinks this guy is making it very tough to find him. We all group back up just outside the ring. Fungus notices that Kaizo is smoking…the smoke has a nice fragrance, but the smoke weaves patterns that drift opposite the breeze. He doesn’t notice Fungus approaching…he is in deep meditation. The shadows stretch away and he sees moonlight that isn’t ready to be there. He is as high as a kite. He can see the plant life preparing for winter. The trees seem anxious to bloom in the spring. And then a woman, radiant and gentle appears…gown flows, and she smiles and sits across from him in the air. He hears an aged voice from long ago…familiar. You passed through, but you didn’t push. You pulled.

I am Arasia. I am the Mistborn. I linger where the world sighs.

You’ve brought hope and peace…and Your kindness grows against the dark confined corruption.

You will go where your mentor once sat…where the stones hum with what lies beneath.

Kaizo feels like he is on the right path. Then he comes back…and sees Fungus staring at him. Affy and Ridley show back up and see Fungus staring at Kaizo, who is smiling. Then Relys shows up. The last of the sunlight fades away….

Ridley asks why we are here at night. Fungus tells him we are here about the “budplug” - his new charm. And need to be here to make it work. Relys is very excited to commune with her goddess. The area starts to fall quiet…and the sound of nocturnal creatures can be heard. The two moons start to appear in the sky…as moonlight is cast on the ground. Runes on the ground start to glow…and almost look like ripples on a pond. Center of the ring is more intense. Fungus and Relys step into the center; Affy about 15’ from center; Kaizo and Ridley about 50’ from center.

Arcane threads start to emerge and embrace Fungus - almost like a mother holds a son. Fungus then hears - “you do not know me, my little root. But I know you and watch you. Embrace the energy and grow. And I will call upon you when I need you.” He sees light consumed into the charm. He now has “The Charm of the Slumbering Grove.”

A sharp sound emerges. 5 portals open. Eladrin step through the portals. A 6th figure steps through - a tall Eladrin, and addresses Fungus. “You walk a path that leads to darkness and pain. Give me that charm.” Another speaks “Just kill him Glazia - just take it back to Lord Selbranan (Lord of the Fey).” Silence you fool…now hand it over. IT IS INITIATIVE.

  • ROUND ONE: Fungus casts spells and disappears. One casts a spell at Affy and a spiritual weapon appears next to her and hits. Another attacks Relys and hits. Relys shifts into a Saber and pounces on the leader. Misses with a claw. Kaizo attacks one and misses, then attacks again and hits, then attacks again and misses. Ridley then steps forward and casts hunters mark on one and shoots her and hits twice. The leader attacks the Saber and hits once. Affy runs toward one and attacks and hits twice. The one hit by Ridley’s bolts points at Ridley and hits twice. The one on Kaizo attacks Kaizo with a thunderwave. The last one holds staff out and casts a spell on the saber and misses.

Spiritual weapon attacks the saber and hits. Weapon attacks Ridley and misses. Affy has to make a wisdom save and does. Relys pounces on the leader and hits with a claw and misses with a bite. Kaizo flips back and attacks the one on him and fumbles, losing his reaction. He then hits and hits twice more. Ridley hunters mark the one he had before and shoots arrows, hitting and killing her. Pixie dust floats into the air when she dies and he sees a ring fall from chest height and land on the ground. He then shoots the one on Kaizo and hits him, knocking him back 10’…and then he moves away. The leader stands up, pulls out a seed and pops it on the ground and a gate opens up - she looks like she might want to make a move to the portal (uses dodge). It also turns into pixie dust and she sees a dagger fall to the ground as everything else disappears…and she dashes toward the middle of the circle. One bad guy looks at Fungus and points his staff and casts a spell and hits. Another moves to the middle, and points at Fungus and misses with their spell.
  • ROUND THREE: Fungus attacks the leader and misses with poison spray. And he misty steps toward the portal…blocking the way. The leader fails a saving throw and takes damage, while the other mage is okay. Bad guy draws out a dagger with a glowing blade and rushes Fungus and misses. Spiritual weapon attacks Affy and misses. Relys attacks the leader and misses. Kaizo runs up to the leader and attacks and hits her, killing her. When she dies, pixie dust flies into the air and plate mail drops on the ground. He then attacks another one and hits him three times. Ridley moves his hunters mark and shoots and hits twice, killing him. Pixie dust floats up and a dagger falls to the ground. Affy dashes over and hits one twice. One throws down a seed and opens a portal and tries to step through - Affy misses her opportunity attack, as does Fungus. The being passes through the portal. The last one points at Fungus and casts a spell and hits and runs toward the portal.
  • ROUND FOUR: Fungus tries to poison spray her…and misses. Relys pounces on her and misses. Kaizo attacks her and misses three times, but hits once, killing her. Pixie dust flies up and a staff falls…the staff has a valuable gem in it. The portal disappears.

We collect the items that fell: 2 daggers, plate mail, a ring, and a staff. Affy questions the power of the budplug…and Fungus tells the group that it is very powerful - it hums with power. Daggers have gems (amber colored stone and citrine - 50-150gp each), staff has an emerald (25-75gp). The ring is magical, but no one can identify it (pearl was lost by Fungus and Feywoven Glyphic cube). Plate has sparkles of reinforcement on it - not magical (it is an adamantine plate). Affy will need to take it to a smith to have it fitted.

Kaizo questions is this what “coming alive means” - and Fungus says this is because they imbued the budplug. Affy recalls Lord Selbrenan was the name mentioned…the one who wants the budplug. Fungus realizes that the Feywoven Glyphic cube and budplug are associated with the fey…and the sprite came out, also fey. He just doesn’t understand seelie vs. unseelie. He knows he’s been given power, but will have to pay off his debt at some point with his patron.

We start to clear out and the Realmwarden run in (Ridley hides), asking what is going on. Affy says all is well here. One looks at her (she’s not sure why)...and they investigate the area, but the group cleaned it up. Affy talks to him as they are leaving…he says that people would like to meet me. He says he needs to talk to others before he follows up with me…says that some witnesses said it looked like we were fending off creatures from the stars. Affy gets his name Aziar. He says he will find her at the Pale Lantern.

We go back to the Lantern and sleep. Get a rest. Ridley sleeps with the ring (+1 ring of protection).

37th of Talil

Next day we wake up to try to sell the daggers. We get 100gp and a 100gp pearl in exchange for the 2 daggers. We sell the staff for 50gp. We go to the smith - to get the armor fitted to Affy for 100gp. Kaizo, Relys and Ridley each get 16gp.

On the way to meet him, Ridley spins the cube…Affy and Fungus are close (Affy puts her stuff to the side, as does Ridley). Ridley rolls 32 [RICHES]...and he and Affy add 2x money, Fungus gets 3x.

Affy mentions going through Ham…and Kaizo says or through the herbalist.

We retire to the Pale Lantern in the evening (after chilling all during the afternoon). The door opens and Cerul appears…

– The End of the Session –

To the Abbey

In-World37th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Pale Lantern, Qal’dynn PlayedSeptember 18, 2025

We are all in the Pale Lantern, eating, listening to a bard. We see Cerul. He comes over. “Did you catch her?” Yes - and I have some news. I sent her to O’naren. A messenger from O'naren had news for me. He said the Abbey has been overrun. One of the giants has been turned to stone. Cerul suspects that the Rel’Udomina might be behind it. 39th of Talil

40th of Talil

We get up and leave the next day - headed to the Abbey. Whispers are gone from Fungus and Affy. Ridley hunts and finds a boar…and gets 30 days worth of rations (5 rations for all 6 of us - we will need to smoke them at night). We know this will be a 4 day trip over hills.

On Day 1, Fungus wants to roll the cube…and Affy and Ridley (dressing his pig) are close (she leaves her packs elsewhere). He rolls [77 -FEY STEP]. Threads of light spiral and stutter…light flickers like dying candles. Fungus can move through the ethereal plane…this is an instant misty step with each move. A shape forms through the ripple and Taniana steps in. We all see her. She tilts her head, and a mischievous smile appears on her sweet face …

"The master felt your borrowed spark,

And sent me dancing through the dark."

She flutters lightly around you:

"Twice in sun and once in shade —

Such hunger makes the fabric fray.

Let once per stretch be your creed,

Lest you starve the Feywoven feed."

She shifts to your other side, looks up with sudden seriousness, voice softening:

"The charm you wield, a root now grows.

A bond, a knot — the patron knows.

When Nell does call, don’t look behind —

For not all debts are of the mind."

Fungus tells the group what she said. Nell is in the shadow because someone in the fey is against her and thus, against Fungus. Nell knows that Fungus has the cube and is abusing it…and only use it once per stretch.

We continue on…until dark. Set up camp. Set up the meat to smoke - Ridley does this. Kaizo sets up watch. Ridley sets up alarm spell. Night is uneventful.

41st of Talil

Day 1/8 of rest for the cube

We see a figure limping on the road as we pack up and head out. It is bloodied elf…who says his friends were killed. They had been at the Abbey. We run up. Cerul tells him to sit…and he casts Cure Wounds. I’m Ruvian…thank you. Don’t go that way - you are going to die. Really - we were going to the Abbey. I had heard there was trouble, but the boulder just crushed the horse and my friends. I got hit with a big log…I was crushed and left for dead. I didn’t see much. I wasn’t at the Abbey….we didn’t make it there. Ridley thinks this is truthful. He said they were going to the Abbey to visit. He wasn’t told how dangerous it was. Fungus fixes him rations and stuff to take. He warns us to turn around. He recounts the tale more specifically. Kaizo tells him to ask for Meladra when he gets to O’naren. Fungus adds - to ask for peace, harmony, and transcendence. Affy asks who they worship (Fralee and Layeth). We proceed forward. Travel until night. Camp is the same. Smoke more meat. Fungus conjures a fey wild spirit. Ridley sets alarm. Night is quiet. No issues.

42nd of Talil

Day 2/8 of rest for the Feywoven Glyphic

We head out. Kaizo talks about how it was too quiet on watch and we agree to have more on watch tonight. We start to notice that the trail narrows. We see trees snapped, shattered remains of wagons, gear buried under boulders. Relys thinks the boulders were tossed. As we get close to twilight after a day of travel, we see boot prints, lots of gear…and we notice something carved into the rocks. Cerul says it is says “Turn Back, Not Welcome” in giant. We look for an area in the trees where we can make camp. Cerul decides to fly up and scout for a minute…to see what he sees. He sees a couple of clearings that look like they might be good. He also sees where a body has been ripped apart - not indicative of a giant, but some other predator. He tells us there is something out here besides giants. We go to the clearing further up. We decide to not have a campfire. Affy/Ridley on watch 1; Fungus and Kaizo on watch 3; Cerul and Relys on watch 2.

Watch 1 - we hear sounds in the distance that sound like thunder. Ridley wants to scout about (29 stealth; casts darkvision). He heads to the right. Affy actively watches from the campsite. Ridley travels a little ways - sees a giant walking in the distance (sees in silhouette about 30 minutes from camp). He then heads back to camp. They finish the watch.

Watch 2 - Wake up Relys and Cerul and tell them about the giant. Affy and Ridley go to bed. The two stand watch at camp, but don’t leave. They wake up Fungus and Kaizo.

Watch 3 - The two stand guard over the camp. Hear rumbling, but stay put. Dawn comes.

43rd of Talil

Day 3/8 of rest for the Feywoven Glyphic

The group packs up and heads out. As we travel, we see dead bodies and evidence of the danger on the trail. We continue further, growing more uneasy. We round a bend and the sounds of nature are gone. Towering before us as we round a bend, we see a stone giant - not moving - he has been stoned. This is Cerul’s friend (Brundhalk). Before Cerul can cast a spell and use the scroll, the ground rumbles and a stony boulder turns into a monster, a tendril emerges (another creature) and two birds dive. We enter combat – IT IS INITIATIVE.

– The End of the Session –

Abbey Arrival

In-World43rd of Talil, 1246 WherePath to the Abbey of Light PlayedOctober 2, 2025

Combat on the Path

Session 16 ended with an ambush.

Session 17 starts with combat … Initiative - as 2 tendrils from a very large being rise up from a stone, 2 giant birds with colorful wings, and a large stone figure emerge (looks like an earth elemental).

  • ROUND ONE: Ridley takes aim at the tendril creature and fumbles and then misses, as he realizes that this thing is hard as a rock. That creature lashes out at Ridley and hits, grappling him, then misses with a bite. Then attacks Kaizo and pulls him in and bites him. One bird flies in to attack Fungus, picks him up, and misses; then tries to claw him and misses. The other bird tries to grab Relys and misses and tries to claw her and fumbles. Kaizo tries to attack the tendril creature and misses twice, then tries to kick him and misses. The stony creature steps back and then rolls in boulder form to Cerul and misses. Fungus blinks and turns large and interacts with the bird saying “fly away, fly away, fly away” - to see if it understands him. Cerul sprouts wings and casts spirit guardian in 15’ ring (on bird and stony creature). Affy turns and attacks the bird on Fungus, hitting once and missing once. Relys shifts into a bear and attacks the bird on her and hits twice. She realizes that these creatures are in league with whatever is protecting the area…maybe summoned by the stone giants.
  • ROUND TWO: Ridley tries to attack the creature holding him and hits a tendril. The tendril shatters…and Ridley is released, as the creature is pushed 10’ away. He then hits again, pushing it another 10’ away (dragging Kaizo with it). Ridley then backs up. That creature then attacks Kaizo. A tendril then regrows. It then reaches for Relys and pulls her close and tries to bite her and misses - but she is grappled and has the poisoned condition. It then tries to grab Fungus and does - grappling him and giving him the poisoned condition and pulls him 30’ closer. One bird tries to grab Cerul and hits and then flies up 30’ and drops Cerul (but he flies and is ok). The other bird attacks Affy and fumbles and then misses. Kaizo attacks and misses twice, but hits with his unarmed strike on the tendril holding him. The stony creature animates a stone and an earth elemental emerges and it rolls towards Affy and hits her, knocking her prone. Fungus misty steps away and tries to poison spray the bird and misses, and blinks away. Affy attacks the bird and hits, missing the stony creature. She hits twice. Cerul attacks with his spell and tries to hit 3 creatures and does - some taking full, some taking half damage. He then uses a weapon to attack the stony creature on Affy and hits. Relys swipes at the creature grappling her and misses, then hits. She lets the group know these are creatures of nature (tells the group what she learned).
  • ROUND THREE: Ridley shoots the bird that is flying and hits twice, killing it - then moves, trying to get out of range of the tendril creature. That creature then tries to grab Cerul from the air and misses. He then tries to bite Kaizo and misses; and tries to bite Relys and misses. The bird flies to get Cerul (Affy misses the opportunity attacks) and hits him, grappling him, and tries to bite him and misses. Kaizo attacks and misses twice, fumbling, then misses again. The initial stony guy rolls toward Affy and misses. The other stony guy rolls into the bear, hitting it, knocking it prone. Fungus pops back in and casts acid spray on the bird fighting Cerul, hitting critically, killing it. He then misty steps and heals Affy and blinks away. Affy attacks the stony creature on her and hits twice, then yells “Affy” loudly to the group. Cerul flies over toward the tendril creature and other stony creature, imposing his spiritual aura, damaging them. Then attacks the tendril guy and hits. Relys casts moonbeam on the tendril creature and hits.
  • ROUND FOUR: Ridley attacks the tendril creature and kills it, putting a bolt into its eye. Then tries to hit the stony creature and misses. Kaizo attacks the stony creature on Affy and hits, then misses, then misses with flurry of blows and then hits with second flurry of blows. One of the stony creatures moves toward Kaizo (Relys misses opportunity attack). It hits Kaizo, but he deflects the damage. The other stony guy then attacks Affy and hits. Fungus pops back in attacks one with a spell, and misses. Then he misty steps and pops away. Affy attacks the one on her and hits once. Cerul flies over and moves his spirit guardian aura to the final two stony creatures who do not save, both taking damage. Cerul then attacks the second stony creature and misses. Relys stands up and moves the moonbeam to attack both of the other stony creatures who save, but still take damage.
  • ROUND FIVE: Ridley shoots one of the creatures and hits twice, good damage. Kaizo attacks the creature in front of Affy, killing it, shattering the rock. Then attacks the last creature, hitting it, then hitting again with flurry of blows. That creature attacks Cerul and hits him, and the spirit guardian drops. He then moves toward Affy (and Kaizo misses his opportunity attack). Affy then moves up and hits the last guy twice, including some additional force damage. Cerul tries to attack and misses. Relys moves the moonbeam over the creature and saves, but takes damage.

Affy apologizes to Relys that we had to fight the creatures of nature. Cerul heals himself. Kaizo heals Affy. Ridley shifts his hunter’s mark to the stone statue to learn of his “conditions.” We discuss making sure that other creatures of nature might come…so let’s try to free the stone giant.

Helping Brundhalk

Cerul tries to use the scroll of Greater Restoration on the petrified giant, Brundhalk. Cerul is going to make sure that he gives himself the best chance to free him. He casts enhance ability on himself. Affy prays to Phoe. Fungus gives him a back rub. Fungus stands next to Cerul - he can speak giant and help calm emotions. Cerul tries to use the scroll, but the magic doesn’t work - and the scroll dissolves into ash. Brundhalk remains petrified!

Looking for Another Way

The group decides to proceed toward the Abbey and try and find a way in. Cerul studies the map to see if there is an alternate way to enter. He finds something that he didn’t know from when he lived there - an undercroft - though it isn’t clear where that is. Cerul thinks about all of the things he knows about the Abbey - clerics, priests, and monks - but thinks that stone giants are guarding the place. With a history check, he knows there are three clans that exist in the Cradle Crest Rise. Two have strong relationships with humankind: Watchers of the Abbey and Watchers of the Bastion. Kharos clan (Abbey)....they have a stone speaker who works for a high carver. For this clan it is Ada, who is a matronly figure (and Cerul’s adopted mother). The third clan - Highridge Dreamers - live high up in the mountains and he doesn’t know much about them. Cerul thinks the giant that we saw from the campsite was probably one from the Kharos clan. He thinks they might not be accommodating if we cross them, but thinks it isn’t hopeless. Helping save the one who was petrified would have helped, but maybe he can convince them he at least tried.

Stone Giant Guardians

The group proceeds forward slowly on the forest line, trying to be quiet. After a couple of hours, we stop and allow Kaizo to take the lead and scout ahead. At a ridge, we can see the Abbey of Light in the distance. There is a large building with a large bell tower, lots of other stone buildings. Kaizo sees two stone giants in the area on the road to the Abbey - talking to each other, keeping watch (they look bored). He points this out to all of us. Relys thinks the Rel’Udomina are in the Abbey…and they have been able to disable the arcane waypoint (or something has done this). Lorian told the group that people can’t get in…so we know something is wrong inside the Abbey. Relys thinks the giants are reacting to their friend being petrified - probably on defense until they can figure out what happened.

We discuss having Cerul talk to the giants to persuade them to let us in to fix things - telling him that he was a friend of Brundaulk and tried to save him.

Cerul knows there is a free tavern/inn for travelers who go back and forth to the Bastion…in the Abbey grounds. There aren’t many other resources in the Abbey…it isn’t a town. But there are probably 400-500 people who live inside the walls. Fungus notices that there is farmland in the valley area, but not inside the walls.

Giant Protectors - Gidfar and Advar

The forest line is 500-600’ from where the giants are. Ridley, Kaizo, Relys, and Affy stay in the woodline. Fungus and Cerul walk down the path, singing giant songs and speaking in giant. The giants take notice and flex and wait for them to get close. Within 75’ away, they hear “none will pass.” Cerul says “we come in peace.” They say that “none with pass while Brundhaulk sleeps.” Cerul explains that he tried to save him with the scroll, and that he knows Brundhaulk. These two giants are Gidfar (who Cerul recognizes) and Advar and he mentions the others he knows and is able to convince Gidfar that he is the little boy that he knew. He says “you are Ada’s boy” and Cerul says yes. And then introduces Fungus, who speaks in giant. Cerul tells them they have friends who are hiding in the hills who are there to help as well. Cerul says that he might be able to help Brundhaulk if they let him and his friends into the Abbey. Cerul and Fungus tell them about the Rel’Udomina, and tell them that they are the ones who did this to him with magic. They ask how many friends. And they agree to let the group enter. “Advar - what do you think?” He says “why don’t you ask Ada?” And he says that is a good idea. They ask if he has anything to show…and he pulls out a carved wooden box with a polished stone…and says to show the box and stone to Ada. She should know the box for sure. He says he will take this to Ada and find us if she does not recognize it.

He says, let’s go get your friend - and puts out a hand to take them - but Cerul waves us to come forward…so the group advances to the giants. None of the rest of the group understand “giant” so it sounds like gibberish. They tell Fungus and Cerul - no one else can come or go until their friend is fixed. They lead the group to the Abbey entrance.

Everything feels normal at first…as the group gets to the front gate. Cerul mentions three areas: the orphanage - which is a large wing of the Abbey where children play. He remembers the transition of the Abbey of Mont Rest to the Abbey of Light. He knows of the Rising Hearthlight (the tavern/inn). He also tells the group of the Order of the Lighted Breath. This was a group who was into meditation, stepping into other areas of enlightenment. Cerul thinks Kaizo would like to be here. He remembers Brother Freya was a part of this group.

Four Orders: Monks of Lighted Breath, Nurturing Light (children, Matron Elbrade Mallow), Reflective Light (scholars, diplomats; led by ??); Protective Light (guardian order; led by Warden); Rising Light (religious group - transition of all pantheons to one Order of Light - led by Luminary). Cerul studies the maps and thinks the Rel’Udomina is probably hanging out below ground, but we don’t know what all they have done here.

At the Rising Hearthlight

The group heads to the Hearthlight. It feels like a dorm. The bottom is the dining hall area and there are stairs that lead to large communal rooms for sleeping. No one is there to greet the group formally. Cerul knows that they used to serve 3 meals a day. Today they are preparing vegetarian broth w/egg and onion. When we walk in the hearth catches the eye, and we see a board there with a parchment. We notice some people putting food at places on tables…setting it out for people to come and sit and eat, Ridley and Relys walk up to inspect the parchment on the board. Most of the requests are old and deal with trades, etc. But one notice catches the eye (which is probably 3-4 stretches old)

– NOTICE TO TRAVELERS AND PILGRIMS –

A cave entrance has been discovered approximately one mile west of the Abbey, beyond the old shepherd’s path that skirts the ridge.

Those who’ve entered have not returned.

The Protective Light advises against casual exploration. Entry is not sanctioned and currently considered a risk to public safety.

If you possess experience in navigating magically warded ruins or suppressed anomalies, report to Warden Valegar for clearance.

Let wisdom guide your steps, and silence guard your tongue.

— By Order of the Rising Light

Cerul walks up to talk to someone who is putting food out. He does not recognize them. He asks about Brother Freya - do you know where he is? He says that Cerul needs to ask someone else. She offers to bring someone older out to help him. Cerul thinks the person is familiar, but doesn’t know their name. They converse. The man tells Cerul that he might want to ask the Matron. He recognizes Cerul. He thinks that she will know where Brother Freya went…he says he left. Cerul asks about accommodations and he points to some rooms. Then asks how we got in and Cerul explains. He tells him about how one of them was turned to stone (how ironic…not funny at all). Cerul says he is friends with the stone giants. Affy asks if he knows anyone with the Rel’Udomina tattoo (shows him her arm). He is tight lipped.

The man offers the group some broth and we all decide to eat. As we are sitting there, a lady nudges in and sits with the group, making herself at home. “You don’t know me, right?” I have a sense about you. I’m Sister Elleran of the Reflective Light (speaking quietly). She overheard us talking about how we got inside. She says she can’t remain still…even though everyone else is playing along. She says there are things going on underneath the Abbey. We think she would know about this stuff. She says something is going on. Strangers have been down under the Abbey and we have been warned that if we speak, the children will suffer. The others are all scared - and have been bound to silence. That’s why I came over - it begins with you. And she picks up the bowl and walks away.

So we decide to open the door and go through to look for a hidden door at the dead end. Affy finds a contraption to open the secret door and she opens it. There are many treasures here - probably gifts to the Abbey that are stored here. Fungus tries to palm something but everyone sees him and convinces him to put the angelic statues back.

  • Fungus Update – Affy asks if anyone else got anything new and Fungus reveals his gills. He says he got them from using the Feywoven Glyphic 4 days ago.   • Relys Update – Relys studied with Pivric trying to figure out why her village Iparos was destroyed. Her studies revealed that there are things that can go wrong with the ritual called the Weaving Night. When these things go wrong, they can open up a rift into the Shadowfell. Her suspicions believe that may be what occurred, but she needs to return to that area of Varenwood to see if there are any distant traces of Conjuration magic used. Relys also believes that she may be able to perform the ritual properly if she has some Nightbud and another component, the fungus known as Moonpetal.
  • Ridley Update – Ridley says he was trying to find out more about Rueh’unar Heildove, someone that he is on the hunt for that he feels is a bad person. Ridley believed that Rueh’unar was involved in criminal activity, but found new information that he may not be a criminal like he was led to initially believe. Ridley is not questioning his mission, because Ridley knows that Rueh’unar is a wizard who curses others. Ridley also mentions that Kaizo was restless…and didn’t sleep well. Ridley stated that Kaizo seemed out of sorts and never visited his spa.
  • Affy Update – Affy shares that she did research on her father and his tie with the Advents of Harmony in the libraries in Qal’dynn, but didn’t have luck. Affy decided to search around the city to get additional information on her dad. While in the city, Affy observed her companions, noticing Ridley talking to people and Relys with Pivric. Affy ended up going through Arcane Waypoint to the Argent Halls, which is now on an island floating in the air. Affy didn’t reveal to the people there, other than to help out to earn trust. A lady at the Argent Halls asked her to research the Feywoven Glypic, which Affy found ironic. Affy didn’t reveal to the people of the Argent Halls that Affy knows about the cube, but researches the item in the Argent Halls archives. Affy learns that the Feywoven Glypic is very powerful, tied to the Feywild, and wanted by an archfey lord named Selbranan. The lady Affy was helping ends up mentioning Affy’s father, in which Affy reveals that Affy is looking for him. The lady offers to contact Affy’s father and let him know that Affy is looking for him. Affy then tells Fungus how powerful and dangerous the Feywoven Glyphic is and the group discusses that he needs to keep it a secret since it could attract those who attacked the party in the Ring of Sylunara in Qal’dynn; as well as others who would want to use the power.

Protective Light Visitor

After catching up, the door of the establishment opens and a familiar halfling steps in–it is Therin Hillabuck. Therin pulls down the note from the board and brings it over to the party. The note is the posting about the danger of the nearby cave. Therein says Warden Valegar has already granted the party clearance to go to the cave, so the party is free to go. Therin explains the route to get there - about a mile out from the Abbey (small cave on the side of a mountain). Therrin saw bodies, bones–old and new–and he didn’t go further. Affy asks about rumors and Therrin says there are none since no one who has been there has come back.

To the Cave

The group leaves and starts to follow the trail for the cave, gathering or purchasing cold weather gear since it’s now winter and the abbey on Cradlecrest Rise is high up. After about 30 minutes of travel, the group sees the opening of a cave. There are some of the bodies and bones that were described at the mouth of the cave. Ridley spots nearby mountain sabers (large catlike creatures) and asks for everyone to be quiet so the group isn’t noticed. Some of the mountain sabers are eating and others that look to have just eaten.

Ridley casts Pass Without Trace, so that the party is more stealthy and can attempt to sneak past while Relys creates a distraction with Druidcraft. The party hugs the left crag wall on the way to the cave, the plan works and the party makes it into the cave.

Inside the Cave

Inside the cave is a long winding tunnel, with light filtering in for about 40 feet. The party has to dodge bones on the tunnel floor as they continue to be stealthy. Once inside, Cerul activates Lightbringer and casts Light on Relys’ staff. Ridley questions why the party is there and they mention the post which says there may be magic relics. As the tunnel is lit, Ridley notices a ring on a corpse nearby—iIt is a Band of Dunduar–which he decides to keep for now. Ridley also notices that it looks like some of the bodies were killed by others, while some killed themselves - perhaps cursed.

Fungus steps around the light to look at the cave. He sees it goes down about 30’ and bends. He sees that they are other dead bodies along the path. Some of the corpses are long dead. The party continues down the path. Relys sends her familiar, the spider Hopper, ahead and Relys jumps on Fungus’ back to carry her so she can see through Hopper’s eyes. As the group proceeds, they notice a corpse move–something that Hopper didn’t trigger. Hopper notices daylight at the end of the tunnel while scouting ahead, but Relys opens her eyes to deal with the moving corpse.

It’s Initiative – Combat vs. the Dead

  • Round One: Relys casts a spell (Starry Wisp) and misses. Cerul casts a spell (Sacred Flame) and hits. Ridley climbs the walls and comes down on the other side of the zombie and attacks it and hits. Affy hits once with her greataxe. Fungus hits it with Poison Spray, but it bubbles off and doesn’t damage him. The zombie attacks Ridley and hits him twice, but Affy reduces some of the damage a little bit.
  • Round Two: Relys casts the spell again and hits. Cerul casts the spell again and hits. Ridley tries to hit the zombie with his shortsword and hits, then hits again, then with a dagger and hits again. Affy takes his head off with her axe, but the creature is still moving so she hits it again and then stomps on its head. Fungus also hits it. It misses both Affy and Ridley.
  • Round Three: Relys misses with her spell, but Cerul casts a spell and takes it down forever.

After the fight, Cerul heals Ridley. There is nothing of value on the dead body.

The Ravine and a Blocked Path

The party moves forward and notices that the tunnel slopes upward and opens to the outside, to a large ravine. Hopper continues forward and sees that the cliff walls from the ravine scale up 30-50’ in height. The path for the group is blocked by boulders and rocks, from which appendages are seen beneath. This rubble pile is about 10’ high. The party doesn’t see any evidence that this was a landslide - perhaps it is the work of giants?

The party decides to climb up. As Fungus jumps ahead and climbs, the pile shifts a little as everyone hears bones break. Ridley notices some gloves on one of the dead - making him think this is a recent death. Each glove has a hidden pocket, where Ridley finds thieves’ tools in one and 2 platinum pieces in the other. Ridley gives the money to Cerul for safe keeping and keeps the gloves and tools. Affy asks Fungus what he sees from the top of the pile. He sees a giant coming around the bend.

It’s Initiative – Combat vs. the Zombie Giant

  • Round One: Fungus casts doomtide - which makes a sphere of inky, black fog appear that will move toward the giant and cause damage. He yells to us to not enter the darkness. The giant moves up, but can’t see Fungus (and sounds like a zombie), then turns to walk away. Fungus shouts “big fella over here” in giant and sees him turn around to face the group again. Relys casts wild shape and becomes a giant spider and climbs up the 50’ cliff. Cerul readies sacred flame and climbs up the pile. Affy climbs up the pile and pulls out her greatsword. Ridley dashes and climbs up to the top of the cliff and kneels for cover after he spots the giant and vice versa.
  • Round Two: the sphere moves forward toward the giant and Fungus casts a spell at the giant and hits (sorcerer’s burst). The giant blindly throws a boulder at Fungus and hits, as Fungus shouts “come and get me.” The giant moves into the darkness and takes damage. Relys moves up to the top of the cliff and moves forward and casts a spell and hits him - since she can now see him from the top of the cliff. Cerul moves down to Fungus and helps him up. Affy “flies” up to the top of the cliff and throws her trident at him and knocks him prone and tells the others he is prone. Ridley stealths.
  • Round Three: the sphere moves forward a little. Fungus uses the same spell to hit him and damages him. The giant stands up and moves forward and tries to find a boulder to throw at me and can’t. He spews toxic waste at the group and misses. He then takes more damage from the fog. Relys casts moonbeam on the giant and he takes damage. Cerul blindly casts a firebolt in the giant’s direction and hits. Affy shoots her bow and misses, then crits, slowing him. Ridley shoots him and hits twice.
  • Round Four: the sphere moves forward. Fungus moves to the edge of the blackness and uses the same spell and hits, killing him with lightning in the face. He then drops the spell and the blackness goes away.

After combat, Relys drops the moonbeam. Affy drops down to pick up her trident, one arrow and one of Ridley’s bolts. Fungus sits down and meditates for a minute. Cerul checks out the body and sees it was a stone giant.

The Black Structure

The party moves forward and snow falls a little heavier. The ravine opens up and reveals a black expanse of black stone walls and towers that look like they have burst up from the ground. There seems to be a fortress/temple that is made of black onyx stone or something - stone that seems to absorb the light. There is an archway carved into the wall, made with precise masonry work. There are geometric patterns carved on the stones. The party notices double iron doors in the archway inset 5’. There are words above the archway–in runic fashion–that the party can’t read from this distance. Noticing no immediate threats, the party heads up to the archway.

Affy and Ridley think the runes are an ancient use of the common tongue and it says “Oath to the Great One, Torec.” The party knows that Torec was the old, elemental god. The temple could have been here a long time, but might have been revealed with the Awakening. Ridley tries to check the doors and doesn’t see anything. Relys asks Hopper to check the keyhole and Hopper can’t get through, so he crawls back out. Fungus thinks there is more to these doors than meets the eye–there is magic here–and that magic means might help. Affy doesn’t see any other entrance. Ridley notices no traps on the door. Fungus tries to pick the lock and it won’t budge. Affy thinks maybe trying something elemental might help. Fungus tries a sorcerer’s burst with cold damage to the door and the door absorbs it…and the door starts to crack and there is sound of iron against stone as the doors open. As the doors part, the party sees stone tiles ahead covered with geometric patterns. Inside is a huge entry wall with towering pillars. It is dark inside, and the ceiling is high.

– The End of the Session –

The Blackstone Crucible

In-World5th of Paramor, 1246 WhereStrange Black Structure PlayedFebruary 5, 2026

Day 4/8 of rest for the Feywoven Glypic

The Steps of the Strange Structure

We think maybe this was exposed with the Awakening, but are not sure.

The Hall of Convergence

Fungus looks inside. There are 4 pillars. Can’t tell how tall the ceiling is - it looks to be 40’ tall, but can’t actually see a ceiling, it is just dark. The sides are black stone walls and there are a pair of doors on each side and a set of double doors at the end of the room. There is something etched into the floor about halfway across. The place seems long abandoned. Fungus passes gas.

Cerul looks around and it has an ancient feel, but doesn’t seem to be elemental - like it had an overhaul or remodel at some point. Cerul thinks it probably dates back to the old gods, but is not someone he is familiar with. He knows old gods existed and new gods have risen and there is a divine energy that exists - so it could be an even older god. It doesn’t seem to be a god of light, but maybe of distorted or bent light - not evil, but nothing enlightening.

We step in and the floor has repeating geometric shapes and somewhat up the walls. At the center of the room there is one tile that turns and we hear the sound of chains. It looks like a broken shard in the middle of the tile. Fungus moves over to a pillar - it is octagonal and rises up. As Affy and Relys approach the symbol it starts to glow. The shard almost looks like it is moving. The two of the them hear “all who pass are judged.” Fungus walks over to one of the side doors and listens and doesn’t hear anything at first - then he thinks he hears a low hum/sustained energy. Ridley goes to another door and hears nothing. Relys goes to the far doors and hears “none who pass are unmarked.” Affy checks another door and hears nothing and goes back to the tile. She gets within 5’ of it and the entire group hears: “We are the keepers that bind and this is the Blackstone Crucible.” Fungus decides to open the door where he is. Affy and Cerul move over to the door next to him, as Ridley casts darkvision on himself and goes into stealth (Rolls a 39 for Stealth).

Binding Workshop

Fungus throws the door open and we see racks and shelves, manacles, rings, black dust clinging to things. No one is present. There is a hum in the air for those who step in. Fungus asks Cerul to hide his light. This room is large and rectangular with doors on the right and stairs at the far end. Fungus and Affy step in. This seems like a ceremonial dungeon. Affy hears nothing at the first door. Ridley comes out of stealth to join us and then goes into hiding again and goes to the stairs to check them out. Relys casts Detect Magic and the manacles and collars have a magical aura (abjuration). As Fungus touches manacles, he feels a pulse run through him - slight energy. Affy opens the first door on the right and she sees a square chamber with black stone walls, a low stone platform is the in the room with iron rings set into the floor. The raised platform has sigils and recesses where they are. Relys sees that the iron restraints are magical, too.

Ridley goes upstairs through a black stone shaft (the tower staircase), but he also sees they go down, too. There is no rail going up or down the stairs - you could fall off the edge. Stairs are well worn. As he moves, he looks for secret passages. At the top it dead ends into a door. He hears wind, but not at the door, but more on the sides of him. He pushes the door open and he sees a hallway - and thinks it goes to the center of the structure. The hallway goes 25’ and then goes right and left (but not at a T - more diagonally). He moves forward down the hall.

Fungus goes to the next door on the right and opens it. He sees a long hallway that disappears into his darkvision and goes straight. He sees many doors on the left and one door on the right. Fungus hears “one, two, three, ah forgive me…the echo distorted the count” and he looks and sees a small creature that looks impish, but isn’t. He looks at Fungus, shakes his head, and disappears.

Ridley gets to the fork and looks to the right and sees a hallway that goes as far as he can see. On the left he just beyond 20’ there is a door on the left. He moves up to that door and listens. He hears snow hitting the wall outside.

Communal Dormitories

Fungus walks to where he saw the imp. Relys and Affy move into the room with the platform. Looks like the recesses were used for melted metal (silver, steel)...can’t make out the sigils/runes. Relys thinks the symbols are created in patterns for strength, to lift things up (lattice-like). They open the door and see Fungus sniffing the air. He opens the door on the left. There is a chamber with black stone walls and stone stabs and bed frames, but no mattresses. On the stone bed there is a book. Its surface is naked with creases as if it has been handled for many years. It has leather and is made of 8 interlocking mantacles that form a perfect circle…and a dark obsidian shard with the word “N’dyr.” This is journal-like. Thin, but resilient pages, written in common. He sees a wooden footchest with iron bands. Fungus reaches to open the chest and a dart flies out of it and he takes poison damage. Inside he sees several small folded black robes and a small lockbox. He finds a key and opens the lockbox. Inside is a single amethyst. With a note “if this fails, we endure.” Fungus isn’t sure how much the gem is worth, but Cerul thinks it is worth 20gp.

Affy walks to the next door and opens it. It looks to be the same as the last room, including the book. She calls Fungus over to check out the chest. He sees a hole and Affy covers it, and Fungus is finally able to open it. There are more robes, a healer’s kit (Relys), a key with geometric markings, and a potion of healing (Fungus).

Cerul and Relys study the two books we have found so far. They realize this is not a story or spell book, this writing is very purposeful. Passages have numbers, parables. There are themes that stand out. Hymns and repetitive sayings. This seems like a godly book. “Some evils can not be destroyed. Destruction is not mercy. Binding is not cruelty, but responsibility.” Text speaks of containment and of those who choose to bind rather than kill. Indoctrination and persuasion. You should understand that these bindings are all purposeful. Trying to figure out what the binding is about. They study a bit longer and realize this will take a long time to totally figure out. The books are all the same (like a bible). Books are not evil or cruel according to the text. References to someone known or spoken of Malgri’n. This seems to be the leader or champion or prophet of this message.

As they study, Affy and Fungus go to the next couple of rooms, which seem to be copies of the other rooms. In these four other rooms, these are all still in the dormitory: the first chest has a couple of robes, flask of body oil vial (QUOTING THE DM, “diddy oil”), and the inside chest says “We bind, We bind, We bind”. Fungus opens chest two: a little book (names that are hard to read and scratched out, binding failures, a belief structure (instead of an eye for an eye, you do something wrong I kill you, it is just locking him up), a scroll (detect magic), a set of fine writing tools (Cerul). The half-big boy took a poison dart straight to the face, disfiguring him as the venom seeps inside fully, his big boy nature tanking the hit and keeping him standing, and inside a potion of poison resistance is inside (Affy) and an obsidian charm. Last chest: big boy failed, he took the dart to the face again… inside there is a small pouch with 12 silver pieces, a silver signet ring with the symbol of this place, and a folded scrap of parchment with a routine written down (wake up, brush teeth….discipline).

The half-big boy continues down the hall and it looks like the floor drops off and it angles to the left and there is a sheer wall of water. The water isn't moving (not frozen), more like a mirror.

Chamber of the Architect

Meanwhile, Ridley, upstairs, opens the door where he is at. He sees the walls curve up from the floor and ceiling. He thinks this looks like a chamber of someone important (maybe the architect).. Sees geometric patterns everywhere. He sees a stone slab desk and best and fixed seat. The desk has shelves and a stone armoire built into the walls, closet-like. He sees a scroll sitting in one of the pockets on a shelf and hears a loud “pop” behind him, but sees nothing. He grabs the scroll and opens it up, but can’t read it/understand it, so he pockets it. On the desk, he finds a book, like what we found in the rooms below.

Chamber of the Custodean

Ridley continues down the hallway on the left side and he sees another door and listens at it and hears nothing. He opens the door. This is a nice room as well, but maybe belongs to the custodian or person who tends to the place. Similar layout to the other room he was in, except the desk is thicker and there is a geometric rune that is not attached to any others that sits in the middle of the room. He checks the desk for a hidden compartment and thinks it might have something to do with that rune. He touches the rune and fails a wisdom saving throw and he feels necrotic energy shoot through him and hears a thud beneath the desk, as a box falls out of the desk. He opens up the box and sees strange writing utensils: skull, frog, fish, that are holding bottles of pigment ink. He pockets the box. He continues on.

Meanwhile, the rest of the group goes to the “water/mirror” wall to check it out. Affy steps forward first and sees off to the left stairs rising up, but to the right, sees a large floating platform that seems to be attached to the doors in the main pillar room. The outside circle falls into emptiness and above is a dome. Our view shows the walls, but think if we were inside it would look like we were looking at the night sky. There is a 15’ gap between us and the dome. There is a platform on top of the dome - likely from the stairs. In the center of the domed room is a symbol that is embedded into the floor, but it is an actual piece of black obsidian - not a carved symbol. We decide to climb the stairs.

Council Chamber

Ridley is on the top floor and meets the group on the top floor. Just as we meet, we see the area open up into a vast rectangular area with a door that leads to the platform. There is a stone dais - like a counselor's chambers - with 4 large chairs. There are 4 large insectoid/demon-like creatures….and

IT IS INITIATIVE: Combat vs. Mezzoloths

  • Round One: Ridley moves up and attacks one with a short sword and hits. He hits again and then hits again with a hand crossbow, and a final hit with sneak attack. Affy dashes forward as a bonus action and attacks another one with her greataxe and misses twice. The one on RIdley casts a spell and darkness envelops Ridley. And then he moves back from the darkness and throws his trident at Affy and misses. Fungus runs into the darkness and moves toward another one he sees. He hexes the creature as a bonus action and then sprays it with poison, using lucky and hits - (poison and necrotic) but he realizes that the poison didn’t do much damage and knocks it prone. It then stands up and casts a spell (cloudkill). Ridley and Fungus have to make saving throws or take poison damage. This area is heavily obscured. Cerul tries to dispel the cloud, but can’t so he stays put. Relys casts a spell which makes her body light up and then moves through the cloud, taking poison damage then turns into a bear. The last insect creature attacks Affy and grapples her with his claw then tries to hit her with a trident and does, but she mitigates some of the force damage with her psionic powers, but is restrained.
  • Round Two: Ridley moves up and hunters mark the insect and fumbles and the foe got to attack him. He then missed the creature with his second attack. He then tries to shoot it and misses. Affy tries to break the restraint and does not. She spits on him. The creature on Ridley misses with the first attack and then crits on Ridley and hurts his left foot (cutting his movement in half). Fungus moves out of the cloud and bursts the one in the corner and uses lucky and hits, doing a little bit of damage. He then casts a spell on himself and moves to the creature. That creature then tries to grab Fungus with a claw and misses, then tries to hit with another claw and hits and grapples him, but he takes cold damage (half) when he does. That damage makes the cloud drop. Fungus takes damage and is restrained. Cerul moves into the darkness and then dashes out so he can take stock of the area. Relys moves up between Affy and Ridley and attacks the one on Ridley as a bear (rends). The last one attacks Relys with a claw and fumbles, then tries again and hits and she is restrained, and she takes slashing damage. She then forces the creature to make a CON save and it does.
  • Round Three: Ridley attacks and misses with the first one then hits with the second attack and takes it down, turning it to ichor. Affy then misses twice (hitting with disadvantage). Fungus tries to misty step and can’t (the power of the blackstone crucible - he is bound). He then tries to burst it and misses. The one on Fungus laughs and attacks with a trident, taking cold damage as he does (but in half) and then attacks again and hits (and Fungus’ armor drops). Cerul runs over to attack the one on Fungus and hits, doing a lot of radiant damage. He takes additional radiant damage failing his CON save. Relys attacks twice and misses twice. The last one casts cloud kill on Affy, Relys, and Ridley. Ridley fails the save and the other two make it, but all take damage.
  • Round Four: Ridley moves out of the cloud. Affy heals herself and then attacks and hits once. Then action surges to attack again and hits once. She takes poison damage at the end of her turn. Fungus attacks the one on him and hits, dealing psychic damage and knocking him prone. He then uses a bonus action to turn large. The creature stands up and tries to grapple Cerul, but misses due to Cerul’s warding flare. He then tries to stab Fungus and hits. Cerul attacks the creature on him and misses. Relys misses with both her attacks. She makes a CON save, but takes poison damage (halved). The last one attacks Relys and misses and then attacks again and hits with a trident.
  • Round Five: Ridley moves over and moves the hunters mark to the one on Fungus and Cerul. He attacks and misses then throws the glowing dagger and hits, killing it. Affy attacks twice and hits once. Then takes poison damage and sheaths her axe. She yells at it. Fungus runs to the edge of the cloud and casts a spell on himself. Cerul can’t see inside the cloud and can’t see anything so he heals Fungus a little bit. Relys fumbles and falls prone. Then attacks again and misses. Then casts some healing on herself as a bonus action. Then takes poison damage. The bad guy then attacks Relys and hits and then attacks Affy and hits.

Cerul tries to dispel the cloud kill spell and does and the cloud disappears. He moves up next to Relys. Relys makes a death save. The bad guy casts darkness and runs away. Affy hits with her opportunity attack. Fungus yells that he is going through the doors.
