Fungus pours a healing potion into Relys’s mouth and she comes back up. We walk around to one of the rooms that Ridley checked out - it is the custodian room. We take a short rest. After this, we hear a popping sound and the little creature appears. He is counting and then stops and says, “I will let Varmuun know.” And pops away.

Affy saw the guy and doesn’t think he is abyssal or infernal. She thinks he is a yugoloth (neutral evil). He seems to be keeping a toll/count of what is going on. It is odd that people from another plane are here.

The Mysterious Gate

The group decides to check the platform. Affy and Fungus step up to open the doors. It opens into a vast hallway…with smooth stone overhead. Eyes twitch in/out focus on depth of stars/non-existence. Strange we can’t see beyond. We see black stone arches that create the dome of the ceiling. As we get to the center of the room, it floats above the stone area below. We can actually look down and see what is below (this area is rail-less). We see an old gate above us with a stone circle around it. The interior of it is shimmering (like a portal). On either side of it, we see massive abacus-like contraptions with heavy stone beads. The railing of the gate is obsidian. Before the gate, we see an obsidian pedestal. We notice the abacus are positioned in specific points and the gate with the shimmering field…there is an image of a forest stretched out. Relys knows that this forest is the Varenwood. She thinks my people created a ritual and thinks that there is something going on with what her ancestors did. She thinks if we step through it, we could go into the Varenwood. Is this real or an illusion? Relys thinks that if the Weaving Night goes wrong, corruption occurs. She thinks the abacus has settings that allow us to dial in coordinates to send us to a certain place. She memorizes this setting to know how to get to the Varenwood. She thinks that the ritual in Varenwood made the Shadowfell travelers come here instead of to the Varenwood (and thinks the travelers from the Varenwood would come here with this setting).

Releasing the Bound

In-World5th of Paramor, 1246 WhereBlackstone Crucible PlayedFebruary 20, 2026

Day 4/8 of rest for Feywoven Glyphic

Kaizo Returns

We stand at the portal, looking at the forest of Varenwood through it. Light seems abnormal. Footsteps approach from behind and we see Kaizo. We ask how he found us - our tracks - and what he’d been doing - thinking. He described how he had been following Ridley and things were off and then meditated. Cerul gives him grief for meditating while we have been in danger. Kaizo asks - insists - that we all sit down for story time.

He does so. Tells us that he’s been having visions about how things have not been in balance - and balance is very important to him. He’s been pulled into two places - an old shrine in Varenwood (a shrine that was meant to keep things balanced, but this shrine was destroyed). Relys had not heard anything about this shrine aka druid circle being destroyed. While this is going on, Ridley notices the fairy Tanana pop into existence and land on Fungus’ shoulder (no one else notices). Kaizo mentions Arisia, he mentions good stuff will give you visions… during this vision he noticed her crying… Cut to fairy, pointing towards the portal, where she says, “Shoddy will be delighted at what you (Fungus) have found” Back to Kaizo, balance, it all needs to be released? Binding is a dam and balance is a river. Tanana tells Fungus “you are near the source - much closer than anyone has been in a long time” –and then she pops away.

We discuss that we need to remove the binding. But where - how? Arisia’s message was that “he has bound me. I am not evil, so I must be…” (and then faded) out.” Binding may be happening here, but also in Varenwood…maybe Shadowfell, too.

Kaizo then tells Ridley that he observed that people watched him, but never approached. That he is being watched - maybe controlled or evaluated? Maybe he is being influenced by the same people who are evaluating Arisia? Something in the world is binding both good and evil and Ridley is on his radar.

As we think about what to do, Relys realizes that the creatures that we fought came through that portal. Ridley sets an alarm at the portal to trigger if something comes through so that we can continue to investigate the rest of the building.

We start to investigate the rest of the top floor, Ridley leading and Fungus in the back. We find a small passage that leads to a door. This side seems to be symmetrical to the other side. We go up to the door and Ridley opens it. Smooth black stone walls…not much in the room. Looks like a leader’s chamber - bed, desk, there is an amulet with the symbol of the BC on the desk. Ridley finds a secret compartment with a small sack and a potion. Affy picks up the amulet - thinks it is more than a holy symbol. She hands it to Cerul and he thinks it might be dedicated to Malgri’n - but it is very pristine, and doesn’t look to be much used. Relys takes a look, too. She thinks the amulet with the symbol of the Cult of Eternal Binding is a key and she holds onto it. The potion Ridley finds is purple with a swirling cloud of pink. Fungus isn’t sure what it is. He also finds 27gp (party inventory). Relys thinks that the amulet might fit into the abacus–the portal key.

We travel to the next room. It is similar in design and construction to the other room. Ridley gives Relys a scroll that he found (dimension door). There is an elongated box on the desk. Ridley thinks the box is okay and opens it, doing some damage to both he and Affy. There is another scroll inside. Fungus looks at it. This is beyond his means of usage, but thinks he could in later years. It is a scroll of imprisonment. He will hold onto it. Affy looks around, but doesn’t find anything else.

As we turn to leave the room, Fungus hears steps next to him and he looks down and he sees the little guy sizing him up. Fungus asks where his master is. He says “at the bottom - you’ll meet him - just take your time.”

We decide to go back to the main level, Ridley leads us to the central staircase - the way we came. Ridley and Kaizo walk ahead in stealth. We come to a dining room area. Very plain. Stone tables carved directly into the floor. Ridley doesn’t find anything hidden. He leads us to an open archway and into a room with a door. He doesn’t see any traps and opens the door. Looks like a place to prepare food. Jars, boxes, etc. in the room. RIdley opens up a jar and the food seems preserved and fresh. We each get 2 rations.

The Shadar-kai Visitor

We go down the hall, heading east. Ridley feels his alarm go off at the portal. He tells the group and Relys tries to look through Hopper’s eyes and doesn’t see what sets the alarm off. We all split up to run upstairs - 4 different directions. As we all run, Fungus hears slight clanging metal and feels a chain around his throat and hears “don’t move, don’t speak…let’s go back down.” As Cerul gets to the top of the stairs, he notices that Fungus isn’t there.

Fungus is getting pulled back. He tells him his name. He pulls him down the stairs and let’s the chain go and then slips around in front of him. He sees a bald elf with light skin and muted gray armor. He has spiked chains in his hands. Fungus has no idea if he is good or bad. He didn’t attack him, but he also wrapped a chain around his neck. He tells him he came through the portal. He asks where he is. Fungus tells him the Blackbinding Citadel (misspeaks). “He is summoning them” and Fungus asks “who is he?” He says “Kael Vorduun (a yugoloth).” He tells him he cannot shout to his friends. He says he was following demons through the Shadowfell and Fungus tells him that we killed the demons. He relaxes a little. Fungus tells him the bad guy is in the dungeon and that we are planning to go to the dungeon below to take on the bad guy. He says he is going to get more Shadar-Kai to take on the bad guy - that he wants to go through the portal. Fungus tells him that we will help him. Proceeds to tell him about Kaizo and friends. Meanwhile, Cerul looks for Fungus and sees the elf. The rest of the group sees no one…then sees them coming up the stairs.

He says “what is that unnatural feeling” and starts to look at all of us. He says he focuses on Kaizo and Fungus. There is a fey presence; it is strong. Fungus says it is him. He asks what our plans are. We tell him that we have been searching around and that the guy is waiting for us and about his familiar/friend who keeps popping up. This guy mentions Gehenna–known for having yugoloth–is sending the fiends through the Shadowfell. This guy is named Acil. He says he is going back through the portal to get help and will be back. We ask him NOT to close the portal here yet.

We decide to go back down. We go to the first door we see. This is a room that feels like the rooms on the top floor - bigger, 2 beds, desk, and a folded cloak at the foot of the bed, small stack of stone tablets, and a horned cup. Relys casts detect magic and notices the cloak is magical. It is black and looks leathery (transmutation). Ridley decides to tell everyone that he found pigments that allow the user to paint something and it becomes a reality. Kaizo takes the cloak for now - until we figure out what it is. He puts it on and it shapes to fit him. Relys looks at the tablets - something looks recently carved. “Containment without renewal is merely delayed failure.” Seems like someone was trying to be deep, but seems a little like word salad. Affy picks up the horned cup.

We make our way to the next room. Stone shelves holding tablets and tomes. Geometric shapes on the floor. This seems to be a record room, references to trials. Detailed procedures about binding. We take a moment to read and look around, trying to figure out how/when the group decided to bind people. Ridley investigates and sees the names Shoddy Nell Rootshifter and Nelindra the Infused in the book ...and it looks likes it is next to the word “Judged.” He also sees the name Selbranan the Moon-touched in the books. Relys still has detect magic up and looks around - doesn’t see anything. Fungus casts a couple of rituals - comprehend languages and identify. Fungus looks at Kaizo’s cloak and realizes it is “Cloak of the Bat.” Ridley ends up getting the cloak. And Kaizo takes the magical pigments.

We go to the last door at the end of the hall and Affy opens the door. This seems to be the biggest room we have found. Discipline hall. Curved walls. Grooves draw eye to the center of the room. Kaizo feels a sense of balance here. RItual markings and grooves where people have tried discipline in different forms.

We go to the center room and see the big emblem on the floor of the symbol. This is a vast chamber with curved walls. There is a circular platform in this room on the floor, but doesn’t look like it has a floor - darkness looks like it goes down to nowhere/is a void. There is a globe of energy surrounding the room (like a snowglobe). We see the cult symbol in the center of the room that is etched out and the obsidian shard in the middle of it is much larger. Fungus goes to touch the globe of energy and takes necrotic damage and knocks him prone in the center of the room. He looks dazed. He touches the shard and it shifts and moves a little. Affy walks over to look at it and picks it up. It is a black diamond - she puts it in her bag.

The little creature appears and mentions “our designation” and then pops away.

We decide to go downstairs. It feels like we are walking through a sheet that stops light as we descend. Anything lit is void of light. Once we get on the other side of this “barrier” things go back to normal. Like upstairs, the hallways lead to the center of the crucible. Everything is quiet. There are skeletons in recesses in shackles. In the distance we see a circular chamber ahead that has chains extending from the walls to the center of the room. Cerul wants to shatter one of the skeletons in a recess and does. And hears laughter ahead.

Don’t Break the Oath

We start to walk down the hall. There is a towering obsidian structure floating in the large chamber. There are 8 manacles holding a tall, gaunt figure. The manacles move as if the figure is breathing. “You have come very far which tells me you are capable of understanding what others cannot.”

Kaizo and Ridley discuss this guy (it must be Kael Vorduun). He says he is the one that has been bound. He says that Malgri’n is the one who bound him. He says that he has been trying to get others to come and unbind him. We ask why was he bound. He says he was an example. Fungus speaks up to ask what it would take to unbind him. And he focuses on Fungus. These spectral chains are not magic anyone has seen. He says “I can help you” to Fungus. He says he can help clear the way for your purpose to Fungus. Ridley is trying to figure out if this guy is good or evil. The crucible will not release me without precise contractual obligations. You would have to take an oath to unbind him to allow his containment to go unresolved. Then you must agree to find something else to put in his place. He says he was brought here by Malgri’n as an example…that he has done nothing wrong. It is hard to resist Malgri’n. His name is Kael Vorduun and is from the plane of Gehenna. He has been here 25 years.

Fungus starts to take the oath to release him. Six of the eight manacles reach out to grab us….and then the other two fall apart and he steps out and then manacles drop. He says I am in your debt. Fungus tells him the name Teldo and he asks each of our name. Fungus tells him about the elf and his friends coming. The creature pops in “is that all we need boss - we get to go home.”

The Crucible has now bound us all to the oath that we need to bind someone else here. We don’t have to bring the person here…if we find someone who is worthy of being bound, it will happen. Malgri’n will make it happen. Malgri’n is VERY powerful.

He turns to leave and floats at a dash speed. Ridley decides to check another corridor here before we go up. His alarm goes off. We head up. We hear the sound of combat on the top floor as we make our way to the main floor. We hear flames, more than two people. We take the tower stairs and as we get to the top and hear the combat end. We head towards the portal and see the bald elf dead…along with 2 others. Relys runs over to loot the dead bodies. Studded leather armor (black) and spiked chains that they use for weapons. Fungus casts detect magic and one of the studded leathers is magical. Relys puts it on and it sizes to her. It would make her look like Shadar-Kai. We also put their bodies in the portable hole.

Portal to Varenwood

We decide to go through the portal. As we step through the gate, the pressure that made us tense is gone. We are in a forest - damp earth and bark. We stand in a small stone circle, druidic marks, stones. Relys feels like she is home. Stones are low and made for small folk. She recognizes this place. Behind us the portal remains open…and we now see smoky glass on the other side. Affy steps up and it doesn’t look like the Crucible, but like the Shadowfell.

Relys thinks that Kael took advantage of the Weaving Night and has triangulated the portal…that he probably took advantage of the ritual and destroyed her village in his attempt to pull people from the Shadowfell to help him at the Crucible. We think that Kael would now be a target of someone we can bind to fulfill the oath to the Crucible.

We decide to make camp some distance from the portal. We head toward the direction of the village - stopping in between the circle and village, under cover of the forest. Before going there, Ridley sets an alarm.

Many things are linked. Kaizo’s meditation & balance is disturbed by Malgri’n. Malgri’n has kept Fungus’ patron Shoddy Nell bound to the Shadowfell, quite possibly because of the Archfey Selbranan. This portal in Varenwood leads to the Shadowfell and must be closed so that Rely’s homeland is safe from intruders.

What to do next?

1. Find the Moonpetal mushroom for Relys in Varenwood in the event the Weaving Night ritual is needed to close the portal to the Shadowfell.

2. Determine how to release Shoddy Nell from the binding in the Shadowfell. What is the relationship between Shoddy Nell, Nelindra the Infused and Selbranan the Moon-touched?

3. See if freeing Shoddy and closing the portal sets things straight for Kaizo.

– The End of the Session –

Into the Shadowfell

In-World6th of Paramor, 1246 WhereVarenwood PlayedMarch 19, 2026

A Visitor – Zaedra

We sit at the camp around a campfire. It is cloudy and getting colder. We are a mile from the portal that we came through and stopped just outside a think wall of trees/bushes/shrubs. This seems like a deliberately thick area. Kaizo sees a hedgehog sniffing his pouch - they lock eyes. Affy asks about the hedgehog - good eating or what? Relys says “good morning” and it replies in common. It then shifts into a wood elf female, Everdawn markings on her cloak. Zaedra Windfury of the Everdawn (from Ferndale) - she smells Kaizo’s Nightbud. Relys asks what she has found.

She brings up the rift/portal…and we comment that we have been there. She says the portal links to the Shadowfell and a building (the Crucible). She doesn’t think it can be easily closed. Thinks we would need to defeat something powerful - the thing that opened it - then can close it with abjuration magic. Her friend Iris (with the Tegenwald Phet) has investigated this as well. She thinks the cult members aren’t there because they went to the Shadowfell and never came back. You either need to go through the rift to kill the powerful person or try to draw them out and kill them here.

As for what took out the village, she doesn’t think it went back through the portal. She says she found gnome corpses 3 miles away.

Relys asks her about the ritual of the Weaving Night, which requires Moonpetals. She has some and gives two to Relys. She then tells Relys about the Everdawn and how she’s heard of our group and that she is recruiting for them. She asks if any of us want to join? Relys does and she is given a pin and told to speak with animals when she enters a new area and they will likely be able to tell her where the local Everdawn group is.

Affy and Ridley ask what type of damage the dead have (the gnomes all over from this town and the area. She says they were drained of life. Iris said traces of dark necrotic evil magic likely drained them.

We know that we are just on the outskirts of Relys’ town. Relys thanks Zaedra for her help and Kaizo acknowledges that he is already a member of the Everdawn and will help figure out what evil did all this damage. She says may Sylunara light your steps, turns into a sparrow, and flies away.

1. We need to figure out where the thing roaming the world is - it is killing.

2. FInd a way to close the rift. Draw out whatever is keeping it open. Handle it. Use a powerful dispel magic to close it

Fungus pulls out the cube and waves it. He hears an ancient voice in his head (I am your hidden root. Shoddy Nell and the Glyphic sings through the rift; my binder hears it, too. Guard the cube for Shoddy alone.) Leaves a feeling behind that something powerful on the other side knows what he did. Fungus says that he thinks that the people through the rift now know where the cube is. Fungus doesn’t want to give it up.

Into Iparos

Relys knows how to step through the branches and leads the group into the once hidden glade of Iparos. This was once a peaceful, charming village. It is now destroyed. Standing stones lean and are broken, floating lantern stones are on the ground, sacred spring is a black stagnant pool. RIdley looks around for clues - he sees small stones all around. Relys thinks that whatever came in to kill the village did it with a shadowy presence because the barrier is still intact. Relys notices that one of the lantern stones is still hovering - she walks over and touches it and the soft blue light turns to gray (she’s sad). Affy asks to hold it (she’s mad) and it turns red. She hands it to Fungus and it turns yellow (he’s chill). She takes it back. She then walks into her grandmother’s cottage, as she looks over and sees the cottage where she was raised, its door off the hinges.

Inside, she sees part of the staff her grandmother used. She knows she needs this staff to perform certain rituals from her village. She casts detect magic. Staff is a +1 staff (but would have other properties should she attune to it). A piece is missing. It used to have a mushroom-like crown at the top, the color of Moonpetals. Cerul casts the spell Locate Object looking for the missing piece - he doesn’t detect it nearby (not within 1000’). She also finds a crystal bell with magic (magic is waning) - it has a crack in it. On the wall is a wreath of silver leaves that were used on the dead (slight magic there). Cerul starts to mend the crack in the bell and the magic becomes brighter. He continues to work on it until it is back to normal. Wreath of Eternal Silver Leaves and Crystal Bell are things Relys puts in her pack.

Ridley sees huge talon marks (maybe like a dragon, but probably not one) are the source for much of the damage to the town. He determines that the creature headed east, probably carrying something. He asks if her village had an issue with a dragon and she says no. Fungus starts looking around the outside of the village. He sees a nice ceremonial robe used when a ritual takes place. Affy looks around and notices a small pouch and picks it up. Inside is a small night bud flower and a moon petal. She hands them to Relys. We ask if they had a wealthy resident…and she says they didn’t really use money. It was “peace and harmony in the commune.” We ask if she’s ready to visit her old house and she says she needs to go. She goes inside and sees her bed is still made. She finds her journal from when she was younger and pockets it. Kaizo walks up and tries to console her with random words of loss and love.

The Shadowfell Rift

Relys takes one more pass through the village and we decide to leave and head back to the portal. As we approach, we line up in a marching order (Affy, Ridley, Kaizo, Relys, Fungus and Cerul). We pass through the rift into the Shadowfell. We see it is similar to where we came from, but lacking life and color. Tall, gaunt trees with no leaves; still air, gray skies. We see movement deep in the trees…shadows. All colors are muted and dull. Kaizo feels terrible, it is drawing everything he has and is taking all of his zen. We see a copse of trees with rocks nearby and take the Shadar-kai bodies out of the portable hole to place here. Cerul starts to remove the bodies. Kaizo feels like he is windblown - feels like he is in the middle of a tornado. Ridley flies up to take a look around. Kaizo feels like he is being squeezed. That something is zero’ing in. He starts to meditate. Fungus starts to cast a ritual. Ridley starts to see movements in the trees and messages Relys that “creatures are starting to close in on the group.” He also feels like stealth doesn’t matter - that something knows where we are at.

IT IS INITIATIVE

  • ROUND ONE: Shadowy figures emerge. Kaizo hits one and puts extra monk-stuff behind it, but it absorbs that damage. He then hits again. Then as a bonus uses a flurry of blows and hits for more damage. He thinks necrotic damage helped and not hurt it. Kaizo and Relys feel that this is something sent to the group - that they were hunting us, but are not the hunter (the person calling all the shots). One then attacks Kaizo and hits with necrotic damage, but it drains his strength. One attacks Relys and misses. Relys shifts into a tiger and attacks. Affy runs over and attacks the one on Kaizo and kills it, then moves over and attacks the one on Relys and kills it, hitting with her sword but also inflicting force damage. Ridley hit one twice and then flew over to another tree. Fungus casts a spell on himself and then hits one, but it does no damage. They all start to look at Fungus. One runs over to Fungus but can’t attack. Another one runs over and attacks him and misses. Cerul casts Radiance of the Dawn and all the bad guys have to make saving throws, as two vanish in radiant light. One runs up to Cerul and fumbles.

Then uses Word of Radiance and he takes additional damage. The one that was hidden lights up, too.

Relys steps down off the rock and attacks the one who has been hit, and hits it, lighting it up. She then attacks again and hits again. You’re a big boy” moves HM over to the “big boy”, shoots at it and misses. He then draws a hand crossbow and climbs out of the tree, runs over, and shoots with his hand crossbow, missing. One of the creatures tries to hide, then attacks Relys but misses. The big boy used a legendary action to hit Ridley… missed due to my goat (the healer) popping off with a flare gun in the dude's face… WOAH 14 temp hp to Riddles... Again my goat… Cerul runs over to the frozen lesser big boy and frees him from paralysis. And then casts Radiance of the Dawn again. Both save, but still take damage.

He then disengages far away. The big boy then gazes at Affy and she takes a lot of psychic damage and is paralyzed. Relys attacks the final smaller creature (she notices that Tananan is whispering something in Fungus’ ear). She hits the creature, killing it. She then runs over to the big boy and hits. The big boy then hits Ridley and restrains him. Cerul misty steps up next to him and casts spirit guardians and kills him.

Affy heals herself with a second wind and dashes over to where she last saw Fungus. Ridley flies up to try to catch Fungus - he has seen him blink before. Fungus makes a death save. Cerul is going to try to dispel the portal. He sees the rift collapse and it looks like it worked. The gate now goes back and forth to the Crucible.

To Qal’dynn

In-World6th of Paramor, 1246 WhereVarenwood PlayedApril 2, 2026

A Visitor – Zaedra

We decide to go through the portal to the Blackstone Crucible - it stays open, no longer attached to the Shadowfell. We get there and note the abacus…Relys decides to study it to see how it works, with help from Cerul. She makes a note of the current coordinates - to note the ones that will take her near Iporos. She learns that she can shut it down by moving the “beads” to one side. And thinks you can control the x axis and y axis to move around the world - glimpsing a blurry view through the portal (mountains, forest, water, etc.). She decides to move the beads a little to see where it resets (we lose vision while it does). As it resets, she is not sure where the coordinates took it. We decide to send a couple of us through to just quickly see where she moved the coordinates to (Cerul and Fungus stay back and the others go through). Relys goes through first, followed by Ridley, Affy, and Kaizo.

Relys steps through and sees no leaves (typical for a cold season). Hears a scattering of something. She thinks she is in Varenwood, perhaps to the east, but no idea how far. Ridley doesn’t see anything specific to identify the area and Kaizo doesn’t see any herbs. Seems like a normal forest. Relys shifts into a owl and flies up to get a sense of the land, looking for water specifically. She doesn’t see any large bodies of water, merely streams and ponds. Looking west, she doesn’t see where we were. Her gut tells her we went about 20 miles.

Relys decides to push the x axis about twice as far. We step through and it is forest again. We come back through and she pushes it again another twice over and feels like she is over water. Relys flies through alone and sees that she is over a large body of water. She thinks it is likely Lake Tribathe. She comes back and shifts back into her form. She makes other notations in her journal of these coordinates, knowing this puts her over the water.

The group decides to head to the Abbey, taking the abacus with us. Cerul wraps it up in a bedroll and carefully places it in the portable hole. We make our way out of the Crucible, marching 2x2 (Ridley and Kaizo leading). We leave the building and there is a layer of snow in the canyon. We are halfway between the temple and the opening to the cave and Ridley hears something.

We see a spectral stone giant’s head coming from the cliffside. There are runes on the spectral form. Translucent and hazy, it almost seems like a portal. He says, I am Amag. I see visitors leaving the temple. Fungus says “Hello” in giant. Little vessel of unclaimed light - looking at Cerul. He continues - talking of Cerul, Muerg, etc. Cerul says, “I don’t believe we have met, hello.” I’m not really here, but hello to you, too. Where are you, Cerul asks. He realizes he knows the answer. There are 3 stone giant clans in this area - they are the Ridgeworn (they protect the Cradlecrest Rise). One clan is the Barg clan (they watch the Bastian). One is the Karos clan (Stonespeaker Atta, knows Cerul). Final one is the Aosul clan - Watchers of all - shamanistic. The High Carver, Uspar, is the one who leads all 3 clans. He told Cerul that this is the guy that took Cerul to Ada. Cerul realizes that his magic is starting to fade. This one says that he watches the emerging temple. But you need to go where you were born…there is something there that he needs to address, but can’t do so until his light is ready.

What he actually said:

Something unresolved waits beneath where you were born, little one. The scar still dreams. Return there when the light within you grows steady… or the mountain may claim what the angel could not finish.

Ridley posits that maybe if Cerul prays to the light he will get answers, because Cerul doesn’t know where he is from. Ridley notices a stone fall off the cliff wall as the figure went back into the cliff. He goes over to pick it up. The stone doesn’t match the other stones from the cliff. This looks like it probably came from a mountain, maybe higher up on a mountain. Ridley gives the stone to Cerul. Thinks maybe it came from a mountain in the CradleCrest (Bastion of Order to Qal’dynn).

The group continues to the Abbey, heading through the caves. We pause as we exit - seeing nothing dangerous. We finally make it to the Abbey of Light, having been gone 2 days.

ABBEY OF LIGHT

We make our way to the Warden, to report back on the status of the cave - what we found and did. The group gives a brief description of the threat being removed, when Fungus starts to tell everything about the Crucible, Shadowfell, portal, dead gnomes, hedgehog person, cube, an abacus, etc. The Warden is confused. We ask for food and shelter and he tells us to go to the Rising Hearthlight.

Ridley asks if anyone here would know of Cerul’s birthplace and we are told to talk to one of the Matrons. We go back to the RIsing Hearthlight. Kaizo wants to meditate, looking for balance. Most of us want to eat and rest. Ridley sets alarm around him. Relys goes through her things gathered from her village (she finds a silver key shaped like a crescent moon in the pocket of the ceremonial robe). We rest.

Kaizo starts to meditate and starts to get his zen back. Fungus snores and farts, things feel right for a few hours. Then he feels a disruption…like something found him, stronger than it was before. He feels dark tendrils wrapping around him and stops meditating and walks into an adjacent room and tries to meditate there. He starts to feel good again, but then a different feeling hits - blood and fire. This feels like something is ripping apart anything it can find - and maybe coming for him. He thinks he is absorbing this wrath for the entire group. Something is very angry at the group and he isn’t sure what or who it is. All he feels is darkness…blood, fire, tendrils. He thinks it might be the Shadowfell. He can’t relax because he thinks this “thing” is trying to bind him. He goes back in the other room and curls up next to Fungus. He gets his rest, but it is not peaceful.

7th of Paramore, 1246

We wake up. Kaizo shares that he had a bad dream and couldn’t sleep. Cerul shares that he spent a lot of time thinking about his birthplace.

Kaizo wants to hang with the monks at the House of Lighted Breath. They bow a bunch. Kaizo shares his experience and asks for guidance and help. He is told that he has “disrupted the shield - what have you done to awaken something that could break through?” He tells him of his recent travels - specifically about Malgri'n and the disciples of his who the group fought. He tells him of the cube that we had - an artifact. He asks if the cube could be a key. Kaizo thinks it could be. He tells Kaizo that Malgri'n might have been looking for the key. He thinks that Malgri'n is the source of his unrest or maybe something else in the Shadowfell. Kaizo tells about losing his balance and about the portals, everything. He tells Kaizo then the shadows are looking for something. Kaizo tells him that we gave the key/cube up. If this is the master of binding and we gave away a key that he is looking for, we probably released some bindings giving away that key…which would anger him even more.

Cerul finds Matron Mallow. He tells her what happened on the road about the visit from the giant. He asks if she was ever told about how he arrived at the Abbey as a child, since he doesn’t know much about his birthplace. {Benediction is the key word} She tells him he was brought to the Abbey. There was a mention that he was carried by Usbar, from a destroyed mountain village called Pekanon. He asks where that is. She said it is on the road between the Abby and the Bastion. He asks how it was destroyed and she has no info about that. She advises him to research at Uktali Monastery or Argent Halls to find information about this old city, if any information exists. She tells him where these places are - neither too close by.

Fungus and Relys go play with children, as Ridley looks on. Affy goes to sit in the Great Hall of Radiance. Ridley and Fungus visit the Shop of Lighted Breath, and meet Kaizo there. Ridley buys a communal hooka at the shop. Kaizo buys herb pouches. Ridley shops for herbs with specific qualities, but can’t find them.

Around lunchtime, we all regroup and go to the House of Reflective Light to use the circle in the cellar. Cerul uses sending to let Qal’dynn know we are coming - he reaches out to the Academy and to the nearest person at the arcane waypoint to let them know the King’s Outlanders would like to use the waypoint immediately. He uses the arcane waypoint to take us there.

QAL’DYNN

We talk about Affy needing to talk to Lorian and Ridley needing to seek information about the wizard he is seeking.

We are greeted as the Heroes of Qal’dynn. We are at the Aetherion Academy - it is a cool day, as we step out into the Quad. Affy asks Cerul to message Lorian about having a meeting today if possible. He does and Lorian replies that we will meet at the Shaded Mantle for dinner.

Affy, Cerul, and Fungus walk about and try to find a mapmaker and jeweler.

Kaizo is going to Tansian’s Herbs (10) and Ridley will follow, stealthily (20), blending into the crowd. Kaizo tries to be tricky and pop up in front of Tansian, but it doesn’t work. Ridley looks around to see if Kaizo is being followed, and he spots someone that he knows looking at them both. They lock eyes and that person pales and takes off running. This person bolts down an alley and Ridley follows. They turn left and cut down an alley to the right and Ridley continues to follow, closing ground. She then cuts left and two heavily armored men step out and say “stay away from the lady - she is under the protection of the Meridian.” The other one eyes him and says "what business do you have with her?” Ridley knows this is Eliyena. He says “she is a client.” A client, the guard says, so you know? So you can help us? We thought she was the key to understanding the noble house. Are you the key? Tell us what you know. Ridley says this is best told in her presence. And they say they haven’t been able to get her to talk. Ridley says he needs to talk to her. They ask if he knows of the House of Myr. They hand him a silver sigil of the Meridian and say that if he can help, then he could become one of them. RIdley asks if he can continue…and they let him go. He tries to track where she went and can’t pick up her trail. He starts to ask others if “they saw a woman run by” and he gets some “yes” and some “no” responses. He didn’t expect to see her in this town.

Meanwhile, Kaizo walks up behind Tansian and she notices him. He asks her about balance, harmony, and transcendence…and she talks of the nightbud and of the Sanctuary of the Three Elders. He asks how it is going. She says it is going well and hands him a letter from (the foreman of the sanctuary)…the build is going well, but he needs another 100gp. If you leave the money with Lord Anceril, they can do more before Natali. He asks about the sales of nightbud. She says they are averaging one per day. She could sell more, but can’t because she can’t keep up with demand. Kaizo asks if she is giving any away for free, but she says she hasn’t because the people aren’t worthy of it. She says a few have come in asking for B/H/T…there are 3 loyal customers who have come back one time each. He tells her that the nightbud will need to be eventually taken to the Sanctuary once it is built. She says that she is trying to create interest, per Kaizo’s initial demands/requests. She says that she would be better off not selling anything for a stretch or two to grow the supply. She gives him the 10gp she has earned from the sales. She says this is helping with the marketing of all of her herbs. He tells her that there is trouble in the world and the spa is much needed, but is a story for another day. He then asks if she has herbs for a communal hooka, and she points him to a shelf. Kaizo buys some “good stuff” for 1gp and leaves to go find Lord Anceril. He finds him and pays him 100gp.

Relys is staying at the Academy to find Pivric. She finds that he has had some classes, but no one has seen him in a few days. This is not uncommon - he likes to go over to Cirandale and hang out in the ring. He goes there and tries to become one with nature - he doesn’t bathe, etc. - and stays there until he has to return for class. She goes to that area to try to find him. She has a hard time locating him, until she smells him behind her. He greets her and is excited to see her. They talk…and she tells him about making it back to her village. He asks if she saved anyone. She says no, but says she found moonpetal and has the ingredients to speak with the dead for the ritual. She could speak with a dead gnome to find out what killed it. She mentions that he needs to bathe. He makes a flower pop up. She realizes that he is trying to become a druid (he stopped eating meat). He says he wants to be like a guy they studied about. He asks of her travels. She tells him about the Crucible, that she joined the Everdawn (and shows him her amulet). He asks how he can join and asks if she can recruit him. He pulls out dry bread and asks if she can sit and catch up. He asks if there is anything he can do for her. She asks him to keep an ear out about rumors of the monster in the Varenwood. She knows that she can use the moonpetal to cast speak with the dead - but it would use up the moonpetal. She wants to be like her grandmother - and knows she needs to find the top of her staff. She lets him know that if she needs anything that Cerul will reach out and/or that she will send an animal messenger. She says he is on the right path and gives him her Book of Nature - which really excites him. She casts druidcraft and makes a huge display of flowers.

Ridley goes back to the herb shop and talks briefly to Kaizo. Kaizo tells him to go in and ask about B/H/T. He takes off his mask and dirties his face and walks in. He asks for B/H/T…and she says “we sell herbs.” Then she catches herself and says that he can get the BHT tomorrow and eventually go to the Sanctuary of the Three Elders after Natali…or can sell him other herbs today. Ridley then name drops Kaizo…and she offers for him to come to the back room to partake of some. He tries to take it, but she sees this, and asks him to leave her shop. He says he will pay for it, but she says that Kaizo is trying to elevate people and says that she wants the nightbud back. He pulls out the hooka and she says she has something else for that. He offers her 30gp for the nightbud and she says no…that it has to be tried first for free. He then tries to convince her that he had a rough childhood and she asks for the nightbud back. She takes some herbs off the shelf and offers to trade. He asks for something addictive and she says she will give him something more addictive. She asks him to consume it right now and he puts it in his mouth. The saliva breaks down the leaf and it starts to dissolve. She asks to see his mouth. She says, he put you up to this, didn’t he? Ridley says yes. Then she asks why he wants something addictive and he says that he wants to get info. She mentions using truth serum…but he says he wants something more painful. She says she will give him what he wants if he promises to not come back. He wants truth serum, too. And also lilies of the vale to dye his hair. He gets all of this for 25gp. As he leaves, she asks if he has a girlfriend…he doesn’t…and she says he can come back.

Fungus, Cerul, and Affy go to the shops to sell extra merchandise and look for a map to buy. We find one for 5gp. This is a detailed map of the Bathean Empire. We buy it. We try to sell the black diamond at a jeweler. He says he doesn’t know if he has the funds for it. It is worth 450gp. He offers us 400gp and a 50gp sapphire. We agree to the purchase.

As the day passes and we all meet for dinner at the Shaded Mantle. Ridley tells the group about chasing a girl he knows, but losing her. He asks Relys and Cerul about sending a message to the girl…and asks him to ask her to meet Ridley at the Shaded Mantle: “The Meridian wants to conclude our investigation. If she can stop by the Shaded Mantle to give her input, they will not bother her anymore.” She replies “No because they want me to turn on the House and I don’t want to die and they will kill me, too.” Ridley then asks him to send one more, “One of our reps will meet you, unarmed. We mean you no harm. They want to apologize. Tonight, please. Death won’t become of you.” She replies “I will meet you on the bench.”

All go in to dinner, except Ridley who goes to the bench. He takes all of his weapons off and gives them to Cerul to hold, as well as the hooka and “warm” herbs.

The group goes in and goes into the basement into the circular chamber. There is a central stage, curtained booths. A drink that lets you hear whispers. We go to the usual booth for Lorian. We brief him on the Blackstone Crucible, fighting in the Shadowfell, Malgri'n , the binding pact we have, Cerul’s birthplace issues. Affy then asks about what he has learned of her father. He says “your father had deeper dealings with the Promissory than most knew. Rhersi Avar is a golden haired female who can speak plainly about the accounts Affy’s dad had. Seek her out in Basctdelm at the Promissory. If the group can make acquaintance with the House of Myr, then you might be able to use the arcane waypoint that they have in Basctdelm that used to belong to Versum Sal.” He doesn’t have any additional information. Affy asks if she is a human and he replies that she is like Cerul. Relys asks if he has heard anything about a monster in the Varenwood. He has not heard anything about that. His focus has been on the Ley Liness that we helped him with. Kaizo lights up his pipe and asks everyone to come in close. He tells a story of his meditation and nightmares and what the monk at the Abbey says (this is the first time he shares everything with all of the group in detail, too). Lorian thinks that Malgri'n had imprisoned someone and the cube was the key and once the fairy took it, the binding was broken and he is mad that his oath/deal was broken….and is likely seeking revenge that we gave up the key that released the binding.

The question comes up - who was released and why? Fungus says it is his patron - the one who gives him his power. He thinks she is a hag, but isn’t sure. She is likely a fey creature of some type, but he has no idea why she was bound. Relys questions whether she is a good person and Fungus thinks she is, because he is. Fungus doesn't have answers to questions and he has not talked to the fairy or his patron since he gave up the cube. He asks us about when we plan to go to Basctdelm and tells us that there is a caravan that is planning to travel there who is looking for protection. We tell him we would be interested in helping them. He tells us when and how to find this group. We tell him about the abacus and what we think it is used for. Relys shares what she learned and we tell him of our trial and error using it. We offer to leave it with him - and tell him of the Crucible. And ask him to keep this between us. We know Lorian has been with the Phet, has worked for the King, has been with the Academy…but is now independent…which is why we trust him because he reports to no one. He has relationships, but doesn’t owe anyone anything.

Meanwhile, Ridley heads to the bench area, musing about how she was an apprentice to a wizard. He casts darkvision. He has wine and 2 glasses for the meeting. He preps the leaf of truth serum and puts them in both glasses with the wine. He stealths around the area (30) and sees her trying to be stealthy as well, but he sees her. He gets behind her and touches her shoulder. She misty steps and pops 30’ away and looks at him. She sees the wine and no weapons. She keeps the bench between them. “I’m here. I don’t plan to go through that again, I have grown in my powers.” Ridley pours wine and sets a glass down and she reaches for his glass and makes sure he sips from both and then she finally takes a sip and says it is good. Why am I here? He says he wants to talk. He sees people following her and he asks her about them. She says that they follow her everywhere. Why are they questioning you about a noble house? She says, they are questioning her about the Rulendors? What do you know? The same things you know. How do you know? Because Heildove told me. He said he would be here. Do you know where he is? Yes I do. Will you take me to him? No because I know what you did to me and that you are doing this for the Ruendors. I don’t want to hurt you. I just want your master. She looks over her shoulder - they want him, too. He says he usually completes his contract is a more timely manner and has been sidetracked. She asks him if he ever thinks he is on the right side of things. He says yes. She asks if he knows about the Rulendors and he says he doesn’t and she says “maybe you should know more about them.” He then says that he is willing to learn more about them if she will help him. She walks up close to Ridley and she says “they are wrong, they did him wrong, he served them for years.” He thinks she is truthful. She says he is here in Qal’dynn and you will never know where he is until you side with him. She thanks him for the wine. She says they want to take down the House and my master just wants to be left alone…and starts to walk away. Ridley tries to cast Hunter’s Mark on her as she walks away and is almost 90’ away. He is able to do it and she doesn’t know. He then slips away and tries to follow her, stealthily, using the mark. She heads to the Lush Lounge Inn to turn in. He doesn’t know what room she is in. This is a two-story building and he knows she is upstairs. He decides to wait a little while, then turn into a bat and fly around looking in windows (polymorph). He finds the window and room she is in. He hangs there until she is asleep. He watches her change for bed and go to sleep. After she is asleep, he shifts into himself and he tries to open the window.

The rest of the group goes to the Pale Lantern. Relys sets up the hooka with the herbs and everyone chills, with Affy watching this time - guarding the group, but also seeing how this affects everyone. Kaizo tries to convince Affy to take part. There are 4 pipes….Fungus, Cerul, Relys and Kaizo take part. This is emotionally calming to the group - they chill. Others come up and ask if they can take part (we say no). There is music. Affy continues to say no at this point. Fungus pays a bard 124cp. Then he pays a bunch of silver. The night is fun for the group. Lots of pressure on Affy. Cerul feels the warmth….he starts to zero in. Relys thinks the guy she needs to find, can wait. The ruined village that is Cerul’s birthplace, can wait. The tendrils that haunt Kaizo, can wait. Shoddy Nel - what is of her - can wait. We should try something cool. They are very chill, as Affy watches.

Ridley tries to open the window and gets in and sees her stir. He sits down with his mask on. He crouches until she is asleep again. He then goes over to her backpack and finds her waterskin and puts a dose of the addictive drug in it. She puts another dose on her rations. Then replaces everything in her pack. He looks for her spellbook, but doesn’t find it. He looks for parchment and ink and leaves the bottle of corked wine on her nightstand. He leaves a note “if you want more, I’ll come find you…I know powerful wizards have sending.” He casts Alarm around her room. As he leaves he notices the two guys from the Meridian outside the Lush Lounge. He goes back to her room and leaves another note - “I’d recommend you leaving another way.” And then leaves and goes to the Pale Lantern.

When he gets there, he sees the group laughing and chilling at the hooka, with Affy standing watch. Kaizo offers his pipe to Ridley, who partakes. It relaxes him, too. There are a lot of people standing around Fungus - he’s been spending lots of money, buying drinks, paying for songs. Affy makes sure everyone gets to their room (she carries up Relys who passed out). Kaizo tries to meditate once he gets to his room. He has no nightmares, but feels a little disconnected from his true focus. He knows he is not 100% - he is missing something - but he sleeps better after the evening with the hooka. The herb pouch was a block.

8th of Paramore, 1246

The group wakes up the next day. Kaizo says maybe everything he shared the night before wasn’t true - that he didn’t have nightmares. We provision up - rations, bolts, whatever people need. We meet the caravan on the SE side of town. There are 3 wagons. One wagon has 2 guards that don’t look like they are from here - they have a heavily reinforced wagon with barred iron doors. There is a little halfling who jumps up and asks if we are here to help. Pa Mottlebrook and Leila introduce themselves. They are carrying prisoners. Another wagon has a tiefling with horses. They sell special stuff, think they know magic. Pa says he is out of ale…has wine for the stockhouse. Is going back to Shademoor. He offers us 100gp for us to escort him to Shademoor and then another 75gp to go to Basctdelm. We agree. Says we will leave soon. Asks if we are walking - no one in the caravan is walking. We ask if it is okay to walk and he says yes.

Ridley gets a message in his head. “What did you do? Where are you? I need to speak with you?” He asks Pa if we are leaving. He responds “I’ll be in Basctdelm.” As we are leaving, Ridley sees a man in purple and green robes in the prisoner’s cart.

– The End of the Session –
A New Adventure · Chapter 26

To Caelora and Beyond

In-World8th of Paramor, 1246 WhereQal’dynn PlayedApril 16, 2026

Escort for Three Wagons

There are 3 wagons:

1. Wagon 1 - Mottlebrook Family (Pa, Leila, 2 teenage helpers) with Affy and Kaizo

2. Wagon 2 - Kael’s Components, a tiefling wizard with Cerul

3. Wagon 3 - The Iron Cage, two city guards, a mysterious guard (with a full black executioner’s hood) and 5 prisoners with Ridley, Fungus and Relys (on Fungus’ shoulders)

Fungus talks to one of the guards about chopping off heads. Ridley thinks one of the prisoners is familiar.

Kaizo tries to climb on the second wagon…in stealth. He sees one crate and several different small bags in the back. There are runes on the posts that hold up the walls of the wagons. The hair on his neck stands up. He tries to discern what might be in the box and how the runes might be linked.

Affy asks him about his cargo - do they make their ale or just distribute it? They make it. Pa encourages her to stay in Shademoor, and to visit the church there. It is very special. He comments about the lumber there - how they have a special person who makes things out of hardwood. The Garden there is really a cemetery - they have a Gardenkeeper who watches the place.

Fungus asks how long the trip will take. It will take 4 days to get to Darafee. Will stay the night there. We will stop at the feytouched meadow. This is a clearing. In the spring it is nice. Mentions he has other stops planned. Darafee to Caelora is another 2 days. Then to the Southern Arch. Then Shademoor. Fungus talks to them about the profit in their wine (a carafe is 10gp; he has 5 barrels of wine).

Pa mentions that there are rumors of people disappearing around the Southern Arch. He doesn’t know much. There were scratch marks and torn clothes and words that folks have disappeared. Pa mentions that it has been a while since he hung out with the likes of someone like Affy (quick discussion here that Affy changes the subject on). Random conversation all day.

We travel the full day and Kaizo eventually sneaks out of the wagon before we make camp.

Camp in a Meadow

We arrive at the meadow. Leaves circle in a pattern. The guards on the prison wagon start to make camp and Affy moves back to question whether the prisoners need to be exercised, have a restroom break. Ridley steps in as well - asks why they are prisoners. The guard won’t say. Ridley offers to help empty their chamberpots. The guards affix manacles to a rope then opens the door to the back and starts putting manacles on each prisoner.

Pa, Leila and their family stop, make camp, and eat.

The tiefling in the middle wagon looks at Kaizo and nods, and then casts a spell around his wagon.

Fungus goes for a walk around the meadow. Kaizo shares what he saw in the second wagon with Ridley - asks him to check it out. Ridley looks at the prisoners when they are exiting the third wagon and thinks one of them is the guy he has been hired to kill. Ridley cleans the chamber pots. Relys forages the meadow and Fungus follows her 100 yards back. She finds supplies to make 3 meals. Affy hangs out with the guards and prisoners - these people are guards of places she hasn’t been. These guards do not have markings of realmwardens…they have a bronze griffon with raised talons on their tabards. Affy asks Cerul about the markings - he’s not that familiar either, but as they talk they think this is heraldry of Basctdelm.

Prisoners in the cart: 2 human men that look like they are bandit/thug types, 2 other human men who are moaning and complaining - perhaps “white collar” criminals, and then 1 in robes (who is an elf). The prisoners sit and eat - manacles and ropes confining them. No belongings on them, but there is a crate on the wagon (that might be holding belongings).

As Fungus circles the meadow, he is looking for signs of fey. He thinks maybe what he sees could be weather-related and not magical. In the distance, however, he sees a light to the SW - thinks it could be a campfire a long way off. He points this out to Kaizo.

Ridley talks to Relys about food. He ends up making goodberries and gives one to each member of the party. Relys gives one of her meals (salad) to Fungus. Ridley talks to the guards about Relys, who comments that she is a cute gnome. He offers a berry to a guard. Another takes one.

The guard who had the hood walks away from the group and takes his hood down. As Ridley approaches, he puts his hood back up - no one is supposed to know who she is. Ridley offers him a berry. Guard comments that Ridley is more his type. They have a discussion about what to feed the prisoners. Ridley mentions that he “executes” people as well. They talk about them both being badasses - the guard shows off a curved battle axe to Ridley, as Ridley takes this opportunity to observe the lock on the wagon and on the lockbox.

Affy attempts to talk to the white collar criminals to find out what they did. They “sold land” that was fraudulent. The hardened guys were criminals - worked out of Basctdelm (mentioned the undercroft of the castle there). Affy talks to the robed guy. He says this was a misunderstanding. She inquires further. He says that he poisoned a shipment that was headed to the Promissory. I have this thing that people think I do, but I don’t really do that. He says that he doesn’t want to go through some of the places we are going. Specifically talks about Caelora and how he doesn’t want to go there. Says he will place a curse on everyone in the caravan to keep us from going there. Says people there are corrupt and want to see him dead. Mentions that Ridley is also one who wants to see him dead. He asks Affy to tell Ridley that “he didn’t do it” but won’t say what he is accused of there.

Meanwhile, Fungus uses special oil on his feet to make sure they are soothed from the day’s walk. He offers some balm to Cerul, and eventually to Affy to lure her away from the prisoners.

  • WATCH 1 – Relys and Cerul on first watch. Relys shifts into an owl to fly and circle. She sees four large 4-legged creatures and 2 humanoids around the far campsite. She flies closer. The creatures she sees are bulas, that are tied up. Two of them are male with antlers. She sees the humans in a bedroll. She flies back to tell Cerul what she sees.
  • WATCH 2 – They wake up Ridley and Affy. They talk about the robed prisoner. Ridley mentions that he is from Cealora and that the people who hired him are from there. He is worried about being there without bringing Heildove to justice before we arrive. Ridley notices that Affy’s eyes are sparkling and she feels warmth in her eyes and in her hands after she rubs her eyes. Ridley walks on the outskirts of camp, presumably scouting. He sees a guard step up to watch him. They talk about Relys - and Ridley mentions the campfire to the guard that Relys noticed people at. He says he is going that way - get out of line of sight of the camp, turns into a bat, and flies back to our camp, then turns back into himself. He hears mumbling inside the iron wagon - whispers. He goes into stealth (rolls a 37 on his Stealth check). Tries to overhear the whispering…and can’t understand what he hears. He notices that the second wagon has an alarm set. He tries to pick the lock on the box on the prisoner wagon and succeeds. He sees a large, decorative tome (with arcane writing), a very nice pouch (2 rings, a greater healing potion), a small sack (components), and a forgery kit and a binder of leather holding up pieces of paper (evidence of things forged). He skims through the contents (noted). He puts everything back in the box and relocks it. He looks into the window and sees a prisoner awake and the other 4 have eyes that sparkle and they are asleep. The robed figure who is awake is looking out the back. Ridley takes the long way around and makes his way back to the campfire. Affy is warm and takes off some of her clothing.
  • WATCH 3 – They wake up Kaizo and Fungus. Affy briefs them about Relys’ findings, then asks about her eyes and what they might know. She thinks that maybe the wizard did something to her. After she briefs them, she decides to prematurely wake up Cerul to get his opinion on her condition. He tries to dispel magic on her. It starts to fade on her. She is better, but feels empty. Ridley comments that the wizard is known for cursing people. And that this is why he’s been hired to kill him. He then says that he is going to kill him before we go to Caelora. Kaizo and Fungus are apprehensive about this. Ridley, Affy, and Cerul try go to sleep. Affy has a hard time getting to sleep - she is agitated and realizes that she shouldn’t have awakened Cerul. She finally gets to sleep. Fungus and Kaizo are on watch now. The tiefling hands Kaizo a cup - “I noticed you haven’t been sleeping well.” Kaizo declines, but Fungus takes it. This is a tea that helps them rest. The tiefling (Kael Vas) argues with Kaizo that he is not fine. And says that if Kaizo every needs to unload to come talk to him. Fungus talks to him about where he is going and why he is going there. He says he is delivering materials to the Tegenwald Phet in Caelora. He says they get their components from Kael Vas (him). He asks them both if they need magical components. He says if there is anything they need, they know who to call. He says he has been “riding with someone.” Kaizo comments that he thought he was riding alone and Kael laughs. Clearly, Kael knew that Kaizo was was in the cart. Fungus acknowledges this. Kael also asks if Kaizo is his friend and Fungus says yes. Kael says that Kaizo is not good and needs help. Fungus casts calm emotions on everyone - which works on everyone (and Affy can now sleep). Kaizo asks for additional tea from Kael. Kael comments that “the little one” can walk - she’s got many years ahead of her. Kaizo asks what he is hauling and he says they are spell components. Kael tried to figure out what Kaizo was thinking while he was riding in his cart all day - which is how he knows that Kaizo is troubled. Kael mentions the dark tendrils. And Kaizo admits this. Kael tells Kaizo that he should inquire with the Phet about his visions to see if they can help. Kaizo asks about everything Kael saw and they discuss this. He says that he needs to try to find a cure.

The Next Day: 9th of Paramore, 1246

We all get a long rest. Pack up and move along. Kaizo tries to stealth into the second cart; Kael notices and tells him to relax there. Relys joins him.

Bulas Wranglers

As we travel, we see 2 big humanoids (Cerul’s height), each walking 2 bulas, and they reach the trail. They are half orcs. They notice Affy. They ask if it is safe to travel north. They are heading to Palason to sell the bulas (100gp for each pair: 40gp for male, 60gp for female). These are beasts that they wrangled in the wild in the mountains. Fungus wants to buy a male bulas. He tries to convince them to take 3pp, 5gp vs. 40gp. He ends up paying 40gp. He looks at both males to see which one is friendlier; asks Relys to see which one would want to travel with Fungus. She speaks with animals - asking if either one like to be a “vehicle for this fine fellow” and points to Fungus. They mention the “oil” that they smell on Fungus - one likes it and one doesn’t. She tells Fungus which would like him. She asks him what his favorite food is and he mentions berries. She makes berries for them all. She asks for his name and he says “me” and Fungus says he will give him a magnificent name. At Kaizo’s prompting, she asks if he is attached to any of the others and he responds “which night.” Conversation ends. Fungus has a new pet.

We continue to move on, bypassing other travels. Pa mentions we will be sleeping in a lodge (Ashbarrel Lodge) on the road prior to Darafee. But when Relys responds that she may not want to, he says that others could sleep in the woods. Kaizo asks about danger - and Pa responds that it would be around the Southern Arch. Kaizo then goes into stealth. Ridley looks around for Kaizo and doesn’t see him. He then goes up to Cerul and asks him about Kaizo - who hears this and then pops into view. They talk about whether or not he still needs to look into the second wagon. Relys hears this and offers to detect magic. Kael hears this and says there is no need to do that - my wagon has no magic other than the runes and magical string. Kael is then offended that Ridley or anyone would want to look around further on the wagon. Kaizo encourages Ridley to offer to buy some of his components. They have a discussion about the type of magic Ridley uses. They talk of holy water, poisons, herbs (he has none). Relys buys the holy water for 25gp. Kael points out “the little acorn” in the bottom of the box and Ridley offers to buy it.

Trygg is what Fungus names his Bulas. It means trusty, secure and safe in giant. He asks Relys to tell him his name and Affy encourages him to tell him himself.

Ridley tells the group he still plans to kill Heildove. Just as a matter of fact. We ask that he doesn’t do anything to compromise our position with the caravan. Fungus says he might help the wizard. Kaizo, Fungus and Ridley talk about curses and the wizard…Ridley doesn’t know if the curses the wizard is accused of delivering killed anyone. Relys jumps into the conversation…that maybe we need to find out more of the story. As the group talks, something smells sweet. It seems to be coming from Trygg. We can’t figure it out.

The Ashbarrel Lodge

We finally arrive at the Ashbarrel Lodge. The wood looks is colored like wood from an ash tree, but constructed to appear more like a dwarven stone structure. Very unique. Pa walks in - very excited to be here “we are going to need some rooms.” This is a big place, big rooms (meant to house a lot of folks). We see some dwarves inside with a row of barrels, all tapped (made out of gray-ish colored ash). They pass out keys to Pa, the guards, the tiefling. We get one room for the group - Ridley will sleep outside (1sp/each).

Only entertainment are storytellers. Fungus decides to buy ale, listen to stories and go to bed. After 3 ales, he is tipsy and sings and dances; after 2 more, he is passed out. Relys takes the ale that Fungus didn’t use and gives it to them. Kaizo meditates in the room. His thoughts are dark and eeries…not great. Ridley looks around outside and tries to speak with bugs in the area - he finds moths and a couple of beetles. He looks for squirrels. He talks to one and offers it berries (it wants nuts). He produces nut-flavored good berries and gives them to the squirrel - he asks him to follow him. He goes over to the empty prisoner wagon and tries to pick the lock on the box on the wagon and can’t. He asks the squirrel to squeeze through the bars and stay in the wagon for a few days. Rest of the group goes to bed and to sleep.

The Next Day: 10th of Paramore, 1246

We wake up. Relys wakes up Fungus who is passed out in the common room. The group heads out. Trygg is excited to travel today. The group packs up and heads out. It is cool and comfortable. Around noon, we round a bend and we see a stone bridge that spans a ravine.

Toll (Troll) Bridge

Next to it, we see a crude wooden hut. There is a sign that says “toll required.” Pa calls out “that is new folks, please check this out.”

Relys shape shifts into a spider and jumps on Ridley. Ridley and Kaizo try to stealth forward, but Kaizo is visible so he waits. Ridley and Relys go forward to the toll house. They notice that some trees have been recently cleared to build this hut. They see the door is closed and the only windows to the hut are high up to let light in. Hut is likely around 8’ around. Relys crawls up the side and looks through a window. She sees a big, blue-skinned guy sitting inside. He has small horns, sharp teeth, and he is scraping a great sword. She has never seen anything like this. She crawls back to Ridley and describes what she sees. He doesn’t know what he is either. Ridley takes Relys back to the group and she relays the info. Fungus and Affy walk to the door, with Relys on Fungus’ shoulder. As they get close to the door, the door opens and the guys steps out and plants his great sword. He says “toll for crossing my bridge - I’ll take one warm body.” Give me one now and you may pass. Affy has not seen anything like this, but she’s heard tales and thinks this is a mix of a hag and demon. Ridley climbs a nearby tree. Affy says “we are not giving you a body.” He says “then you are not crossing.” Fungus says, “how long have you been here?” and “how many people have you eaten since you’ve been here” and he replies that few willingly, but I have to eat. Fungus goes back to “put up his bulas” and grabs the group. Affy asks him why he can’t hunt game. She tries to intimidate him and can’t. He says: “Boys let’s eat.” Three trolls appear.

IT IS INITIATIVE - Combat vs. the Oni and his troll minions

  • Round One: Ridley shoots the closest troll and hits., pushes him back 10’; casts hunters mark on the blue guy. Hits another troll and pushes him back 10’. Troll climbs up out of the ravine and rushes forward. Kaizo rushes forward to the nearest troll and stuns it, misses, then hits again. Another troll moves up the ravine and runs forward towards the group. The blue guy casts darkness in the trees. Fungus moves back, becomes large and casts a spell on the closest troll to him and inflicts damage. Relys is on Fungus’ shoulder. She jumps off and becomes a saber and runs through the darkness and jumps onto the troll that Kaizo is attacking. She attacks and hits twice, dealing radiant damage. Cerul runs around the spell and casts a fireball on two of the trolls. Affy attacks the blue guy - hits 3 times (he drops the darkness). The last troll attacks Cerul and he throws out a warding flare. He hits Cerul.
  • Round Two: Ridley hides, then shoots the blue guy and hits twice. Troll is no longer stunned. Kaizo attacks that troll and hits, stunning him again. Then runs over to the blue guy and misses twice. The troll steps out of the blackness and attacks Cerul and hits once. Blue guy uses sword to attack Affy and hits twice and then disappears. Fungus moves his blackness 10’ away from him. He yells to Affy to run away from the blackness. He casts poison spray on the closest troll and knocks him prone. Relys crits on the troll, dealing radiant damage. Then crits again, killing him. Then she hides in the forest. Cerul casts radiance of the dawn on the two trolls next to him. Then casts healing word on himself. Affy runs to the troll and attacks twice, with a crit, knocking him prone. He then stands up and attacks Fungus, hitting once, and fumbling. The blackness disappears.
  • Round Three: Ridley looks for the invisible blue guy and doesn’t see him. And hides. Kaizo moves toward the other group, but holds his reaction to strike if something hits him. A troll stands up and attacks Fungus and hits twice then fumbles. The blue guy attacked Fungus and missed. Then attacks Affy and hits. Fungus misty steps away then moves away, imposing disadvantage on one of the trolls and Affy. He then casts poison spray on the blue guy and misses. Relys moves to the trolls and attacks one of them, hitting it. Then attacks again and hits, killing it. She then tries to hide and can’t. Cerul attacks the last troll with burning hands and kills him. Affy attacks the blue guy and hits once.
  • Round Four: Ridley takes out the blue guy with a bolt.

We loot the bodies - nothing on the trolls. The blue guy has a sword (rune covered), leather armor and a ring. Fungus finds a potion of healing and a scroll (Scroll of Spider Climbing) in a pile in the hut. Affy asks Fungus to check out the stuff on the blue guy. There is magic on the sword and ring. As Fungus does this, we grab the caravan to move them forward, burn the trolls, and burn the hut. Relys and Ridley look for a stash of treasure. They see a cave under the bridge and it smells bad. They see rotten flesh and bones, fur. But also find 1200cp, 850sp, 35pp, and 220gp. They see wagons and other debris. They find a silver necklace with a bloodstone (75gp), carved ivory kalhbit statue (150gp), a seal making kit (25gp), and a carved onyx figurine of a wagon (100gp), traveler’s clothes, chain shirts, arrows, quivers, wagon wheel, regular lanterns, cask of wine.

Fungus identifies that the ring is a Ring of warmth (and takes it) and the greatsword says “Trollsplitter” in Giant (runes) - maybe for Affy?

The guards, Pa, Kael step up and say “money well spent.” We pack up and cross the bridge.

– The End of the Session –
