Don’t Threaten Us

In-World10th of Paramor, 1246 WhereBorealian Loop PlayedMay 7, 2026

On the Road to Shademoor and beyond

As we start to head out, Affy asks Pa if she can ride in the wagon to study the greatsword. He agrees. We continue forward. After a few hours, 2 riders on horseback approach quickly. Ridley and Cerul both see them coming from behind us. They are guards - one burly, one elvish. “Listen up - we are taking our friends back - open up the cage and stand back and no one gets hurt.” The guards on the wagon bow up. Affy walks to the back: “Who are you?” And they say if you aren’t going to release them, then we will be back.

They turn to ride away. Affy yells “stay here” and pulls out her bow to hit the retreating elf and hits, minimal damage. Cerul hits the same one with a scorching ray and then shoots the other, burly guy, missing one and then hitting once. Relys uses speak with animals to yell at the horses: “please your riders are getting you into trouble, come back, we don’t want to hurt you.” One stops and it is INITIATIVE.

INITIATIVE – Combat vs. 2 guys

  • ROUND ONE: The elf sends a fiery bead and then kicks his horse to run away. The fireball hits the area around the wagon. Horse and executioner make their saves; Affy, Relys, and Cerul fail their saves; Ridley makes his saves. The two guards fall, the executioner and horse live. Affy uses her rush to take the dash action and runs forward 60’ and shoots another arrow the additional 60’, hitting. The burly guy runs away. Fungus runs up and picks up Relys and uses dimension door to pass them, then uses a bonus action to turn himself large and moves 10’ from Relys. Ridley moves up 80’ and shoots the burly guy’s horse twice, killing it. The burly guy is prone as he falls off his horse. Relys shifts into a tiger and jumps at the elf, trying to knock him off his horse, but she misses. She moves to him and attacks with her claws and hits, but he stays seated on the horse. Cerul runs 30’ up and at 90’ away, and misses with his spell.
  • ROUND TWO: The elf attacks Affy with an arcane burst three times and she almost goes down, but her orcish traits allow her to stay up, barely alive. Relys takes an opportunity attack and hits him, but the elf continues to move. Affy uses a bonus action to heal herself and then moves up 30’ and fires two more arrows at the elf’s horse, hitting twice. Burly guy stands up and climbs up on the elf’s horse and pulls out a spear. Fungus runs to the horse and throws “sticky juice” at the horse, freezing it for 4 rounds. Then casts a spell on his staff. Ridley moves up 40’ and is 60’ away and hunter’s mark the elf and hits him twice, dealing 33 points of damage. Relys runs up 35’ and jumps at the wizard and tries to knock the elf off the horse, and would have hit, but the elf uses a shield spell and block him. She then attacks burly man and hits him, once and misses once, then disengages and moves back 5’. Cerul is 150’ away and moves up 30’...and tries to firebolt the elf, hitting him.
  • ROUND THREE: The elf talks to the burly guy and says “we will avenge you brother” and then misty stepped behind a tree. Affy moves up 60’ and then misses twice with her arrows, targeting the burly guy. She then yells “surrender, bastard.” The burly guy jumps off the horse and attacks Fungus with a spear and misses twice, then hits once. Fungus moves behind the tree where the elf was and doesn’t see him and is invisible. Ridley moves up 80’ and is next to the tree, and takes the search action to look for tracks. He sees two sets of footprints - one that is big next to the tree (Fungus) and another that sees prints trailing off into the woods. Relys runs up to burly guy and, thinking he won’t give up, attacks him and hits twice, inflicting great damage. She then disengages and jumps 10’ back. Cerul runs forward 30’, yelling- “where did the elf go?” and casts see invisibility. He sees Ridley and an invisible Fungus.
  • ROUND FOUR: The elf is about 25-30’ away from Fungus and Ridley and Cerul can see him running in the trees. He becomes visible and starts floating in the air and is now above the tree line. Affy runs forward 15’ and shoots her bow and hits the burly guy once, it is a crit, then stows her bow. The burly guy runs up to Affy and misses three times. Fungus tries to hit the burly guy and misses and then taunts him, but it misses, but he is in between him and Affy. Ridley follows the tracks on the elf, moving forward 30’ and they stop. He looks up to see if he can see him. He climbs the tree and sees the elf about 15’ away, partially covered. He grabs his whistle and casts summon beast on the elf - and a hunting falcon shows up on the elf and misses his attack. And Ridley climbs down a little. Cerul flies up and sees the elf flying, swatting at a falcon. He then attacks with a fireball and aims it up - hitting the elf and falcon, killing the falcon and elf. Relys runs up and attacks the burly guy, missing twice.
  • ROUND FIVE: Affy runs up, pulls out her sword and attacks the burly guy. Affy fumbles, allowing the guy to take an attack of opportunity and he hits Affy with her spear. Affy then hits him, and inflicts additional fire damage. The burly guy then misses Affy 3 times. Fungus tries to poison and paralyze him with a vial of carrion crawler poison, but misses. Ridley hides, then shoots the burly guy and hits, then shoots again, critting him, but the burly guy is still up. Relys runs up and shifts back into her gnome form and then tries to calm the horse and does. Cerul flies 30’ closer and is now 60’ from the burly guy and tries to wound him with a spell, but the burly guy saves.
  • ROUND SIX: Affy steps up and kills the burly guy with a big hit, using acid on her blade. She then steps forward and crushes his face with her boot and spits on him : “We asked him who they are and this happens - it didn’t have to be like this.” Relys heals the horse and befriends it. She names him Anjou, after his favorite food, pears. She begins to look through his saddlebags.

Ridley loots the elf - sees strange tattoo markings on the back of his neck. He doesn’t recognize them. He cuts off his ear. Fungus drags him back, telling Ridley he is a disgusting person. Affy doesn’t recognize the tattoos, either. They loot the body and find a nice ring on each hand, one is a platinum ring with a nice design (and is valuable - Relys thinks it is worth 50-100gp). Pouch has 45gp. The other ring looks valuable and is plain, but has a shield look - looks like a ring of protection (Affy takes it). The burly guy has a pouch with 45gp. We give both pouches to the families of the dead guards. He also has 3 spears, a circular shield with spikes, and studded leather. He also has tattoo markings on his neck. Seems like “gangster” markings. Ridley and Affy flay the tattoos off of the elf and burly guy.

Relys goes through the saddlebags. She finds 2 silver daggers, 6 days worth of rations, and a bag of pears. We keep the saddlebag off the dead horse.

We go back to the party. Ridley comforts the executioner and hands the pouches of gold to him to cover the loss of the two guards. Affy offers to ride with him, but he tells the group that the wizard is missing from the wagon. Neither Cerul nor Relys see the wizard - Relys notices the other prisoners are very quiet. Ridley asks Affy which criminal did the worse crime - she’s not sure. RIdley pulls out a prisoner and he has the same tattoo as the ones on the guys we just fought. Affy pulls out her tattoo and shows him. The guy almost gets sick from seeing it and admits that the tattoo is an initiation marking for a group that works in the Bathean Empire - they try to take from the rich. They are called the “Inherited” - they take from the rich and keep it (inherit it).

The Rongri

Ridley and Affy have a conversation in their head about killing them - Ridley in favor, Affy not. The rest of the party hears the rest of the prisoner whispering. Kaizo comes walking around, explains that Pa and kids saw what happened and asks where the Rongri came from? He says that the rongri went into the trees. There is a discussion from the executioner about letting him take the bags and go - leaving the prisoners with us. Affy convinces her friends to let justice play out and have the executioner continue to stay with the caravan. Relys and RIdley go to the tree line and she finds claw marks on the trees, that matches the rongri. Ridley then sees that there are many trees and that the rongri likely disappears. He then sees a fuzzy moth fly by and he is contemplating how the wizard pulled off his spell. Relys talks to the frost moth - “I only bloom in the winter.” She asks him if he helped the wizard. The moth says he doesn’t know. She thinks he is telling the truth. She thinks that the wizard was able to polymorph with a moth cocoon.

Ridley walks back to the lockbox and sees a set of keys on the floor. He opens the lockbox and sees that the magic tome is missing.

The dead guard bodies are put in Kael’s cart. A squirrel pops up in the cart and Relys talks to it: did you see him turn into a rongri and he nods. She asks why he is in the cage. He responds that he was asked to watch the wizard by Ridley. He describes what happened to Relys. And he decides to jump on her horse and join the group. Fungus names him Nuts.

The group gathers around Cerul for some healing and a short rest.

Pa pulls out his map and we compare it to our map - his notes the camp sites. We decide to move forward to the next camp site. It is called Silent Watch. Relys and Fungus entertain the children as we move to the campsite.

Arrival at the Silent Watch campsite

The night smells like burning pine smoke as we climb a hill…and see a campfire off to the side, and a guy splitting wood. We make it to the site. Pa tells us that Realmwarden rangers like to hang out here - a good place to take watch. This is Kinwae Drael. Cerul and Relys recognize the name - this has historical significance (like a bad thing happened to someone with that surname). He offers us to stay with him, there is a warm fire, and he has stew. He tells us that the stones help keep the area warm. Affy introduces herself and the group. He says he is a patrol member of the realmwardens. Fungus tells him about the ambush on the road. Ridley tells him about the wizard who disappeared. Kinawe asks for more details and steps off with Ridley to talk to him. He tries to reason with Ridley that if his captive was the most important thing then he didn’t have to join the fight….so to take the blame off his party member. He offers to help track the wizard once he leaves and goes north - and will send word to Ridley. He tells Ridley that things will work out. Ridley tells him about the wizard, about his abilities, and his apprentices. He asks why Ridley is hunting this guy. Ridley says he is a wanted criminal who has assisted with the murder of guards. He again asks why he was initially hunting him and Ridley doesn’t share everything. Kinwae just tells him to trust his companions. They go back to camp.

Relys asks if the stew is vegetarian and says that she will forage - but Ridley gives her goodberries. Relys organizes the saddlebags and her pack and the horse. Affy asks Kinwae about his travels and then Fungus runs his mouth and tells him everything. Cerul pipes us to tell him about the trolls from that morning. Affy asks about anything coming up in their travels and he tells them that there might be trouble in Halesworth. Relys tells him about rumors of trouble at the Southern Arch…and he says that Gar wouldn’t let anything happen there (Gar - is a goliath who has a dockhouse at the Southern Arch). Pa joins in that he thinks the trouble is closer to Bloodbeak.

We decide to eat stew, take watch, and get rest. Cerul and Fungus take watch together and Cerul asks him about some of his spells and agree that Fungus will communicate when casting spells so the rest of the group knows what to expect.

It’s the morning of 11th of Paramor, 1246

We wake up. Kinwae is leaving - safe travels my friends. We pack up and get the caravan going. Cerul helps preserve the dead bodies. The group heads out, with Ridley and Relys in the back. They are looking over their shoulder. They see a mounted figure following the group. Ridley pulls back a little to move off to the side to let the person catch up so that he can follow them. He notices this person is a woman and doesn’t look threatening. She seems to close in slowly. Relys seems to notice that the woman is looking off to the side at Relys. Ridley knows this is a lady from Qal’dynn.

RIdley looks over at Relys and coughs…”this is something I have to take care of, I will catch up momentarily.” She walks to Ridley and says “where is he?” He tells her that he escapes. She questions that Ridley may have killed him and he tells her he didn’t. She believes him and sighs. She says “I know you feel like you failed, but this is for the best.” He asks where he went and she says that she has her suspicions but can’t tell him knowing that Ridley will hunt him and kill him. She tells him that she can’t say much since she has no confidence that Ridley will do the right thing.

The party is now 60’ away. Once the party is 200’+ feet away from them, Ridley pulls his crossbow and it is INITIATIVE.

  • ROUND ONE: He summons a beast behind her. This is a jackal fox. Ridley commands the fox to knock her out. He then closes the distance to her. The fox will then go and try to non-lethally shove her and fumbles, bouncing off the horse. She gets off her horse and looks at Ridley and says “for Heildove” and she slaps the horse to run off to the party and walks up to Ridley and doesn’t attack.

Relys speaks with animals - “whoa, friends, what is going on?” The horse stops and runs around Relys, as she jumps off her horse. The fox says “got to knock it out” to Relys. And the fox attacks the horse and hits, knocking it unconscious. Relys can tell that the fox was under Ridley’s control.

He then carries the woman in chains up to the group. Relys cures the horse. The group asks Ridley about the woman, and Affy asks her name. Eliyena, says Ridley, she is a clue to him finding the wizard. Ridley speaks with the horse and tries to talk to him. Ridley dismisses the fox. Ridley tries to feed the horse goodberries, but he isn’t interested. Relys gives pears to the horse and Anjou. Ridley throws the woman over the horse, after gagging and binding her, and rides the horse with her. Affy asks if she is okay and she shakes her head. But the group leaves Ridley with his captive for the rest of the ride.

Arrival at Darafee

The caravan continues. Twilight starts to close. The group arrives in Darafee. There are frost moths in the air. Pa says we have to go to the Toad to rest since it is getting late. Maybe some of us will be more welcome than others. We find the stables to house the animals (3sp/each to house). Ridley hands the stable boy 1gp to groom the horse. Affy asks Ridley what is going on - is this woman a flight risk. The executioner asks Cerul for help to escort the prisoners to a room - he agrees to help. Fungus offers to help, too.

We walk into the Cozy Toad. We find the main level. This is a nice place with a wooden smell and lanterns lit with fungi (very natural). We find help and are able to get as many rooms as needed. They also can serve food and drinks to the group as well. There seem to be regulars. There is also a light shining down on a guy with shaggy hair, telling stories to people. RIdley asks for his own room, too. And gets some pushback on getting a room. Ridley leaves the inn with the lady. Relys, Affy, and Kaizo get a room. Fungus and Cerul get a room next door to the room with the guard and prisoners. Relys shifts into a spider and follows Ridley.

Ridley finds a place in the bushes and casts alarm (but Relys makes it inside his circle). Ridley keeps her gagged and tells her that he will have questions for her in Caelora. Relys thinks that she is being submissive and is 100% committed to her cause. She thinks her life and sacrifice is worth whatever this is with Ridley. She thinks that Ridley is confused.

Meanwhile in the Cozy Toad, the group goes to their rooms. It smells like evergreen and fresh bread. This is very warm and comforting. We get mugs and food. Our attention is drawn to the human in the corner who is lit up and telling stories. Everyone else here seems to be an elf. Fungus walks over to hear the story. He is talking about being on the road just before the Southern Arches. He tells of a loud roar. He was driven off by something that scared him. He mentions that everyone at the camp scattered away. He stretches the story into a very lengthy tale. People are captivated - even the regulars. Fungus jumps into the area to tell a story….does so with sound effects. Tells the story of the trolls under the bridge. The crowd seems to be captivated, too. Affy offers to buy him a cold one and walks over to join our group. He says, “it is so good to see, uh.” He then says that Affy seems comfortable. This is Thallan Darego. Affy tells him that what Fungus says is true and shows him her sword and tests him to see if he speaks giant (he doesn’t). She then asks him about his stories and the truth of it. He admits to embellishing the story, but he admits that he heard the roars. Affy asks him where is coming from and where he is going. She asks if he can take a message to the King of Qal’dynn. Cerul offers to write the missive - it will be about the trolls and about Inherited. Kaizo tells him about the weed he should find when he’s there. We tell him to go to the Pale Lantern. He then goes over to relieve Fungus and tell other stories to the group.

Meanwhile, Ridley summons the fox and asks him to watch over the woman while he walks away and goes to a general store. Relys sees Ridley leaving and sees the fox standing watch over her. She notices that the woman is bound and gagged just enough to keep her from casting spells or audibly talking. She shifts into her normal form and unties the gag. She is taken aback. The fox growls. Relys then talks to the woman. Look, I know my acquaintance can be a little much, but I’m trying to find out what is going on, because he won’t tell us anything. She says, your friend has a lot of loyalty, because it is blinding him stubbornly. She tells Relys that the wizard was falsely accused of cursing someone in Caelora that Ridley knows. She knows that the wizard (Heildove) would not have cursed anyone. That the Rulendors asked Ridley to help them. She says that she knows that Heildove is innocent and she owes him her life. Relys says that she is on the side of good and would like to help. The woman says that the noble house of Rulendor in Cealora is corrupt and that they are going after Heildove because he would not cross a line that they wanted him to cross. She says that she is not saying that Ridley is wrong, but that his loyalty to the Rulendors may be harming others. Relys says that she will need to gag and keep her bound - that she doesn’t want to betray Ridley. She asks to go to the bathroom. Relys helps her and rebinds her, feeds her. Relys can tell that she has hope that Ridley will find his way to the right side of things and that her mentor is safe for now.

Thallen tells one more story to the room. He talks about this area (we think back to Pa’s map) and the forest beneath Murfield. The woods to the south of Murfield are going dark and dead than winter brings. Something is wrong there. What he describes reminds us about the Shadowfel. He says that hunters won’t go there now. That the land seems to be possessed. But mark my words, the forests to the south of Murfield - they grow hungry. Affy tells Fungus he did a good job with his stories.

Relys shifts into an owl and flies out of the circle and sets off the alarm. Ridley hears the alarm spell go off and shoots the owl - thinking it was a wizard (and not Relys). He hits and damages the owl and now knows this is not a polymorphed creature. So he lets it go.

She flies through a window of the tavern and shifts and rejoins the group.

Ridley is there with the fox and woman. The fox stares at him. Ridley asks the woman what the owl was doing. He asks if this was a friend of hers. She responds that she kind of likes the owl. He then re-asks - what was the owl doing here. Ridley casts speak with animals and asks the fox about the owl. That was some little cute person…it was Relys. He tells Ridley that she had a conversation with Relys and Relys gagged her and then left as an owl. Ridley drops clothes and soap at the woman’s feet and takes the manacles off. He says she won’t get far. He gives her a waterskin. He then burns the woman’s clothes. After she cleans up , he feeds her goodberries and binds her again and they go to sleep.

It’s the morning of the 12th of Paramor, 1246

We wake up and will be heading out the next day with the caravan.

– The End of the Session –

Caelora has Issues

In-World12th of Paramor, 1246 WhereBorealian Loop PlayedMay 21, 2026

That morning in Darafee

We discuss what we know and what has happened recently. Things began to turn strange when we discovered an artifact, the Feywoven Glyphic, beneath the Broken Citadel. We continued on through the land of elves, the Borealian Sway. When we arrived in O’naren, we learned that there was an underground network trying to undermine the elven leaders, called the Rel’Udomina. We spent time rooting out their evil and gained the favor of elven King Highvale in Qal’dynn. Upon exploring a dangerous cave near the Abbey of Light, we learned of a structure known as the Blackrock Crucible that belonged to a group of cultists called the Keepers that Bind. These cultists learned a group of druids in the gnome village of Iparos performed a ritual called the Weaving Night that went terribly wrong and opened a rift to the Shadowfell. Something dark came through that rift and destroyed Iparos and took off somewhere across Myrdae. Being that the cultists worshipped a demigod, named Malgri’n, which was known to inhabit the Shadowfell, the cultists used their Anchoring Gate to create a portal atop the rift so they could enter the Shadowfell. The group was able to leverage the rift to use the Feywoven Glyphic artifact to release an imprisoned fey, Shoddy Nell. Shoddy Nell was able to return to the Feywild as Nelindra the Infused.

We wake up and head outside. The Executioner asks Ridley why he has a prisoner, which is discussed only briefly. Ridley decides to let her ride her horse next to him. Pa says that when we get to Caelora he’d like to have a couple of days rest there, as the Executioner says he will hire Realmwardens to go ahead and escort him to Basctdelm.

And so, the party is following a lead for Affy in Basctdelm to find her father. Upon that road, they have been able to learn more about the person that Ridley is looking for, although he recently got away.

Back on the Road

On the road, Kaizo knows that there is a stop after Caelora called Adore, which is a temple of elven monks. Kaizo also knows the Southern Arch was built a long time ago by giants - huge stones, long bridge. Cerul may have knowledge about that, too. We head down the road - same formation. Pa, tiefling, Executioner wagons. Next stop is Whisperpine (Kaizo knows this is just a campsite). We pass some travelers, then some Realmwardens approach and head to the prisoner wagon and talk to the Executioner. One is hired to join the party. Fungus goes back to talk to him. His name is Realmwarden Eversoul (first name Hagmer in elvish). Affy asks him about Whisperpine and he comments about Illuminot (when Tudara is visible and Endolar is full).

At campsite Whisperpine

We make it to Whisperpine. There are many evergreens, common to Halesworth. We set up camp as dark is falling. We hear rustling to the side, through the evergreens. Fungus and Affy go check it out. They see a clearing over a hill and vegetation is shorter over the hill, cloudless night. They see an individual who is laid back against a big tree. The noise was him snapping logs to get comfortable. He is big for a human, midnight blue leathers trimmed in silver, a very nice spear next to the tree. He glances our way and smiles and he has tusks, well groomed, dark hair. Fungus and Affy talk to him - he wants to watch the sunset and moon show from here and invites the two to join. Kulv Farbreak makes sure Fungus knows that there is plenty of room for Affy, but not so much for him. Conversation about whereabouts. He mentions that Caelora is the oldest elven city in Myrdae - the origin of his spear. He is originally from a clan in the Crimson March, but his family is from the original Mahruud - they are trying to rebrand and rebuild themselves. He talks to us about our adventures and accomplishments to date. We ask what he does - lots of travel, adventuring. Fungus asks him to join us. He declines and then Fungus dips back to camp, leaving Affy and him alone.

Back at camp, Ridley feeds his captive a goodberry. Relys talks about gagging the prisoner. Kaizo notices that RIdley is treating her well. Ridley asks if she is part of the Rel’Udomina - and she looks like she has no idea what he is talking about. She looks at Relys for help. Ridley explains that the prisoner who escaped was her mentor and that she has information about him. RIdley has a goal to achieve and she is a tool to make that happen. Relys says that unless she has committed a crime she shouldn’t be gagged and bound. He takes the gag out and she says that she has no spells prepared other than Misty Step. She says that she isn’t here to hurt Ridley, but is here to tell him that Ridley is wrong - and will ultimately have to tell the ones who hired Ridley that they are wrong. Kaizo weighs in here and there. Cerul is off studying. The others in the caravan are nearby but not paying attention to this conversation. Relys looks and sees a reflection of light near the woods, a little flicker, over Ridley’s shoulder. Relys excuses herself to go potty, she tries to find a good space to squat down and do what needs to be done. She's like 30ish feet away from the group, and she whispers to the darkness “Oi, if ya here to help da lady, im on ya side” (in a high pitched voice as she normally does) She thinks it’s the wizard without any further thoughts. Relys fails a charisma save against some force and loses control of herself. She is incapacitated condition. When she returns a few minutes later she walks around and glances at everyone very slowly and makes a complete circle around the fire. After she walks the circle, there is a chill. Kaizo thinks something is up, but can’t put his finger on it. Relys feels a chill and hears in her mind - “avenge me.” Kaizo and Ridley see Relys stumble and catch herself and she comes back to herself. Ridley asks her if going to the bathroom hit her that hard. She feels like (with her nat 20 insight roll) she was possessed - like maybe an ancestor. She asks Ridley to walk back into the woods with her to investigate. Once they leave, Fungus takes the gag out of the captive’s mouth and unlocks the manacles. He tells her she has to stay.

Meanwhile, Affy and Kulv are sitting and watching the moons. Kulv shares stories about his travels. He seems a little envious of the fame of our group. He talks about Sylunara. His eyes mirror the moons, one red and one blue. She comments on this and he says that he has followed Sylunara for a long time and has grown more fond of that goddess over time. He says he is “moontouched” - it is where his abilities come from - his ability to protect himself and in things “arcane.” He then grabs the spear and mumbles words and vines wrap around the spear. Affy comments that his abilities seem like Fungus’ abilities. She then shows her psi-leap by flying 60’ away - and says that she doesn’t fully understand her powers. She hangs for a little longer and then wishes him well and returns to camp.

Meanwhile, Ridley and Relys are in the woods trying to figure out who or what may have possessed Relys. They can’t find anything. They notice Affy flying. Relys turns into an owl and Ridley turns into a bat and the both fly up to check things out. They speak to each other as Relys casts speak with animals. Ridley sees Affy walking away from the half orc. He then sees the orc take the spear, twist it and pull it apart and take half of it and stick it into the ground and then small orbs, the color of the moons, circling his staff. He points this out and the two swoop down to land on the tree near that half orc. They notice him reattach his spear and then set out his bedroll and go to bed. They decide to go back to camp.

Meanwhile, Cerul is off studying and meditating. Kaizo pulls out his pipe and lights it up and looks up at the captive, trying to determine if she is telling the truth on why she is there and also if she is a threat to Ridley or the party overall. He doesn’t think she is a threat to the party, but she could be a threat one-on-one. He also thinks she is being truthful. He offers her some pipe, but she declines. He asks her what her story is. She says she would like to see that Heildove is okay and get the target off his back. Kaizo comments that there is love between Ridley and the captive. He asks her where she is from - she is from Palason. She met Heildove in Qal’dynn a few harmons ago - he needed to share his knowledge and took her under his wing. He won’t give me the details of everything, but I know that the people coming after him want him dead and he is wrongly accused. Kaizo senses that she is scared that she is going to die and comments as such. She asks if he knows the Rulendors - he does not. She says that she fears for her life since they want Heildove and he is not here, Ridley does not have him. Kaizo is trying to figure out if he can protect her and he thinks that she thinks that he cannot. Fungus tells her that she can leave if she wants - Kaizo says that he can’t let her go on his watch (his promise to Ridley).

Ridley and Relys fly over to where Relys was originally possessed. Ridley is trying to find out if his bat-sense helps him figure out what is going on. He notices something at the edge of his 60’ range triangulated with the campsite and they fly toward that. When they get within 10’, they see something - and must make wisdom saving throws (and both do). It turns and looks at them - it is a ghost of a forest gnome and releases a terrible visage (which they save on). Relys lands and shifts back “I’m a friend - what happened to you?” It looks at her knowingly - it is one of the elder druids from her home that she recognizes - she says “Elder, do you remember me?” He is hesitant to talk. He turns to face north and then says “I just hope that your path doesn’t lead you too far astray,” and starts to go north. She senses that he came specifically to find her to ask her to avenge their village. She thinks that the spirit is not going to leave her alone, but is also not going to get in the way. It is clinging to her…wherever she goes, it will go. Until she is able to put it to rest, it cannot rest. Ridley says to make sure that vengeance doesn’t consume her soul. They discuss killing the creature that killed her village…as they make their way back to the campsite.

Meanwhile, Kaizo starts to tell the captive about his backstory. He then asks her why she is important to Ridley. Ridley and Relys come back. Affy asks Fungus if she noticed Kulv’s eyes and explains why. Then asks Relys about what is going on with her. Group talks about these things. Affy comments that the captive is no longer bound and gagged. Discussion about Ridley being the “jury and executioner” and that she is not the target of his bounty, but that he is holding her, hoping that the wizard will show up. She is the “bait.” Affy calls for Cerul…and asks if he can help send a message to the wizard. Fungus starts to cast a spell around the fire and calms the emotions of everyone around the fire. RIdley tells Cerul what to say. The group advises that Cerul make it a friendlier message. He does.

Discussion about how much Ridley is making on this bounty. He is making no money. Cerul gets a message back from the wizard. He says he cannot get there that fast…and that he will avenge his apprentice. We go to bed. Regular watches - no issues.

The morning of the 13th of Paramor, 1246

The next morning we wake and decide to head to Caelora. Ridley tells us his plans - he was going to secure her in the town then reveal to the Rulendor family that he has a valuable asset that he is holding. We discuss the plans as we walk. Ridley describes himself as a lapdog of that family…and Relys asks if he wants to do that forever. He says that he wants to retire from that….

In Caelora

We get into mid-afternoon and we see Caelora on the horizon. Ridley stops outside the city to dye his hair white. This is a magnificent terraced town. We come to the stables first - the Silver Hoofyard. Pa says “let’s take 3 days and meet here on the morning of the third day at the Silver Hoofyard.” The group agrees. Realmwardens talk about the dead guards and about having the Executioner and prisoners move on the next day. We ask about boarding the animals here and do. We see a procession of Realmwardens approaching from another direction. We notice a couple of them have banners - Dark Blue and Crimson banners (Crimson group is larger). The Realmwardens are guiding two horses that have bodies laid over them. The Dark Blue group heads towards the Rulendors. One notices Ridley and motions Ridley over. Ridley knows him as Urovil Rulendor. He is a cousin of Nanise Rulendor. “Welcome back” he says to Ridley. He is covered head to toe with fancy elven clothing. Ridley notices a burn scar on his face that he’s never noticed before. “It is a pleasure to see you.” Ridley: My apologies sir, you are catching me before I have a chance to dress up.” Urovil: Hopefully you have good news? RIdley: Yes - I do. Urovil: That’s great news because I have had a bad day. It would appear as though the Airithels had a little bit of a hunting accident. Ridley says, “I would like to freshen up and formally present myself to the house.” Urovil: When can we expect you? They agree on tomorrow - for an early lunch. Then he walks back and hands off his horse and goes on his way.

The group decides to go to the trade terrace. Discussion of where to stay the night. Ridley suggests the Gilded Pigeon rooming house. Fungus pushes back since we have money. Ridley explains that particularly Fungus and Affy will have a hard time fitting in. Affy takes her scarf and fashions it around her head to somewhat disguise herself. In response, the stablehand says that we don’t need to worry. She tells us of the noble houses: the Rulendors, the Airithels, and the Venathrials. When Affy asks a follow up question, she changes the subject and walks away. Ridley explains about the politics of the city - elves rule, money rules, noble houses don’t mix. Ridley and Relys talk rats and Affy mentions that she had a pet rat (Smarmy) and grew up with goblins.

Relys and Ridley take the captive to the Gilded Pigeon.

The rest of the group goes to the Forge, then the Daefar Terrace. Something doesn’t sit right with Cerul - his gut is turning. He feels sick. As he follows the trail from where the horses carrying the dead bodies came from, there is something evil (opposite the light) that he feels. They get to the Cast of the Knowing Hand - a 4-quadrant area (artisan, jewelcrafting, armorsmith, bladesmith) - and there is a centered courtyard with wares displayed. Everything is of high quality. Cerul talks to the armorer and is looking for a shield of the enchanted variety. She says that she will have to see if she can find something for him - she has nothing at the ready - but says to come back tomorrow.

MAGIC SHIELD AND PRICE FOR A SMALL +1 LIGHT HAMMER

Kaizo spent 110gp to send 100gp back to the Sanctuary.

Group goes to the Daefar Terrace, passing and observing the bathhouses on the way. Kaizo is particularly observing the glass-roofed buildings, herbs, etc. - inspiration for the Sanctuary. He sees a woman with vine tattoos, mossy colored hair wood elf and she says to Kaizo “welcome to Dawnpetal.” He then asks if she has Nightbud and then goes into his pitch about his herbs and what he is spreading. She misunderstands Kaizo’s intentions and calls her brother to come over. He immediately intercepts the conversation and doesn’t give Kaizo a chance to explain…but he finally does and goes through his pitch again. But he is unable to convince them of his intentions. He asks for their “behind the counter” best and they say they don’t do that here. Affy doesn’t take kindly to their rebuff and tries to help Kaizo and gets mad and leaves. Kaizo decides to help a lesser vendor in the poorer sections of town since they don’t believe that he has Nightbud.

Cerul mentions that he sensed something bad and noted they headed to House Rulendor. He doesn’t act on it for now, but keeps it in mind.

Kaizo looks for trade space and can find it and then decides to find Ridley at the Pigeon to inquire about finding another herbalist.

As Ridley, Relys, and Eliyena walk, they go around the top of the Daefar Terrace to head down to the quarters and hear someone trying to catch them, huffing and puffing. Before they catch them, the rest of the group is coming out of the herb shops and Affy catches a familiar person walking around the other side of the pool - it is Kulv. Ridley turns and sees Nanise: she says: “I’m glad I caught you before tomorrow.” Ridley introduces Relys to her. Ridley says he was expecting to see her tomorrow. She grabs a pouch and hands it to Ridley, saying: “I’d like to relieve you of your duties and I will tell him tomorrow that you were unable to make it to lunch.” And she is backpedaling…and he is trying to get to the bottom of what is going on, but can’t.

– The End of the Session –

Into Hell

In-World13th of Paramor, 1246 WhereCaelora PlayedJune 18, 2026

Ridley & Nanise

Ridley, Relys, and Eliyena are walking. Eliyena says to Ridley “is she the good one?” He says I don’t know and runs after Nanise. Eliyena asks Relys what that is about and Relys tells her hopefully it means that he no longer works for them. Meanwhile, Ridley catches up and Nanise tells him that she cannot speak to him openly here and that she won’t meet at his attic. They agree to meet at the Wayfarer’s Rest in two hours for dinner.

Affy & Kulv

Affy starts toward Kulv and yells his name. He looks back at her, grinning. He hoped our paths would cross and she replies “of course they would - you knew where I’d be.” After a brief discussion, they agree to meet at an overlook on the far side of the Daefar Terrace to watch the moons rise.

Fungus & Cerul visit Vossel’s Oddities & Essences

Fungus and Cerul make their way to the Reverie to get washed. Fungus decides to drop some coin buying random things. He likes the Oddities store - it draws him in. Smelling dried parchment and herbs, he catches alchemical scents. He sees a half elven female, older, who is moving stuff on shelves and he offers to help her. As he does, he notices a bottle. She declines the help, but he asks for help with the bottle - it has a faded handwritten label that looks familiar and nostalgic. Cerul is looking around and he sees the woman walk up to Fungus. She reaches up and snaps Fungus out of his trance “that is one of the old stock, most people don’t look at it twice.” This looks like scented oil of some type that might be spread around to make wood and other things smell good. Given how old this is no one wanted to spend the 10gp that Lyn’don wanted for it. Fungus - “oh, you know Lyn’don?” They talk about him….Fungus says he knows him - is not his biological father, but is close. Fungus says I haven’t seen him - I presume he was dead. Then she calls Fungus by his real name and he nods and she walks off and says I’ll be back. Cerul - hearing this - asks Fungus who Lyn’don is and he says he was someone who took care of him when he was young. While they wait, they look for additional bottles. She comes from a back room holding a big book. Fungus says - how do you know me? And she said that he spoke of you as he was training me. He gave me this book, but based on what he told me, I think it would be better for you to have it. I don’t think there are any other Teldos. And Fungus says “no - it is rather unusual.” Looking at the book, he realizes this is a book that was used to teach him when he was young. He doesn’t remember much. She says - there is something in the back and she pulls out pages and hands them to Fungus. He asks when she saw him last - he’s trying to figure out if he is dead. She says that she thinks he is alive. The last time he was here and he left that book, this bottle, and a few more - then headed to Basctdelm, he said he was looking for Maester Sylvandor. He asks if she has more time to talk about him. He decides to hang out and ask questions while she works. Cerul looks on as the torn pages, as Fungus continues to talk. Cerul notices that the torn pages have markings that give off a soft golden glow before he closed the book back up…and points it out to Fungus. They open the book and see the light. As Cerul gets closer to the pages, the light gets brighter, then dimmer as Fungus pulls it away. They don’t recognize the markings - think this is in a language they don’t understand. Fungus casts Comprehend Languages spell to read the text. He reads the words and they start to come into view. It is about Cerul based on the way the light responds to Cerul:

“This mark was on the blanket he was wrapped in when I found him. Sylvandor called it ‘the last light of Pekanon.’ I don’t know what it means, but it glows faintly when the boy is near. If something happens to me, he needs to know where he came from.”

The book itself doesn’t have anything to do with Cerul. This is more of a journal of Lyn’don’s. But those torn pages refer to Cerul and the mark glows because of him. The mark seems familiar to Cerul, but he can’t place it. He asks Lira if she knows of the mark - she says she has never seen it glow. Clearly, Cerul and Fungus need to follow up with Sylvandor and/or Lyn’don at Basctdelm. Cerul mentions that Uspar said that there is something unresolved at Pekanon…which is where Cerul is from.

Fungus continues to shop as he talks to the shopkeeper, Lira Vossel. He wants to find a healing potion and trade something for it - maybe his potion of fire breath (which is worth 200gp). She finds a Greater Healing potion and trades for it. She says that she hopes Fungus finds him and to tell him hello - and he says he will try to bring him back.

Warmth of the Lantern Stone

Relys and Eliyena speak, waiting for Ridley to return. She feels warmth in her pouch and investigates. She feels the Lantern Stone - it is glowing warm, giving off a dim light. She has never noticed the heat. It appears to be yellow. She feels that this is trying to tell her something but isn’t sure what. This thing is more than a mood stone - it seems to be something else. Ridley returns and sees her eyeing the stone. They talk about it and Ridley thinks that Caelora is a magical place and is the oldest elven town…so maybe the magic here is triggering something, but he isn’t sure what. Perhaps someone who is from here might be able to help figure this out. He thinks the Airithels (nobles) might be able to help or someone in the Terrace of the Eternal Song. He offers to go with her, but that it will have to wait until tomorrow.

What to do with Eliyena

He says that he needs to put Eliyena in the attic, but they discuss. He checks the pouch he got (250gp). Discussion continues about why Eliyena needs to be in the attic. Ridley says that something else is going on and he wants to get to the bottom of it. And that because he is concerned for her safety he wants her to stay there. Relys offers to keep Eliyena with her and Ridley says that he doesn’t trust her. They continue to discuss the attic - pros and cons. Relys calls Ridley an asshole and says he is being cruel to Eliyena. He concedes that Eliyena can stay with Relys. And then says that Relys should take Eliyena out and keep her company while he is having dinner. She tells Ridley that she and Eliyena are going to the bathhouse. Ridley then offers for them to come with him…they agree to go and sit nearby and get a drink…if Ridley pays with his new coin. Relys also says she is going to get them a nice room with a bed. Eliyena asks for her arcane focus and Ridley declines. They then discuss the attic and Ridley says he fixed it up after he broke in and took it over. But either way, the gals will stay at the Inn.

Wayfarer’s Rest – Kaizo & the Stranger

Kaizo goes to the Wayfarer’s Rest - he’s the first to arrive. It is very nice and very old, but well kept…warm and welcoming light. One person gets Kaizo’s attention because he is dressed like Kaizo and leaning over the bar, perhaps drunk. Kaizo walks over and orders a drink, trying to catch what the guy is mumbling. He can’t catch much, but the guy smells of booze. He thinks this guy was a warrior of mercy. He is a dark-haired woof elf. The guys sighs and looks at Kaizo - you carry yourself like one of them. Kaizo: one of whom? Him: One of those from Adore. Kaizo: What does that mean? Monks go to the Abbey of Light or to Adore. Most of the Warriors of Mercy go to Adore. Kaizo: I have spent some time there and you look like you have too, long ago. Kaizo: so what has you in such despair? Him: I’m out of drink….and they don’t let me back in there anymore. Discussion about ranks between the two - the guy asks if Kaizo was involved with the politics there and Kaizo says no. Guy - they forced me out. I was only there a decade. I don’t think it is right that they forced me out. Kaizo: why did they? Him: I questioned them - just like the guy who was here asking for herbs, named Haleth. (Kaizo doesn’t know the name). Kaizo: what type of herbs? Him: I think he was looking for answers more than herbs…he was asking too many questions, mostly about me (he was one of his students). Kaizo tells him we will be passing through Adore - you know where Haleth is? Him: He is missing now - has been missing for a stretch or so. More discussion of why he was kicked out…once Kaizo orders him another drink. He offers that he thought they were changing their ways, stances, routines, practices, etc. - everything was off. But I don’t want to drag you into this. Maybe we can catch up later. Kaizo - where are you staying? Him: I’ll be here. He then plops his head down. Kaizo asks the inn keeper if there is a room. They are 8sp/night (2gp for wealthy; 4gp for aristocratic). He offers to get the man a room for 2 nights on him…and pays the barkeep 3gp and tells him to “keep him watered” for the next couple of days. Kaizo wants to keep the man there for a couple of days until the group leaves.

Wayfarer’s Rest – Everyone is there

Everyone heads to the Wayfarer’s Rest, except Ridley. We all sit at a table. Ridley and Nanise get their own table. Affy tells Fungus and the group about Kulv. Fungus agrees to follow later and try to listen, stealthily. Relys implies maybe there is something romantic, but Affy talks about how she has never liked her “orc-ish” looks, wishes she were more human. But won’t judge the guy based on his looks…yet. Kaizo is impressed by what Affy shares. Everyone orders a meal (5/sp each). Kaizo asks why Ridley is sitting over there with a lady. Eliyena and Relys answer. Kaizo implies that Eliyena and Ridley have something going on…but Eliyena denies this. Cerul pulls out the paper with the mark and it glows. The group asks what this is. Relys pulls out her glowing stone. They both try to explain where/why/and how their items glow. Relys says she will find out tomorrow hopefully. Cerul explains the tie of him to Fungus. They then talk of their need to go to Basctdelm. They ask Fungus/Teldo about Lyn’don. He tells of how he ran away when a house burned down when he was younger and never saw Lyn’don again. Fungus then shows his wares - a large chest he bought, the aromatic oil, etc. Kaizo shares about his new monk friend from the bar…and how we need to go to Adore because there is strange stuff afoot there.

Ridley Speaks with Nanise

Just before Nanise gets to the table, Ridley tries to get the best bottle for his table (25gp). He then sits and Nanise approaches the dark corner table. She is hooded. The group watches from across the room. Ridley asks what is up. She says that she has heard rumors of all of Ridley’s doings, like the Outlanders of Highvale, etc. She says that she doesn’t want to hold him back. Ridley thinks she is holding something back. He says for her not to beat around the bush…she doesn’t need to hide anything from him. She says that she shouldn’t speak with him, because it could be dire. She says he deserves the truth: house Rulendor has changed. Urovil is not the man he was. He has changed. She says she doesn’t know what he is involved with but he spends a lot of time in the basement. Ridley comments about the burn mark. She says it is not old. She reaches into a pouch and presses something into his hand “if you really are a hero - this will open a hidden entrance into our cellar (this is a key). Go there and don’t confront him alone. I don’t want you to kill him, I want to know what is going on with the House. I would like to restore it. Just know that he is dangerous and he has great influence over my father.” Ridley agrees to do this as a favor to her. They spend some time with small talk. She says that she is scared. She seems hopeful as she looks over to the party. He confirms that she no longer wants him to go after the wizard…she says that he was framed and she released him from that duty. He then tells her about Eliyena and how he was going to use her as leverage. She replies that Ridley has become dark…and he reminds her of the things that she has paid him to do. She says not to remind her - that she is glad that he has become a hero. He is humble about it, but she says that she has faith in him and for him to stop chasing river rats. She questions why he is hanging out with a half orc and he replies that all tools are useful. He offers for Nanise to take the bottle, but declines. She thanks him and says “may the jackal be wild” and leaves.

Kaizo watches Eliyena to see if she is jealous that he is paying such attention to Nanise. He realizes that Eliyena is very deceptive. Ridley then walks over to the group. He briefs us - tells us that he is relieved of duty and has been asked a favor before we leave. He tells us what the favor is. Then Cerul comments how he sensed evil from that man when we first arrived in town. The group agrees to visit the cellar tomorrow night - Ridley will scout the route tonight (as a bat). Then he tells Eliyena that she is free to go and offers her wine. Cerul mentions that he will put her up for the night.

Affy leaves, with Fungus trailing, to meet Kulv. As Ridley and Relys scout together. Affy approaches the meeting spot and he is sitting there. Kulv is looking at the moons. He pulls out a leather casing around a glass jar and a cork and 2 cups. This is a nice steaming tea. He says, let’s walk. And they do. Fungus sees them in the distance. He tries to get closer without being spotted. He notices a couple of areas where they walk and he’s holding a cup up and he reaches Affy’s back to guide her. He describes the moons as “honest.” He guides her to a bench and they sit and talk about nothing. Then they talk about the moons. I haven’t been honest with you, Affy. I like you and I serve a greater power - Selbranan the Moon touched. In all honesty, I’m a bit torn now that we have spent time together, but he’s been watching my giant friend. He knows of the powers that binds him. His pact is not a gift, it is a chain. I think Selbranan (fey lord of the moons - he had a conflict with Shoddy Nel…had her excommunicated) can offer Fungus something far greater. So why talk to me, says Affy. You know him and he can help you, too. How can he help me, she asks. He knows what you seek. So you know? He knows about your father. He can help you. You can help get Fungus to break the bond with the hag and turn to Selbranan and he will help you. Selbranan recognizes the potential that Fungus has. Can he meet with him, she asks? Perhaps. Think on it…I’ll be around. He leaves and Affy looks for Fungus but doesn’t see him so she returns to the inn.

Meanwhile Ridley and Relys fly to the house - Ridley knows the way. They find the entrance to the cellar. They see some Realmwardens facing outside, see a couple of sconces that give off dim light. See a couple of doors that lead to a study and a kitchen. Then they fly around to check for hidden traps or anything else dangerous - they see nothing. They see no lights inside the cellar. Might see someone in the kitchen or study, but it is late. Think that if the guards can be distracted, they could get into the cellar. The two think that tonight might be a good night to go…and fly back to the inn to rally the troops.

Affy sees Fungus. She tells him what happened. Discussion about how Selbranan tried to stop Fungus from helping Shoddy Nell. Affy convinces Fungus to at least talk to Kulv. She asks Fungus about the last time he heard from his pact leader - it has been a while. Discussion then about how the moon lord might know something about Cerul.

Ridley and Relys return and he asks where Eliyena is - she went to bed. They tell what they discovered. Ridley goes to check on Eliyena; Kaizo says he is going to relieve himself and follows Ridley; Fungus is hesitant to go as Affy, Cerul, and Relys talk about going. Ridley goes into the room where Eliyena is…and sees her sit up. She says “what do you want?” He pulls out the wine bottle and offers some to her. He looks around the room for someone else and sees no one. Kaizo pees in a corner at the end of the hall when he thinks Ridley saw him. He then goes by the room to see Eliyena. She says “what again - but then says oh, it is you.” He says it is Master Kaizo and she asks if he has glasses for the wine. She asks if he is jealous that Ridley might be courting him. He says that she is creatively allergic to the truth and that she is free to go…and says that she should be gone when he comes back. She asks if this is a threat and he says yes. She says she will consider that and gives him the bottle and pushes him into the hall. He goes back to the group.

Fungus questions if we should all go. We agree to go. Party gets on the outskirts of the estate. Fungus goes on record that this is a bad idea. Ridley and Relys get rats at the sewers and speak with them. They bargain with rations and then to go to the guards at the gate and annoy them. The rats agree to anarchy. A swarm of rats go towards the guards. The guards kill the rats. We sneak in (good wise). We go to the cellar door. The little queen of the rats (Relys) casts detect magic and she senses strong conjuration magic on the other side of the cellar door. We discuss for a moment and then open the cellar door.

There are narrow stairs. We go up single file. The air is unnaturally heavy and cold. Smell stagnant water and sulfur. Come into a room with a waist-high marble basin. Water in the basin. Cerul is nauseous - it is almost smothering. He lights up his lightbringer sword as a reaction. When he does, we see the man with a fiendish winged creature that hisses on his shoulder. He says “Ridley - I had hoped we could speak as civilized men. But now that you know the truth - now that you have seen what is under my house and what I have turned to - join me to help bring the house up to its full power. You will never have to be a hired hand. Heildove would have seen us to ruin.” Ridley says “your cousin is hot and it was easy for me to help her.” He says - she is weak and I am strong. What do you hope to accomplish? Ridley says - I was asked to find out what is going on here. He then reaches for the water and starts to touch it, as Ridley hits the imp with a crossbow bolt. As the man touches the pool, he turns an icy blue and disappears. The imp remains.

IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat vs. Imp

  • ROUND ONE: Relys starry whips the imp and hits. He drops into the ground and turns into a rat and looks helpless. Affy rushes forward and swings at the rat and misses, then reaches down and picks it up, grappling it. Cerul runs forward and casts fairy fire to light up the room, but doesn’t see him. Kaizo runs into the room and goes up to the door and stands in front of it and holds his action to attack should anyone attack. Ridley walks over and says to the rat in infernal “I want you to tell me everything that is going on here.” He has speak with animals going. “Oh - he’s not here. Smell it - smell it.” RIdley - that is a portal to the 9 hells? “Join us, join us. Allow me to go get him. We will offer you something.” Only Relys understood the rat-speak. Fungus walks in and asks what’s going on? Does nothing.
  • ROUND TWO: Relys walks in and the rat says “please rescue me” to her. She says “I know what you are.” She walks to the edge of the basin and investigates the basin, where she senses the magic. It is dark and murky and smells of sulfur. The water is not of this world. She sniffs and says “that is foul.” The rat shifts into a huge hairy spider and wraps around Affy. And says “please let me go.” He releases himself from Affy and he runs. Affy tries to attack and misses. He runs away and makes it to the basin and jumps in and we are out of combat.

Cerul checks the basin. This water is from the Nine Hells. Fungus wants to capture some of this water in a vial. Affy warns him not to do it, but he does, touches the water and disappears. RIdley and Affy immediately follow. Kaizo, Relys, and Cerul pause….then Cerul follows, gritting his teeth. Relys follows. Kaizo lights his pipe, takes a deep puff, and follows.

The group is pulled through darkness, freezing cold, and slams down in a rectangular chamber. The air is so cold that it burns. There is a low moan. Blue light glows from the ice. The walls are frozen ice that include victims who were entombed while alive. At the far end of a chamber is a doorway. There is a basin in the room that is bubbling blood. There is a balcony on one side off the room, there are two doors opposite. As Ridley pops in, he sees Fungus filling a vial with blood and pop away. He pops back into the other room. Fungus appears as Kaizo is still there. Then retouches the water and pops back, followed by Kaizo. The group makes CON saves - and all make it - fighting off the cold.

Ridley starts to walk to a door, slips and falls. This is difficult terrain. Affy notices footprints that approach the balcony and more that go to the door. Ridley tries to walk to the door and falls. Cerul thinks this is the frozen layer of hell - Stygia. Cerul announces this. Affy says this is the frozen level of hell. Fungus walks to the balcony. Ridley walks to the door. The group divides between the door and balcony. Ridley gets to the door and sees another chamber that is even colder. Chained to the walls are 8 humanoids that are stripped bare and tortured. Ridley notices that the ice walls are not empty - frozen within are more bodies. To the left one of the bodies twitches…maybe this one is still alive. There is a door on the other side of this room that is open - to a field of ice. Ridley makes it halfway across the room (full speed). Affy makes her way (half speed) to the door. Relys moves to the balcony (half speed). Cerul standing by the basin with Kaizo. Fungus moves to the balcony (half speed) with Relys. Relys and Fungus can see all of Stygia in front of them. Smell of frozen metal and ancient stagnant death. The balcony is 70-80’ above the River Styx. They see glaciers, stalagmites that encapsulate creatures, stormy skies with green lightning; green insect-like creatures gliding with grace on the ice; other creatures as well moving around. They are horror-struck by what they see. Cerul walks half speed to the door. Kaizo stays at the basin.

Ridley is at the door of the other chamber, as Affy watches from a quarter of the way through the chamber. When Ridley steps to the open door he must make a CON save and he stands before a wind-swept plateau of blue and black ice. There are icy stalagmites and twisted and frozen trees everywhere. There is biting wind with a low, grinding moan. He sees to the left a passage where the steps go down a cliff that is 90’ from the door. In the distance he sees figures and shadows moving and flying toward him. Affy notices Ridley disappear. Relys and Fungus notice two imps pop down on the balcony and say “they’re here” and laugh. These imps are 15’ from them. Fungus blasts one of them with poison and hits - it acts like it was hurt, but then flies up and laughs, unharmed. Relys says to the other - who is expecting us? It says “well the master…the master is maybe not expecting, but accepts all who come….and points to the wall and says, and we will put you up there (as he points to the ceiling and those frozen in it). Relys and Fungus back into the room and toward the basin.

Ridley sees that the footprints go to the stairs. Affy asks in his head “where are you going?” He says I’m going to check something out and come right back. Fungus shouts out “he knows we are here.” Affy yells who is he…and Fungus says “the master.” Affy moves further across the room to see if she can see Ridley. Ridley is moving toward the path to try to follow the footsteps. Cerul yells “Ridley, I think we got the info we came for.” Ridley pulls out an acorn and tells a bird to fly over and look around. He thinks the bird will make it back. He stays put. Fungus moves closer to the doors to the second room. Group has quick discussion about going back through and destroying the basin on the other side.

Ridley hears from the bird that there are people advancing and that someone went into a door at the bottom of 90’ slick stairs. Ridley thinks that something is coming and is heading his way. They look like dark shadows that have no problem moving. These seem to be fiends. Ridley tells Affy he is on his way back. Once he makes it into the chamber he goes to the prisoner who seems to be alive. Ridley falls as he tries to go that way. Cerul and Affy notice shadows approaching. Kaizo sees a big hand come over the side of the balcony and start to come over. He yells “it is time to go - danger coming over the balcony.” Relys stands next to Kaizo. Everyone starts to move back to the basin. Affy sees the person twitch. She moves over to the twitching person at full speed and pulls Ridley up.

Meanwhile the creature stands at the balcony with a large spear and the imps say “I told you, I told you.” Fungus casts a spell (doomtide). Kaizo holds his action to attack if something attacks. Cerul stays put in the doorway. He hears Fungus casting a spell and sees that Ridley is going to try to unlock shackles and he notices a dark shadow at the far doorway.

– The End of the Session –

Broken Oaths & The Fiend

In-World13th of Paramor, 1246 WhereStygia PlayedJuly 02, 2026

The Chamber of Broken Oaths, Stygia, the Fifth Plan of Hell

The party is in the area of two large rooms of frozen walls. One room holding a balcony overlooking the river Styx and providing a basin of blood that magically returns one to the material plane. The other room, a prison or torture chamber made of walls of frozen bodies. A large Ice Devil and his imps have climbed through the balcony. Coming from into the other room, a Bone Devil.

IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat vs. the Fiends

  • ROUND ONE: An insect-like devil starts coming over the balcony and throws a wall of ice on the other side of the chamber, splitting the party. Cerul was caught in the middle of it and takes cold damage then pops into the room with Affy and Ridley. The devil moves to the middle of the room next to the basin - and looks around threateningly. Ridley spots a devil coming in the door of the chamber. It is skeletal with a large tail and big wings (bone devil). Ridley uses his tools to release the prisoner from his manacles. Ridley then grabs the prisoner and tries to move to the door using a cunning action to dash. He then tells his bird to fly by the devil and attack it. Fungus casts the doomstide spell in the room he’s in - targeting the 2 imps and devil, but making sure he doesn’t hit Relys or Kaizo: both imps fail their saves, but the devil saves. He then pulls out his club and casts shillelagh and moves to the side, taking damage from an opportunity attack from the devil. This attack forces him to lose his concentration and doomtide drops. Kaizo runs up and hits the devil with force and then uses stunning strike, but the devil saves. He attacks again, getting a critical hit, imposing disadvantage on the devil’s next attack. He then tries to stunning strike again and the devil saves. He then uses a bonus action to flurry of blows, missing twice. He then moves 25’ away. Rel casts a spell and is now glowing and then wild shapes into a saber, then backs up to the wall. The bone devil moves to Affy with ease. He attacks and hits once, misses once. He then tries to hit her with his tail and fumbles, losing his reaction. He then looks at Cerul and says “you do not belong here, but if you want to stay, we will let your friends go” in his head. Cerul laughs and says “you are right, we don’t belong here.” Affy attacks and hits critically, and tries to use her sword to inflict additional fire damage (which does nothing). She then gets an extra hit from hew and does. Then takes her second attack and hits. She stays put. Both imps offer Fungus and Kaizo a “deal” to live, but both decline, and the imps fly away/disappear. Cerul sees shadows in the distance, but doesn’t see additional threats approaching. He casts bless on Ridley, Cerul, and Affy and heals himself. Then moves away from the wall.
  • ROUND TWO: The insect devil moves forward closer to Rel and Kaizo. He says “I hate cats” to Rel, then says, “but more than cats, I hate elves” and attacks Kaizo, hitting twice. He takes damage and he cannot take a bonus action or a reaction and his speed is reduced. He then attacks Rel with his tail and hits. He then says telepathically to Fungus “I guess there are no deals to be made.” Ridley pulls out his crossbow and hits. He then tells his bird to attack and it does, hitting again. Then Ridley and the prisoner move toward the door (that is iced over). Fungus charges at the insect-devil and tries to hit with his club and, feeling lucky, does, knocking him prone. Then misty steps away, trying to inflict extra damage on the devil, but it misses. Kaizo attacks with advantage on the prone devil and hits, inflicting force damage. The uses a point to try to stun him, but he saves. He then hits again, more force damage. He then tried to stun again and it works, dropping the wall of ice. He then moves away. Rel attacks and misses twice and then moves to the corner. The bone devil attacks Ridley with disadvantage, giving Affy an opportunity attack that hits, and Cerul fires a warding flare as he tries to attack Ridley, fumbling, falls down, but stands up. He then attacks Affy with his third attack and hits, damaging and poisoning her (imposing disadvantage and she cannot be healed). Affy attacks and hits with the first attack, then misses. She uses action surge to attack again and misses twice. Cerul attacks with radiant damage kills him. He then heals himself.
  • ROUND THREE: The insect devil stands up, smelling blood in the air, and looks into the other room (he can’t do anything because he is stunned). Ridley shoots him from the other room, hitting and inflicting damage, pushing him 10’ away. He then moves with the prisoner into the other room. He then shoots the devil again and hits. The bird then flies in and crits on the devil. Fungus runs over to the devil and attacks with his club and misses. Fungus then teleports and gives the queen of rats 7 temp hp (over the 4 she had). Kai goes up and punches and hits and stuns him. Rel hits the devil and then notices an imp next to her. She moves away from the devil and imp to the other side of the room and slips and falls. Affy uses her psi-leap to fly 60’ into the other room, then attacks. An imp appears and tries to hit her as she flies by, but misses. She misses the devil, but then hits and does damage and extra acid damage. One imp attacks Cerul and he imposes a warding flare, causing the imp fumble. One imp attacks Ridley and hits. One attacks Kaizo and hits. One attacks Rel and misses. Cerul attacks the imp on him and hits, dealing radiant damage.
  • ROUND FOUR: The bone devil is stunned and does nothing. Ridley shoots the imp on Cerul and hits, killing it. He then shoots the imp on Kaizo and hits, imposing damage. He then flies the bird over to attack that same imp and it hits, killing it. The bird then flies closer to the doors. Fungus moves over and attacks the devil and misses, but stays put. Kaizo moves forward carefully to attack the devil and hits, dealing force damage. He then tries to stun him and the devil saves. He then hits again, more force damage. Then he uses a flurry of blows and hits. Rel attacks the devil, hitting critically, inflicting a lot of radiant damage and lightning damage. She then attacks again and hits, doing more radiant damage. Affy then attacks with her sword and hits, killing him “stay in hell beast.” She then moves to the imp on Rel and attacks and hits. The imps blink and run, but Relys gets an opportunity attack because she has blindsight. She hits it and kills it. The other imp got away.

The fight ends.

Ridley comments that we need to go after the noble that we followed in, Urovil. We talk for a minute and heal up. Affy uses her Second Wind ability (x2) as Cerul casts Mass Healing Word on the group and then a Cure Wounds on Kaizo. Cerul notices shadows moving outside.

The party starts to move to the far door, ready to find Urovil. Ridley says he will leave since he knows which way he went. We talk about how “someone” is making deals from hell with people on the material plane. We think these folks frozen in the walls are ones who broke their contracts on the material plane who were forced into hell to pay the consequence. We realize that the water from the River Styx on the material plane will bring people to hell, whereas the blood on this plane in that basin will take people back.

As we start to move, we realize that Rel glowing is going to attract attackers, as well as Cerul’s light on his sword. They both kill their lights. We move carefully so we don’t fall. Affy asks Ridley about his bird - he says he “tapped into the weave” to summon that beast. Affy thanks the bird for its help. Ridley tells her the bird’s name is Ada - which is coincidentally the name of Cerul’s mother and he tells us that. We follow a path that Ridley scouted earlier and come to a door.

At the door, an imp pops up. “Oh - are you here to make a deal?” Affy says “is Urovil in there - if so, maybe…and get the fuck out of the way.” And the imp pops away. Ridley listens at the door and hears Urovil’s voice, speaking in infernal “I’m sure they are coming for me, I’m sure.” And then a response “You…let’s see how you handle them.” Ridley then knocks on the door and the double door opens slowly. The doors start to close, but Affy jumps through, and the door closes her inside alone with creatures. Affy sees that the room opens up to a balcony that is over the River Styx.

Ridley moves forward and casts Hunter’s Mark on Urovil, then throws his dagger and hits. He then draws this crossbow and shoots and hits. Ridley sees the shadowy form at the end of the dock disappear. Ridley then hits again from the nick property of his weapon. The group hears something behind Fungus. This, this is what caused the shadow…A hulking, nightmarish figure— a grotesque parody of nobility wrapped in tattered finery appears. His mottled blue-green skin pulls tight over a skeletal face, lips peeled back in a perpetual, jagged grin full of razor teeth that gleam like wet bone. Glowing red eyes sweep the room and settle on Urovil. One clawed hand grips a tall, ornate staff topped with a sinister finial, while the other rests lightly on his chest like he's savoring the moment. The other reaches out towards Urovil. The skull in Urovil’s head crushes to ash. Urovil’s eyes go blank and he falls to the ground. The devil looks to the group, “Take this weakling and leave my realm or suffer my wrath.” Fungus thinks this devil is likely Urovil’s patron, holder of this lair and in command of everything else we fought so far. He tells everyone “he is dangerous - are we fighting or running?” and then goes invisible. The devil looks around and glances at Cerul. Cerul says “RIdley - you got what you came for - can we go?” Ridley says yes - I just thought you would hate devils. Cerul says, “but they are in their domain - they can stay here.” Cerul looks at Urovil…and then sees that the skull that this devil is not holding is now Urovil’s skull covered in blood. I’d like to leave with him.” The devil says “that is not happening.” Fungus then moves back to Ridley and picks him up and starts to move away with his body, shouting “we have to go, we have to go, we have to go.” Cerul says “we are leaving and Ridley is coming with us and that’s that” and heals Ridley for 19 points and then advances forward 15’. She says no and moves forward. She doesn’t persuade him. Ridley is in Fungus’ arms and shoots his crossbow at the devil, but it hits Fungus, as the devil charms Ridley as he is about to shoot. He looks at Cerul and says which one are you leaving me? And he says “Urovil” And the devil says “I already have his soul.” Fungus goes large form and picks up Rel and action dashes forward, slipping and falling, standing back up and moving forward again. In front of Kaizo, an ice shard shoots up in front of him. Kaizo moves back to Fungus and asks for the flask of blood and puts a drop on Fungus and Rel (and they both disappear). He then moves to Affy and hands her the flask and there is a drop coming out that hits Kaizo and he leaves. Rel has to make a death save.
  • ROUND FIVE: Affy walks over to Ridley and says Kaizo says to take this. Then asks the devil “why do you need someone.” And he says after all the trouble you all have caused, I’m owed. And she says “take me.” and a spike shoots up from the ground and knocks her to 0 hit points, but she doesn’t fall (relentless endurance). Ridley takes the vial and pours it on Affy and Urovil and they disappear. He then tries to shatter the vial on Cerul, but it crashes to the ground. He then walks next to Cerul, but doesn’t touch the blood yet. Both Cerul and Ridley have to make wisdom saving throws. Cerul saves, Ridley does not, taking necrotic damage and is blinded. Fungus is in the cellar of the noble house. He sees the prisoner, he sees Relys, Kaizo, and Affy. He takes the healing potion out of Rel’s pack and feeds it to her. Cerul grabs Ridley’s arm and pulls him to the ground then pulls his arm on the ground. Ridley hears the fiend telepathically say “I will remember you - this is not the end” and they both disappear.

We are all in the cellar, sometime after midnight on the morning of the 14th of Paramor, 1246.

– The End of the Session –

Adore Is Up In Smoke

In-World17th of Paramor, 1246 WhereCaelora PlayedAugust 6, 2026

We head out on the road. As we ride, we cross a river and are talking about our experiences. Kaizo wonders what we are going to run into as we get close to the Southern Arch. He’s concerned what he is going to find among the monks. He knows there is corruption and is worried about what is causing it. He knows there is no balance in lands right now.
