He will worry less when we know where it is and deal with it. Relys is also thinking about this “monster” and is looking forward to putting this to rest. This is the reason she is adventuring right now. We aren’t sure where the “bad one” is - we heard it was up north, but then got word there was something going on in Murfield. Ridley is thinking about buying a house and a lodge to retire to. Affy is concerned about selling her soul to the devil - and the deal that Kulv offered to maybe “fix” this. She wants to find her dad, but is more concerned about her soul right now. Kaizo also comments that he is missing his pipe.

Fungus finds out his ring is a Ring of Spell Storing with the fly spell inside.

We know that Pa got a headstart and said he was not going to the Southern Arch without us. Kaizo remembers a quaint campsite in the area named Rustlight - named so for a rusty lantern. As we travel, we know it is getting cooler - especially at night. The sun starts to set after a long day’s travels. We are looking for the campsite. We eventually see a clearing with a large rusted lantern. Affy looks and sees wagon marks, but doesn’t see signs of Pa. Campsite smells of pine needles and earth. We find a spot to settle. Affy looks around for Kulv - doesn’t see him either. She asks Relys if she sees him. She doesn’t either. In fact, there is no one here yet - it is early in the night.

We set up camp. Fungus sings. Kaizo asks Fungus to retrieve a high limb for the campfire (he uses a cantrip). Relys forages for herself, but everyone else eats a ration. Kaizo climbs up on top of the wagon to meditate. Cerul and Fungus discuss spells, his shield, etc. Affy sits by the campfire. We see a half dozen people coming down the path - all dressed alike. They are dressed in loose, bulky clothing to keep warm. They each have packs, except one guy who has a really big backpack. They are all elves. They look our direction and give us a nod, but then go set up their own camp. Fungus goes over to introduce himself - Cerul tags along. Fungus tells him his name and asks who they are and their names and where they are going. They reveal they are going to Cealora and he offers to tell them about it. He asks what they are cooking tonight and offers to help them. Fungus and Cerul notice they are monks and Cerul thinks they are from Adore. Cerul tells them that we are traveling with a monk, too - he is meditating on the top of the carriage. He thinks Kaizo is from Adore. Fungus comments that we have heard there is trouble that way. Cerul notices that the big pack was placed in a protective place. Cerul tells them about the rumors of trouble at the Southern Arch - they claim they are not familiar with the troubles. Cerul then guesses they are from Adore and they say “yes, yes we are from Adore.” He then tells them about disturbances in the forest and they said they don’t know much about it, but they get a sense that these monks are not forthcoming (one guy nudges another to shut up as he starts to speak).

Meanwhile, Ridley is tending to his horse: Relys goes to the edge of the forest to pray, holding her stone. Affy sits by the fire. Kaizo is still meditating. Fungus takes a bowl of food to Kaizo and wakes him up (Fungus eats the food). They tell him that they are from Adore and are not full of information.

Kaizo walks over wearing a coat he got from Fungus and says hello. Kaizo asks where they are from - they answer from the monastery at Adore. Lots of intros and bowing. Kaizo mentions Master Lang. Then asks why they are leaving Adore. They reply “to stretch their legs.” And Kaizo challenges that - but they are not intimidated. Affy picks up what is going on and walks over. Kaizo tries to survey the monks to see who might be the weakest link - he has a hard time picking one, but decides to pick out the cook (he thinks he is the most junior). Kaizo tries to talk to him more about where he is from. That guy wants to walk around the campsite with him. Ridley goes into stealth to try to sneak away, but knows that they probably took inventory of who was in the camp when they arrived. Kaizo doesn’t feel threatened - just feels like they are being cautious.

Kaizo walks with the cook - asks him lots of questions. The guy dances around answers, but he finally asks how Kaizo heard there was trouble at Adore. Kaizo says he picked it up on the road. He asks if there is any truth to it? And the monk asks if Kaizo has good intentions. Kaizo affirms this and the guy reveals that there is trouble there. They were sent to Caelora by Elandri Avunahorn, the spiritual leader there. Kaizo knows this person - he knew her back when. Kaizo asks how she is doing and he is told she is not doing well. Well, she is doing well, but the other leaders not so much. He says that half of the monks are acting out of character, but he cannot disclose more of his mission. He doesn’t know much more than that, but people are distancing themselves from each other, dividing into two clear groups. Kaizo asks for advice, and the monks tell him to pass Adore by. Kaizo presses for more information, and the monk says he fears for the monastery’s downfall, making Kaizo promise to keep it to himself.

The chef monk walks over to Relys and asks what she found foraging, and if it could add flavor to his stew. She explains the herbs she found, and offers them to him.

Ridley positions himself in a tree to watch everything - as a lookout guard if needed.

Affy asks them about if they are fearful and comments how they have no weapons. They comment that they “are protected.” Affy comments how they don’t have any big guys like me and Fungus - and how that pack looks really big for them. Fungus asks them what they have in it and they don’t answer, but instead ask him what is in his pack. He spends a long time revealing his contents.

After a while, Kaizo spends some time bidding the monks goodnight. We decide to take watch. Fungus comments that Ridley is always hiding. Affy says he is watching - no worries. She offers to take the first watch with Ridley. Then Relys and Cerul on second watch. Then Fungus and Kaizo last watch.

First watch - the stew the monks made smells very good - very enticing. Affy communicates with Ridley telepathically here and there, but otherwise they both just stand watch. Nothing happens and they eventually wake up the other two and then go to sleep.

Relys shares with Cerul that his grandmother’s spirit is in her stone and asks Cerul to take a look at it. He thinks initially that there is dark, necromantic energy associated with the stone. His insight tells him that there is a connection in the stone with a supernatural power. But the stone is not dark. But he is concerned that it could manifest itself in a negative way - that something could lash out. He feels the pain coming from it, but can’t sense much more than that. He is disturbed and doesn’t know if someone who is exposed to this stone long term might corrupt the holder. They discuss the motivations Relys has - that putting her family to rest will be good, but she shouldn’t hold those emotions too tightly. That justice is better than revenge. Cerul just comments that there is a fine line that she shouldn’t cross. They notice the monks have a watch, too. Fungus is asleep with the monks and Kaizo is meditating with them.

Cerul and Relys finish watch and wake up Fungus and Kaizo. As they pass the carriage they hear some strange noises coming from the carriage, but don’t investigate. They then go back into the carriage to go back to sleep.

Fungus scrapes the remains of the stew from the pot. Kaizo shares his mead. Fungus summons Tannana - he realizes that he can look through her eyes. He gets Tanana to look through their big pack. She finds that there are lots of scrolls inside. Fungus tells Kaizo and he wonders why they are taking them. He thinks they are likely the most valuable scrolls from Adore.

In the carriage, the others sleep, but Affy feels very cold and feels black water rising. As she opens her eyes, she sees the devil. She sees her friends being pulled away from her in chains. She shakes her head, knowing she couldn’t do anything. The devil leans in and says “I have yours, until you can provide a soul more powerful.” She feels the grip of chains of pulling her in…as she wakes up with the cold and black water clinging to her thoughts. Then she feels something warm on her breasts. She pulls out the parchment that was put there. There is writing in a dark language, but it resolves as she looks at it. Affy tells them she has this paper, but only Ridley and Affy are the only two who can read it. This is what it says:

This is a contract from the devil.

By the cold waters of Stygia and the authority vested in me,

I already hold claim upon the soul of Az’efal Fairhand.

In recognition of this claim, and in the interest of mutual advancement, the following terms are offered:

Should the undersigned locate and freely present to me a soul of greater utility, value, or potential than her own, I shall release her from the existing claim and, in addition, grant her such knowledge and power as befits the exchange.

This offer remains open until accepted or until I choose to collect what is already mine.

To bind these terms, the undersigned shall sign below in her own blood.

— Ducrurith, of the Styx

She tells Cerul and Relys. Fungus and Kaizo walk in and hear some of this. Discussion about this. The thought is that Affy will have to try to trick someone else to offer their soul, and that maybe the devil might have someone in mind who he has a mission for. Cerul thinks this is bad business. Affy decides to hold off for a little while. Ridley thinks that maybe if we were to kill the devil the contract would be voided. Fungus tells her to sign it and let’s move on - tells her to think of him as a “patron.” The group decides to go. Fungus yells “take care of your scrolls” to the monks as we pack up to leave (they look around nervously).

We pack up. Fungus drives the carriage and Kaizo and Affy sit inside. Cerul walks next to the carriage. Relys and Ridley ride horses.

The group travels and smells wood burning and ash. We see Adore on the horizon covered in flames and smoke. We continue forward and try to rush. We want to see if there are survivors. Marching order: Kaizo dashes with Ridley, Relys shifts into a bird to scout. Fungus next. Then Affy and Cerul in the back.

Kaizo wonders if he messed up - if those monks were legit.

The temple is blackened. Smoke rolls across the grounds (dim light inside of the smoke ring). There are shapes forming ahead who see us and IT IS INITIATIVE - Combat vs Corrupted Monks

  • ROUND ONE: We see emerging from the smoke some creatures. Ridley runs over to the nearest monk casts hunters mark, and stabs it with his dagger, hitting. He then attacks again, missing. Then fires his crossbow and hits. Kaizo moves up into the bush and pops out to attack a monk. He misses twice. Then tries his flurry of blows and misses twice. He then jumps inside the bush (half coverage). Affy dashes forward with a bonus action and then attacks, fumbling, losing her reaction. She then attacks again and hits. The monk attacks Ridley and hits twice. Another monk runs up and attacks Affy and misses twice. Another monk runs up to Fungus and attacks and hits. Then attacks and crits, and Fungus loses half his hit points after the crit damage. Another monk runs forward to try to punch Kaizo and hits, stunning him. The second attack misses. Cerul casts spirit guardian. Fungus moves away (giving the monk an opportunity attack that hits, imposing damage - as Fungus imposes damage from that hit) and then misty steps near the bush where Kaizo is (and gives another one an opportunity attack which hits, but also imposes damage back). Suddenly another wave of monks crashes out of the smoke and runs forward. One attacks Ridley and hits (and he drops hunters mark) then attacks again and misses. One runs forward to attack Affy and misses twice. Another runs forward to hit Fungus and does (but also takes damage). The second attack hits as well. Another one tries to attack Kaizo (who imposes disadvantage) and misses twice. Relys casts a spell and is now glowing and flies into the smoke. Suddenly another two monks join the fight. One attacks Ridley and hits. Another one attacks Fungus and hits.
  • ROUND TWO: Ridley hits one of the monks and hits once, misses twice. He disengages and moves away toward Cerul. Kaizo is stunned, but comes out of it at the end of his turn. Affy attacks one and crits, killing him. She then attacks another and hits twice, inflicting some decent damage. She stays put telling them “you guys missed the first time - you should stay here and try again.” One of the monks tries to move out of Cerul’s spirit guardians and runs to attack Affy and misses twice. Another tries to attack her and misses twice. Another monk attacks Kaizo and misses twice. That one disengages and moves away from the bush. The others disengage and move away from Affy. Cerul runs up, moving his spirit guardians, and four of the monks have to make WIS saving throws. He then casts radiance of the dawn and they must make CON saving throws. Then he uses his bonus action and heals Ridley. Fungus goes invisible and then drinks a healing potion. One disengages and runs away, but Affy swings, misses, and it still takes damage. He then attacks Ridley and misses twice. Another one runs to Ridley and attacks and hits Ridley, despite Cerul’s warding flare. Then moves away from Ridley. One attacks Kaizo and misses, but then hits. He makes a save, but still takes damage. Another one attacks Kaizo and misses twice. Relys flies back from the smoke and to the bush and transitions into a scorpion from her owl position. Then attacks one and hits it, grapples it and inflicts damage from her moonlight, then more damage from her elemental fury. Then attacks the other one and hits it and he takes radiant damage and is grappled. Then uses her tail to attack the one on the left and inflicts piercing damage and poison damage, plus radiant damage. A monk close to Relys attacks and misses twice and disengages. Then another moves in to attack Cerul and misses twice and misses twice.
  • ROUND THREE: Ridley attacks and hits, then moves and hits again. Kaizo moves out of the bush and one gets an opportunity attack with disadvantage, and misses. Kaizo then attacks one and hits. Kaizo then attacks again and fumbles and falls prone, but stands up again. Then tries flurry of blows and hits, but then misses. Affy moves up and attacks one and misses, but then hits 3 times, using an action surge, and kills him. One monk enters Cerul’s spirit guardians circle and takes radiant damage. It then attacks Cerul and hits, inflicting damage and it then disengages and moves away. One moves up to attack Kaizo and misses twice then disengages and moves away. Cerul casts radiance of the dawn again and can go after four monks. He then moves forward and his spirit guardians hit both. Fungus attacks a monk with poison spray and crits, killing it. He then uses a bonus action to heal himself. One disengages and moves and attacks Ridley and hits twice. One then attacks Cerul and misses. Another moves up to attack Relys and misses. Relys tries to squeeze the one in her claw and misses. She then attacks with her tail and misses. Then uses the other claw and hits, imposing radiant and lightning damage. The other monks attack. One attacks Kaizo with disadvantage and misses twice and disengages. One moves up and attacks Ridley and hits twice. Then it moves into the smoke as it disengages.
  • ROUND FOUR: Ridley attacks the one on him and misses, then hits, then crits on his last hit. Kaizo attacks the monk in the claw and misses once, then hits. Then uses flurry of blows and misses, then hits and kills the monk. Affy attacks one and kills him, then attacks another and kills it. Two monks run away. Cerul moves forward and hits one with spirit of guardians and it takes damage, killing it. Fungus casts poison spray on the remaining monk and crits, killing her, melting her skin.

The smoke starts to drift away and a figure steps out into view. He has a staff in one hand and he raises up another hand. He is covered in soot and blood and he steps forward. His face comes into view. This is the prisoner from the wagon, Rueh’unar Heildove. He says “Peace - I am not your enemy here.”

– The End of the Session –

Two Heads Are Not Better Than One

In-World18th of Paramor, 1246 WhereRuins of Adore PlayedAugust 21, 2026

Standing in the smoky ruins of Adore

Seeing the wizard approach, Affy asks Rueh’unar, “what are you doing here.” He replied that he heard what was going down here and had concerns and got here as quickly as I could. He asked about the people were escorting and we reply that we let them go ahead - and have plans to meet back up. He replies that we need to take stock of what is left. Ridley tries to determine if he is telling the truth and is confused. He thinks that Heildove might be justified being here and is likely honest. Kaizo speaks up and offers some thoughts about being a storm or a mountain when problems come. Heildove thinks we need to rummage around to see who or what is left.

Fungus speaks up and is confused - we saw you in a cage last time and Ridley was supposed to kill you. Ridley has never seen Heildove dressed like this - he appears to be well equipped as an adventurer. He notices a large folded satchel with one fastener on it that looks like a 3-pointed/4-green gem brooch.

Heildove found the coffers. Fungus walks over to shake his hand. Affy speaks up that the family in Caelora is no longer going to be a threat. Ridley jumps in to say that he is no longer hunting him. Ridley tells him that the family has other concerns. Affy mentions that Urovil is dead and Fungus adds that they are concerned with hell.

Heildove says he doesn’t trust just anyone with this gold. Kaizo pipes up with another analogy about crossing a river - swimming or being wise and learning where the stones are. He says there are valuable items inside, but nothing he wants. Affy says “you mean, you’ve taken what you wanted already.” And he replies that he has taken nothing. He says there are things inside that need to find the way back to the monks.

This area is very ruined, burned and smoke damaged. There are dead bodies. Fungus thinks there are no additional threats inside. Affy asks exactly how it is that he arrived here and at just the right time. She intimidates him. He thanks her for revealing that the Caelora threat is no longer. He mentions that his estranged brother - another Heildove - had business in Adore, which is why he is there. As Relys looks around, she notices some Rel’Udonima tattoos on some of the dead. She finds a tree limb with a bronze bell (and thinks it has some value-10gp). Fungus finds two clay jars with a familiar red liquid (2 potions of greater healing). Affy presses further about his brother. He says he found out that the King’s Outlanders had stopped the RU at the Abbey of LIght and Qal’dynn…so he pushed out to Adore with his RU influence. She asks if he saw his brother and he said he came out when he heard the last of our fight. Ridley asks how he was able to hold his own inside and he said that not all the monks were turned yet.

Cerul walks in and notices that some areas were preserved - or at least someone took some care to try to save an area…places where there are water. Cerul comments that we might need to look for an underground area and Affy agrees to go help him. Ridley asks Heildove to show him the coffers. There is an intact chest. He tells Kaizo that he would be the best equipped to make sure it gets into the right hands. Heildove uses his staff to levitate the chest and sets it in front of Kaizo. He then uses a mage hand to help open it. There is a huge pile of gold. Ridley thinks this is likely 5000gp.

As Affy and Cerul walk around, Affy sees a set of seven smooth river stones etched with symbols of Fralee that are valuable (used for meditation) - 15gp. Cerul notices a small bronze sculpture that has intertwined vines. It looks like a dryad holding a bowl. This looks valuable - he asks Kaizo what this could be. It looks like it could be a statue of Fralee. Kaizo thinks it is her. Kaizo then comments about religion - how it is not just following a god, but living like one. As Cerul picks up the bowl, it fills with water (50gp). Kaizo tastes it - it is good, clean and fresh. This is a magical bowl.

Relys casts detect magic as a spell and sees a few things: Heildove’s staff and something on his hand, the bowl with water (conjuration), the healing potions, and she sees a fallen tree to the right and ruins of a large chamber and senses divination magic coming from that direction. She heads in that direction. Ridley catches up to her and comments “no more scorpion” and she comments that she needed to cast spells.

Affy and Cerul are looking for underground passages and just find water springs, but don’t seem to find anything. Affy yells to the group to look around as well. Ridley finds a small metallic box; Fungus finds another small square locked case. Fungus tries to open the lock and finds that it is a container of salt (4 containers). Ridley finds calligraphy tools in the box. Fungus thinks this salt is likely used for alchemy. He thinks they might be valuable and rare (10gp). Relys finds lots of symbols of Fralee in the chamber she enters. She sees a body of a woman crushed by a limb, but thinks she was killed from something else. She sees on a table in the center of a room something that looks like a nice piece of ashwood, with dark etchings with elven script. She reads and thinks this is a Ouiji board (at least a Myrdaen version).

Affy goes off behind a bush to do what she needs to do. Fungus goes and conjures bananas to eat while walking around. The old man wizard is going back to rest. Kaizo is going to put the bodies to rest and clean the place up and do the best to address the situation, Relys and Ridley help him. Fungus with his bananas goes and calls out for Pa and then tries to search for Pa. While looking for tracks, trips on a banana (natural 1) and ruins any chances of finding tracks. Fungus is a bit distraught and begs the party to see if they can use magic to locate Pa. Relys makes the astute observation that Pa was at least a day ahead. The wizard feels pretty used and is going to sleep. Affy comes out from behind the bushes and goes to speak with the wizard.

Relys looks around for any signs of life - of vegetation or animals. She finds a cat's tail plant in the water in the central area. She speaks with plants. Hello - how are you? I’m glad you made out unscathed. The plant said it was scary. Relys asks what it saw. The plant asks how far back she wants it to recall. She says that she will tell her everything. She saw monks walking around and wee folk arrived. The monks welcomed the wee folk and told them to stay there. It was late and they went to sleep. The plant watched the moon - especially the blue one. Then early in the morning the horse arrived. It was still dark. The horse had a rider, but the horse was burning (the rider was not). The horse came through the sky burning and then stopped burning and landed. A guy stepped off of the horse and didn’t tie him off. The horse sniffed the water and didn’t drink. The man went into that building (and points to a tree). The plant heard yelling and then the rider came out and got his horse and when we rode away and the horse lit up and he flew off. And then the wee folk came out and the little ones were crying - they weren’t happy. And the father one said they needed to leave so they did. The plant then said that later that day, before the sun was straight up, people started arguing with each other. Another guy came out of that tree and started splashing oil on everything and set everything on fire. And then there was fighting and smoke and fire and then you guys showed up. But that other guy showed up first and made fountains of water everywhere. Relys asks if anyone else fled the area. It responds that no one did - they all fought. Relys thanks it for telling the story. She then tells the group the story and Affy asks what the person looked like who set the fire. Relys asks this to the plant and he says that the firestarter was a monk leader. And that the guy on the horse had on a mask. He was darkly robed, masked, and hooded. She decides to cast plant growth and decides to spend the night here, enriching the plants. She is hoping the vines will help “bury the dead.”

The rest of the group follows Heildove back to a clearing he had found for a campsite. Cerul wants to use the pool onsite to scry on Pa to make sure they are okay. He knows that the pools are sacred to the elves. They are rumored to have something to do with the elves crossover. They are called daefar pools. He feels confident they can be used for scrying. Kaizo speaks some wisdom about this to Cerul and he proceeds. Cerul sees Pa and he sees him. Pa says something is off. He is handing off his horse to someone - likely a stable person. He hears flowing water close by. He sees firelight shining off of a building and he sees a nice rock structure and empty docks next to a big body of water. Things are dimly lit from one of the moons. He sees Pa paying someone to take care of the horse. And Pa puts him arm around his wife and asks about their children - the wife replies they are inside and sitting at a table, preparing to eat. Cerul isn’t sure where they are - he’s never been there. Ridley asks how Cerul can see them and if there was a way someone could avoid scrying. Cerul tells him that this is the “power of the light” and there are ways to avoid it, but that you will get a funny feeling if someone ever scrys on him. Cerul thinks they are at the Southern Arch. Cerul decides to send Pa a message to ask them to stay at the Southern Arch until we arrive. “Pa we are in Adore. Glad to see you are safe. We will catch up with you tomorrow.” Pa says ‘ What - how do you know I’m safe. That Adore place was creepy. Did you get that same vibe? See you tomorrow.”

We make camp with watches. Fungus comments that he misses the magical cube. Heildove has a fire going and a tent set up. It also looks like he has rabbits roasting. Affy sits down and tells him what Relys heard. He tells her that must have been his brother. He has some nefarious ways to be sure. He thinks that one step back just pushes his brother harder. He comments that we are just at the “edge of Halesworth” and maybe he is eyeing new territory. He says he was hoping that the RU were pressed back. But is appears that they are trying something anew. Affy confides that we are the King’s Outlanders. He pulls out a bottle of wine and says “I am in great company.” And then pulls out a box from his pack and opens it and it has a collection of brass goblets and passes them out with wine. I had no idea based upon…and looks at Ridley…well, I just had no idea. Fungus says he will gladly tell stories of our travels. And proceeds to tell him stuff. Fungus comments how Heildove’s bag looks “handy” - it is quite a haversack. Fungus tries to tell everything. Ridley watches with interest - knowing that Heildove is known for spellcasting. The vibe is this guy is younger version of Lorien. Is all about himself. A typical wizard. Affy then asks him about the brooch on his bag. He comments that it is a “hey if you know me I’m here to do business” symbol. This is a symbol of the Promissory. Ridley has heard that they are a group who hires people to acquire goods and artifacts. Fungus uses baby oil on his feet and Heildove asks what it is for. Affy asks if there are others he works for. He says he worked for the family in Caelora, but that is no more. He then asks if Ridley wants to work for him, but he says he is talking more in jest. But says the Promissory is always looking for people to help find items. Ridley asks him to let his group know he is open for business - as he is always looking for coin. Fungus asks if they can speak in private and they get up to walk away, as Heildove hands the others another bottle of wine.

As they walk away, Fungus asks him about a scroll that is powerful that he wants to sell, but isn’t sure where or how to sell it. It is a scroll of imprisonment (level 9). Fungus says this will get him into trouble. Heildove offers him 5000gp for the scroll and says he will pay him when they get back to camp. They walk back.

While they are gone, Cerul, Ridley and Affy discuss future plans. Ridley then comments about having our souls sold to the entity under the Blackstone Crucible. As they return, they hear us talking about soul selling. Heildove starts to take gold out of his pack to pay Fungus and we question what is going on. Cerul pipes up to remind Fungus about why the group needs to scroll. Fungus tells him that we need the scroll and Heildove puts the gold back in his pack.

When they come back, Affy tells him she knows a little of his group and asks if he knows of a Thornage in their service. There are a lot of people in the world I live and work with. He is dodging her question. Would it be enough to say yes or will there be follow up questions. She tells him it is important. He says he doesn’t really know him. He mentions both of his first names and says that he has quite a reputation. He is known for getting what he wants. There is a tale that he found a woman of strange blood, but he desired her so deeply and stole her heart. But it is just a tale, right? And she comments that she has heard the same tale. And with that, he is ready for sleep.

We all decide to sleep. Ridley says he will cast the alarm spell. Kaizo and Relys are together and Kaizo keeps watch over her. Ridley then tells Heildove about his apprentice - he is curious how she is doing. Ridley tells her about capturing her and taking her to Caelora, then about Kaizo talking to her, etc. Ridley thinks that Heildove looks disturbed by the conversation. He questions why she took Ridley along for the ride. Time to rest. Affy asks if he is going to cast a spell as well. And he does - and steps into his dome of safety.

It’s the next day, the 19th of Paramore, 1246

Near the end of the 8 hours, the spell goes off and Ridley wakes up with his weapon drawn - and it is just Relys and Kaizo. Relys has one level of exhaustion. We can’t see from here the work that Relys did all night (we stayed a little far away and upwind). Heildove comes out of his dome and offers everyone tea. He says he will be heading in a different direction and offers us safe travels. Affy asks if he has words of advice should we encounter his brother. He says that surely we could let him know. But then she counters with words to find out if he would like to see him alive or dead. He said he would understand no matter the condition. Heildove says he likes Fungus’ bulas and pets him and heads out.

The group packs up, Relys sleeps on the carriage, and Cerul rides Relys’ horse. Affy, Relys, and Kaizo ride in the carriage. After a little while, we see some snow flurries start to fall. Cerul, Ridley, and Fungus are riding outside. Ridley hears a loud tree snap and break ahead. Cerul and Fungus didn’t notice it - they were talking. Ridley offers to ride ahead to scout. Ridley rides ahead (15 stealth). He comes around a bend and he sees a tree crack off the edge of the road and fall to the side and hears something he doesn’t understand (but thinks it is giant). He comes back to the group and tells everyone. We agree to proceed.

Cerul, Ridley and Affy advance. We hear the giants talking about “smoke and eating” and know they are talking about going to Adore. Cerul uses a booming voice to tell them “friends ahead.” The giant laughs and says “we will eat raw” as he sees us.

IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat versus Ravenous Ettins

  • ROUND ONE: Ridley casts hunters mark, then attacks with his bow. He hits and does piercing and force damage, pushing him back 10’. Then attacks again and misses. Fungus stirs up the folks in the wagon (Kaizo and Relys) and then flies up and ahead and grows larger. Cerul knows these are Ettins. Cerul sprouts wings and flies up 40’. Then casts scorching ray on one of the creatures - two hits, one misses. The first ettin looks over and says “let’s get the fat one” and he runs forward and grabs a branch off a tree and launches it at Fungus, as well as a rock on the ground. Fungus uses lucky. He fumbles the branch, hitting himself with it and doing some great damage. The rock is aimed at Cerul, who casts warding flare and the rocks misses. Kaizo gets out of the carriage and bonus action dashes ahead, casting longstrider. He uses a point to dash ahead and makes it to an ettin. The other ettin then attacks Kaizo - throwing a rock at him. He blocks most of the damage and ends up taking 6 points of damage. He then throws a rock at Cerul and misses. Relys stands up in the carriage and opens the front door of the carriage and casts moonbeam on it. He saves and takes half damage. The last ettin moves away from Kaizo and runs up to attack Affy. He misses with the first attack and then hits with his second attack, imposing disadvantage on her next attack. Affy attacks and fumbles, falling prone. She stands back up and attacks again and hits, imposing disadvantage on him.
  • ROUND TWO: RIdley shoots an arrow at the one he marked and hits, pushing him 10’ out. He then dashes on the horse and shoots again and hits again and pushes him back into the moonbeam. He saves, but still takes more radiant damage. Fungus flies even higher (now 100’ up) and then blasts an ettin with poison spray and hits, imposing significant acid damage. He then knocks him prone. Cerul is 30’ up and flies down and attacks the one on the ground with his sword and hits. He uses a spell in the sword and the guy saves, so takes no damage. That one then stands up and moves away from Cerul (no opportunity attack) and runs over to grapple Ridley and he sticks him in his mouth and bites him. He then walks over to a rock with Ridley in his hand/mouth, leaving the horse behind. Kaizo moves to the closest ettin and attacks- it is critical hit. The ettin drops the battle axe and breaks. Kaizo uses a stunning strike and stuns him, in addition to inflicting 29 points of damage (using 2 points). Then he punches him again - another critical hit. He uses another point to inflict another 29 points of damage. He then uses another point and flurry of blows and hits twice, inflicting another 18 points of damage and poisoning him. That ettin is stunned. Relys gets out of the cart and moves forward. She moves the moonbeam to the stunned creature and it takes half damage. The last ettin leaves Affy and runs to Kaizo and fumbles with his disadvantage and falls prone and stands up and attacks again, but Kaizo imposes lucky and the ettin misses. Affy dashes forward and hits, then hits again and uses a charge on her weapon to impose additional fire damage and disadvantage on his next roll.
  • ROUND THREE: Ridley uses steady aim and attacks the ettin and hits and pushes him back. Then shoots him again and misses, using his heroic inspiration to avoid a fumble. Fungus flies over and cannonballs on an ettin, taking damage with his fall as well, yelling “cannnnoooooooonballlllll” as he drops down. Fungus takes 40 points of damage as he inflicts 42 points of damage to the ettin and they both fall prone. He then lays on the ground and casts shillelegh and smacks him and hits imposing more damage. Cerul runs forward and casts radiance of the dawn on both ettins in his path. Both save, but take radiant damage. Then uses a bonus action to heal Fungus. The ettin on Ridley bites him again and hits and gnaws on him, imposing additional damage. Then bites him again and gnaws for additional damage. The guy yells “this guy is good I want to eat another one” and runs forward to the larger group. Kaizo attacks and misses, then attacks again and hits. Then uses a bonus action to punch another ettin and hits, imposing the next roll against him to be with advantage and his movement is halved. That ettin grabs and grapples Kaizo and bites him, and he takes damage and the guy gnaws on him. He then steps out of the moonbeam, pulling Kaizo through the moonbeam, as Kaizo takes 25 points of radiant damage from that moonbeam. Relys whistles the horse over and jumps on. She then moves the moonbeam to the prone ettin, who takes radiant damage, and dies from the moonbeam. He raises into the air and dissolves. Affy bonus action dashes over to the one holding Ridley and attacks, missing, but then hitting and slicing off his testacles and killing him.
  • ROUND FOUR: Ridley attacks the last ettin on Kaizo and crits, stunning him. He then attacks with the second arrow and hits, killing him. He flies up in the air as arrows are in both nipples.

The fight is over. Fungus says “that was awesome,” as the rest of the party comments how it was crazy that he fell so high from above. Cerul heals Ridley; Fungus heals himself; Affy uses second wind to heal herself. The ettins have huge axes and Cerul throws two into the portable hole to sell in the future. Kaizo speaks about hunger and the need to eat - words of wisdom. Relys crawls back into the carriage to sleep. Cerul gives Fungus heroic inspiration for this day.

We continue forward and spend the rest of the day traveling. We stay on the road. We travel through the forest. There is light snow. At some point, we reach a crest in the road and see the river ahead. We see a bridge crossing the span of the river. The bridge is stone and there are giant runes carved on it. There is a building on the other side. The building looks like the one that Cerul saw when he scryed on Pa. Cerul reads the runes…and we stop here - at the Southern Arch. It is twilight.

Session Summary

Session 15 – New Friends, A Charm and the Ring – Talil 37, 1246

  • Regrouped at the Pale Lantern in Qal’dynn and divided treasure and rewards.
  • Received a sealed invitation from Lorian Ormseer to tea at the Leaf Light in Cirandale, to discuss Abbey matters.
  • Fungus experimented with the Feywoven Glyphic, gaining a permanent effect that left him resistant to cold but unable to touch cloth or paper safely.
  • Met with Iofyr, who combined the Nightbud seed and Lusterbee wax into a golden-purple charm, explaining it must be empowered at a fey site such as the Ring of Sylunara.
  • Spoke with Lord Anceril in the Crescent Courts, who promised to review Kaizo’s petition for land to build a healing spa.
  • Traveled east into the Cirandale district, arriving near the Ring of Sylunara, preparing for the ritual to empower the charm.

Session 14 – Thinbrew and the Lusterbee Queen – Talil 36, 1246

  • Party tracked Thinbrew, a rogue alchemical encampment of ex-students, goblins, and bugbears.
  • Fought spiders, warped alchemists, and a rogue mage.
  • Recovered Glimmerree, the stolen Lusterbee queen, housed in a manmade wooden hive.
  • Looted black-market potions, salves, and supplies.
  • Returned Glimmerree to Mistress Toilee Goldbinder, earning enough Lusterbee wax for Iofyr’s mixture.
  • Returned to Qal’dynn with Abbey of Light and Affy’s father as next hooks.

Session 13 – Orcish Shadows and Lorian’s Favor – Talil 34, 1246

  • Affy warned by Borge of an orc corpse in the river, tied to her tribe’s excommunication.
  • Fungus given a new riddle by patron: a flask, petrification, and Nightbud.
  • Met Iofyr, who demanded rare Lusterbee wax for petrification mixture.
  • Lorian guided them beneath Moonroot Hollow to repair a ley-thread.
  • Fought corrupted energy eels; repaired thread successfully.
  • Rewarded with an Ioun Stone; learned Abbey waypoint is failing.

Session 12 – Audience with the King – Talil 32, 1246

  • Escorted to King Artes Highvale with Keanus Evergaze as ally.
  • Presented proof of Rel’Udomina infiltration in Qal’dynn.
  • Hamalex named Ambassador, but required to remain in the city.
  • Party rewarded with gold and status.
  • Sent to Moonroot Hollow to investigate ley-thread disruption.
  • Met Lorian Ormseer, who revealed young Threadborn mages had destabilized the ley-thread.

Session 11 – Civisabul and Keanus Evergaze – Talil 31, 1246

  • Emerged from Rel’Udomina hideout into university halls.
  • Battled Kyrenn Embersong, assassin disguised as Realmwarden.
  • Escaped into Civisabul university quad, encountering suspicious Realmwardens.
  • Brought Keanus to Pale Lantern Inn; he vouched for them.
  • Fungus experimented with Feywoven Glyphic (burning clothes incident).
  • Relys spoke with Pivric, learning of Weaving Night in Iparos.

Session 10 – Rel’Udomina Hideout II – Talil 30, 1246

  • Explored torture chambers, disguise rooms, and corpse disposal pits.
  • Fought Rel’Udomina agents, blink dogs, and an illusionary dragon.
  • Killed female commander; looted mithril armor and venom dagger.
  • Freed prisoners, including Keanus Evergaze.
  • Realmwardens arrived—revealed traitors within their own ranks.
  • Escaped upward into Civisabul.

Session 9 – Rel’Udomina Hideout I – Talil 29, 1246

  • Entered Rel’Udomina hideout.
  • Discovered documents naming Esinni Qadul “The Prominent.”
  • Fought spies, guards, and summoned creatures.
  • Recovered incriminating evidence, including encoded maps and disguises.
  • Confirmed Rel’Udomina infiltration of diplomatic circles.
  • Prepared to descend further into the hideout.

Session 8 – Whisperbend and the Hidden Gate – Talil 28, 1246

  • Met the Disciples of the Wind, a nomadic group camped outside Qal’dynn after the gates were closed.
  • Encountered disguised Rel’Udomina assassins at a desecrated shrine, intent on killing Ambassador Vondrel.
  • Fought and defeated the agents, uncovering evidence including Abbey ties and a hidden passage map.
  • Found “Whisperbend,” a forgotten tunnel beneath the cliffs into Qal’dynn.
  • Inside, solved puzzles, defeated a guardian construct, and gained access with a heart-shaped gem.
  • Discovered incriminating documents and a scrying pedestal targeting King Highvale.

Session 7 – Ambassadors and Orcish Clues – Talil 27, 1246

  • Returned to O’naren after Silent Circle; met Captain Salaal and noble Ravas Elorhen.
  • Learned Qal’dynn had closed its gates due to Rel’Udomina infiltration.
  • Ravas hinted at Hamalex’s possible redemption and value as a diplomat.
  • Affy confronted half-orc Tavrus at the Truffle Trap, who bore a pendant like her father’s.
  • Encountered Pinprick’s Peculiarities, a flamboyant gnome merchant with strange goods.
  • Fought Rel’Udomina agents posing as Elle’berian pilgrims at a shrine; clues pointed toward Whisperbend.

Session 6 – The Cackletongue Lair – Talil 26, 1246

  • Ventured into gnoll caverns corrupted by spores and demonic influence.
  • Faced rust monsters, fungal oozes, and psychic threats.
  • Quasit revealed Cerul’s celestial heritage, calling him “angel blood.”
  • Cerul’s wings manifested, unleashing radiant destruction on gnolls.
  • Defeated Gnazex, gnoll leader of the Cackletongue tribe.
  • Recovered treasures including a Ring of Fire Resistance and Nightbud.

Session 5 – The Oracle and the Silent Circle – Talil 25, 1246

  • Party split: Cerul, Ro, and Fungus visited Oracle Zanasee Tsal’daree; others dealt with Vrathin and realmwardens.
  • Oracle hinted that Fungus’ answers lay with Iofyr; gave him the Charm of the Slumbering Grove.
  • Returned to Silent Circle, finding Kosani corrupted by spores alongside gnoll spore-servants.
  • Defeated corrupted druid, gnolls, and wailing fungus in a brutal battle.
  • Secured Nightbud seeds, a Potion of Clairvoyance, and Boots of Elvenkind.
  • Learned Rel’Udomina is behind Vrathin’s maps and infiltration schemes.

Session 4 – O’naren and the Silent Circle – Talil 24, 1246

  • Entered O’naren; townsfolk treated Affy with prejudice.
  • Investigated Vrathin the mapmaker, who claimed his wife Elryan was Cloak of Defiance.
  • Found evidence (half-burned map) contradicting his claims, tying him to Rel’Udomina.
  • Heard Kulak the mercenary drunkenly boast about silencing “woman and brat.”
  • Traveled to Silent Circle, finding signs of gnoll desecration and Nightbud corruption.
  • Encountered gnolls in sacred grove and recovered important clues.

Session 3 – The Feywoven Glyphic – Talil 23, 1246

  • Experimented further with the Feywoven Glyphic, producing varied effects: exhaustion, riches, comprehension of Sylvan, and figurine of wondrous power.
  • Summoned the wanderer Ireron from cube manipulation.
  • Traveled eastward, camping with Nillian the elf and suffering a blight attack en route.
  • Encountered Elryan and Paridan, fleeing wife and adopted son of Vrathin; hired mercenaries pursued them.
  • Depending on outcome, party either found them dead (with map scrap) or alive but secretive.
  • Entered O’naren with growing suspicions of Vrathin’s ties to Rel’Udomina.

Session 2 – Beneath the Broken Citadel – Talil 22, 1246

  • Explored subterranean labs and ruins under the citadel.
  • Fought skeletal guardians, oozes, and discovered an infernal summoning chamber linked to Asmodeus.
  • Found teleportation circle, broken Feywild portal, and cube later named Feywoven Glyphic.
  • Learned the site tied together fey magic, infernal power, teleportation, and underdark access.
  • Cube activated with dangerous random effects; party began experimenting.
  • Returned to Mossholde with pressing questions and a new artifact of concern.

Session 1 – Meeting in Mossholde – Talil 21, 1246

  • Party assembled at Mossholde outpost, meeting Marlowe and Harva.
  • Night interrupted by wounded hunter Tabury Eillek, who reported ambush by strange creatures.
  • Party followed his trail, encountering gremlin-like fey and a mysterious skull-wielding leader.
  • Rescued a wolf pup from a cage; Ro adopted it.
  • Discovered stolen goods, alchemical supplies, and ominous powder.
  • Found hidden trapdoor in Broken Citadel, setting up deeper exploration.

DM Brief

DM Brief

Session 00/01 - 01/16

I plan on running a campaign that is driven by you. To do this, Session 0 will be about working together on a few things, and here’s what you’ll need to be prepared for:

1. Character Type - Have an idea of what type of character you want to play. Many of you are way ahead on this and have already created the character.

2. Goal(s) - Make sure that you have thought of at least one, if not more, goals for your character. It is a good idea to have 3 to 4 goals for your character at any given time. There is a caveat … make sure you are flexible to adjusting that goal during Session 0. We will ensure that the goals align to party cohesion.

Examples

Goals can be as complex or as simple as you would like. Here are some examples:

Goal

Reason

Win the heart of a certain person

A deep seated desire to be loved

Discover the true identity of an important person

The need to be appreciated

Slay a powerful monster

A promise to a dying family member

Discover the location of a fabled feature

A hunger for power

Obtain a magical artifact

The search for spiritual enlightenment

Prove the existence of a certain phenomenon

A prophecy

Interrupt a ritual of negative effect

Prove themselves worthy of a love interest

Session 02 - 01/24

Please review your character’s goals and update them if needed.

Everyone should be level 2.

Review your Notebook. Anything in Red should be new information from the DM

Session 03 - 03/06

Everyone is still level 2 … careful about murdering the citizens!

Review your Notebook … I totally jacked ‘em up!

Campaign Planning

Session 00 - 01/16/25

XP: Discussion about do we play someone else’s character if they can’t be there. Pro/con. No one seems to care if their character is played by someone else. Therefore - XP will always be split evenly among the 6 characters since they will all be played even if the IRL person isn’t there.

Check the site if you care and want to be invested. If you don’t want to, that’s ok. But DM will communicate with the group and with each character via the Google site.

Look at the Session Notes (and EVERYONE is welcome to take them) but at a minimum, check the DM Brief. Read during the 15 minutes before game time.

Leveling will require a long rest.

Review of changes in 2024 rules. Lots of conversation about reactions - agreed it was from start of turn to start of next turn. Potions are now a bonus action.

Discussion of what is an action - if DM asks us to make a check it will NOT burn an action. If we elect to do something (skill check), it will. DM will also try to make use of the Passive Perception quite a bit…

Discussion about the party make up:

Two full healers (Cleric, Bard), Scout (Monk), we don’t have a rogue (but Bard is rogue-ish), we have a lock picker, we have lots of magic. Group seems to feel like everyone picked what they wanted and we have a decent mix of characters and proficiencies.

Hit points - discussion about standard vs. rolling. Group will pick standard.

Surprise rules - combat starts; people who are surprised have disadvantage on initiative.

Will need a Healer’s Kit to be able to use hit dice on a short rest.

Death saves - first death save fail is a level of exhaustion; and only get exhaustion back on a long rest. If you are healed and fall again, and make a fail, you get another level of exhaustion.

Crafting magic items…seem a little overpowered in new 2024 rules. This is something you can work with the DM on to “work up to being able to make something”...but you can’t just make something every time you have downtime.

Table chit chat - just don’t be distracting. Keep it quiet. You need to focus on the campaign itself and be ready when it is your turn on initiative…so know what you want to do. Had a big discussion about moving down a slot in initiative order (permanently), but decided not to do it. Decided to just follow the rolls and if you aren’t ready, you just do nothing or set a reaction (current mechanisms that already exist).

Rules lawyers - if a player makes an error, just discuss. If the DM screws up, players should feel comfortable to point out what happened. If it is a rule that needs a ton of discussion, we will do the best we can and can take up the specifics after the game.

In game - don’t look up a monster we are searching for and/or are fighting. DMs will flavor monsters and they may NOT match the Monster Manual.

Having character goals is important. At some point during play in a session, you may find that the plot speaks to you/your character. This is intentional. DM goal is to have everyone invested in the campaign and he will try to pepper the campaign with everything we gave him/continue to give him.
